Can Language Models Learn When to Abstain? Policy Learning and Calibrated Answer Selection
语言模型能否学会何时放弃回答？策略学习与校准的答案选择
A useful language model should know when to answer and when to say "I don't know." Can we improve that decision by training the model itself or by training a separate component to check its answers? We study both approaches on Qwen3-14B for factual questions, using a score that rewards correct answers, penalizes mistakes, and allows abstention. Across three training seeds and two held-out confidence thresholds, a learned answer selector improves threshold-averaged utility over the original model's calibrated confidence filter. On TriviaQA, mean coverage increases from 20.2% to 30.9%, while the pooled error rate among scored answers decreases from 23.0% to 14.5% under exact-match grading. The tested reinforcement-learning changes do not establish consistent gains. On NQ-Open, the selector improves threshold-averaged utility over the filter but still performs worse than always abstaining. A partial human-reviewed audit preserves the TriviaQA finding. Our results support learning to select answers in this setting while showing that reliable abstention across datasets remains unresolved.
中文翻译
一个有用的语言模型应知道何时回答，何时说“我不知道”。我们能否通过训练模型自身或训练一个独立组件来检查其答案，从而改善这一判断？我们在Qwen3-14B上针对事实类问题研究了这两种方法，采用一种奖励正确回答、惩罚错误并允许放弃的回答评分机制。在三个训练种子和两个保留置信度阈值下，学习到的答案选择器在阈值平均效用上优于原始模型的校准置信度过滤器。在TriviaQA上，平均覆盖率从20.2%提升至30.9%，而在精确匹配评分下，已评分答案的合并错误率从23.0%下降至14.5%。所测试的强化学习变化未展现出一致收益。在NQ-Open上，选择器虽优于过滤器，但仍劣于始终放弃回答的表现。部分人工审核审计验证了TriviaQA的结果。我们的结果支持在此设定下学习答案选择，但也表明在跨数据集实现可靠放弃回答仍是一个未解决的问题。
👤 Juan Terven, Diana-Margarita Córdova-Esparza, Ruben Gomez, Carlos Miranda Rocha, Julio-Alejandro Romero-González et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.13617]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Training User Simulators with Latent Intrinsic Feedback
基于潜在内在反馈训练用户模拟器
User simulators are gaining attention as environments to train LLM assistants that can better cope with the nuances of multi-turn conversations with real users. Prior work on user simulation has emphasized generating realistic user utterances, leaving unexplored their potential as a source of direct feedback on the assistant's responses. Such evaluation signals may not be explicitly communicated in language, but can instead reside in the latent reasoning of users. In this work, we introduce user simulators with intrinsic feedback, namely, models equipped with latent evaluation states: internal thoughts, reactions, and scalar ratings that evaluate the assistant from a first-person user perspective at each conversation turn. We train LLM-based simulators using interaction logs between real users and assistants and demonstrate that intrinsic feedback improves simulation realism and critiquing behaviors versus prompted simulator baselines, while also providing turn-level evaluations that existing fine-tuned simulators lack. Further, evaluations generated by our trained simulators correlate better with human judgments than those of prompted simulators of the same size. Lastly, our experiments reveal that simulators with intrinsic feedback provide effective reward signals for multi-turn RL training, matching setups that rely on external judge LLMs for rewards while cutting RL compute by more than half.
中文翻译
用户模拟器正日益成为训练大语言模型助手的环境，使其能更好地应对与真实用户的多轮对话中的细微差别。先前的用户模拟研究强调生成逼真的用户话语，而未探索其作为直接反馈来源的潜力。此类评估信号可能并未显式体现在语言中，而是存在于用户的潜在推理中。本文提出带有内在反馈的用户模拟器，即配备潜在评估状态的模型：每个对话轮次中内部思考、反应和标量评分，从第一人称用户视角评估助手的表现。我们使用真实用户与助手之间的交互日志训练基于LLM的模拟器，并证明内在反馈提升了模拟的真实性与批判行为，优于提示型模拟器基线，同时提供了现有微调模拟器所缺乏的轮次级评估。此外，由我们训练的模拟器生成的评估与人类判断的相关性优于同规模的提示型模拟器。最后，实验表明，带有内在反馈的模拟器为多轮强化学习训练提供了有效的奖励信号，在奖励依赖外部裁判LLM的设置中表现相当，同时将RL计算量减少超过一半。
👤 Tarek Naous, Craig Boutilier, Wei Xu, Ofer Meshi 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

BLiMPPI: Benchmark of Linguistic Minimal Pairs for Polarity Items
BLiMPPI：极性词最小对的语言学基准测试
Negative polarity items (NPIs), such as "anymore" and "ever", are grammatical only in select environments, predominantly in the context of negation. Because their grammaticality depends on both syntax and semantics, they have a rich, multilingual literature in theoretical linguistics, yet work on NPIs in language models is almost entirely confined to English. We introduce BLiMPPI, a multilingual evaluation suite of over 12,000 NPI minimal pairs in 33 languages, built by a pipeline that discovers NPIs in Universal Dependencies (UD) treebanks, labels their licensing environments, and perturbs these into minimal pairs. Evaluating 12 multilingual and 2 monolingual models alongside n-gram baselines, we find that multilingual models struggle most with lower-resource languages and with pairs that are not n-gram solvable. We further examine the effects of NPI typology, language family, licensing variability, and NPI-licenser distance. Data: this https URL
中文翻译
否定极性词（NPIs），如“anymore”和“ever”，仅在特定语境中合法，主要出现在否定环境中。由于其合法性同时依赖句法和语义，它们在理论语言学中拥有丰富的多语言文献，但关于语言模型中的NPI研究几乎完全局限于英语。我们提出了BLiMPPI，一个包含33种语言超过12,000个NPI最小对的多语言评估套件，该套件通过一个流水线构建：在通用依存树库（UD）中发现NPI，标注其许可环境，并将其扰动为最小对。在12个多语言模型和2个单语言模型上进行评估，对比n-gram基线，我们发现多语言模型在低资源语言以及非n-gram可解的配对上表现最差。我们进一步分析了NPI类型、语言家族、许可变异性和NPI与许可者之间的距离的影响。数据可用性：此https链接
👤 Arzu Burcu Güven, Anna Rogers, Leonie Weissweiler, Joakim Nivre, Rob van der Goot 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.13550]

Personalization Without Stereotyping: Do Speech LLMs Know When to Turn a Deaf Ear?
个性化而不刻板化：语音大模型何时该装聋作哑？
Recent speech LLMs can leverage rich paralinguistic cues, enabling more complex and personalized interactions. However, little work investigates how this sensitivity can lead to stereotyping, and existing evaluations largely measure demographic disparities without distinguishing beneficial personalization from stereotypical behavior. We introduce BASIS-Bench, a benchmark designed to disentangle these two aspects through three axes: (1) demographic robustness, measuring disparities in content-based tasks across speakers and demographic classification capabilities; (2) stereotypical bias, measuring whether perceived demographic cues trigger unprompted stereotypical behavior; and (3) personalization, measuring whether models can tailor responses to demographic characteristics when explicitly requested. We evaluate eight recent speech LLMs and find that, despite often recognizing demographic cues, most fail to translate them into appropriate personalization. Explicit demographic labels improve personalization but also amplify stereotypical associations already present in the textual backbones. These findings highlight the need to train speech LLMs not only to perceive demographic cues, but to learn when they should shape their responses.
中文翻译
近期的语音大模型能够利用丰富的副语言线索，实现更复杂和个性化的交互。然而，很少有研究探讨这种敏感性如何导致刻板印象，且现有评估大多仅衡量人口统计差异，未能区分有益的个性化与刻板行为。我们提出了BASIS-Bench，一个旨在分离这两个方面的基准测试，涵盖三个维度：（1）人口统计鲁棒性，衡量不同说话人之间内容任务的差异及人口统计分类能力；（2）刻板偏见，衡量感知到的人口统计线索是否引发未经提示的刻板行为；（3）个性化，衡量模型在明确请求下能否根据人口统计特征定制回应。我们评估了八个最新的语音大模型，发现尽管多数模型能识别人口统计线索，但很少能将其转化为适当的个性化响应。显式的人口统计标签虽提升了个性化表现，但也放大了文本基础模型中已存在的刻板关联。这些发现强调，训练语音大模型不仅需要感知人口统计线索，更要学会在何时应据此调整回应。
👤 Kshitij Ambilduke, Caroline Brun, Thibaut Thonet, Vassilina Nikoulina, Marcely Zanon Boito 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.13485]

Proactive Agentic Orchestration
主动型智能体编排
Agentic orchestration delegates tasks to sub-agents with independent contexts, providing a key path to extending the effective execution horizon of large language model agents. However, existing orchestration methods face two limitations: in execution, current methods fix sub-agents' goals and harnesses at creation time, limiting their adaptability to dynamic real-world environments; in optimization, sparse and delayed feedback constrains effective learning of the orchestration policy. To address these limitations, we introduce POrchestra, an on-the-fly proactive agentic orchestration framework. It allows sub-agents to proactively communicate with the orchestrator at any logical point to request harness adjustments while maintaining continuity of local execution. This mechanism not only enables sub-agents to adapt seamlessly to dynamic environments but also naturally inspires On-Policy Orchestration Distillation (OPOD) based on hindsight feedback. OPOD attributes sub-agents' proactive revision requests back to preceding orchestration decisions to derive targeted improvement signals, and leverages these signals as process supervision alongside the final reward to optimize the orchestration policy. We evaluate POrchestra on three long-horizon agent benchmarks spanning deep research, dynamic digital assistance, and software engineering. POrchestra improves task success by an average of 9.1% relative to the strongest baseline; combined with OPOD, it improves small-orchestrator success by an average of 10.0% relative to existing training baselines. POrchestra also achieves a more favorable cost-performance trade-off. Our code is publicly available at this https URL .
中文翻译
智能体编排将任务委派给具有独立上下文的子智能体，是扩展大型语言模型智能体有效执行范围的关键路径。然而，现有编排方法存在两个局限：在执行层面，当前方法在创建时固定子智能体的目标和工具，限制其对动态现实环境的适应能力；在优化层面，稀疏且延迟的反馈制约了编排策略的有效学习。为解决这些局限，我们提出了POrchestra——一种实时主动型智能体编排框架。它允许子智能体在任意逻辑点主动与编排器通信，请求调整工具，同时保持本地执行的连续性。该机制不仅使子智能体能无缝适应动态环境，还自然启发了基于事后反馈的“在策略编排蒸馏”（OPOD）。OPOD将子智能体的主动修订请求回溯至之前的编排决策，生成针对性的改进信号，并将这些信号作为过程监督，与最终奖励一起用于优化编排策略。我们在三个长周期智能体基准上评估POrchestra，涵盖深度研究、动态数字助手和软件工程领域。POrchestra相比最强基线平均提升了9.1%的任务成功率；结合OPOD后，小规模编排器的成功率平均提升10.0%。POrchestra还实现了更优的成本-性能权衡。代码已公开于此https链接。
👤 Xueyang Feng, Chaozhuo Li, Xiaohe Bo, Jiakai Tang, Chenxi Li et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

MoPrO-SD: Modular Prompt Optimization for Stance Detection
MoPrO-SD：立场检测的模块化提示优化
Stance detection remains challenging for large language models (LLMs) due to implicit opinions, sarcasm, and context-dependent expressions of stance. Although automatic prompt optimization offers an alternative to fine-tuning without updating model parameters, existing structured approaches are not specifically designed around stance-label semantics and class-specific ambiguities. We introduce MoPrO-SD, a stance-specific prompt optimization framework that decomposes prompts into functional semantic modules and refines them sequentially using LLM-based feedback. We study three refinement strategies: instruction-only optimization (I), instruction with reasoning (I+R), and instruction with per-example reasoning (I+R+E), using class-conditioned errors for stance-label refinement. We also introduce VaccineStance, a new human-annotated benchmark for vaccine stance detection. Experiments across four datasets and five open-weight LLMs show that MoPrO-SD improves upon the human-written Base prompt in many settings. On SemEval-2016, class-conditioned feedback achieves higher mean Macro-F1 than pooled feedback for four of five backbones. Both reasoning-based strategies, I+R and I+R+E, achieve strong performance, with their relative effectiveness varying across models and datasets, while remaining competitive with recent prompt optimization methods. The MoPrO-SD code and VaccineStance dataset are available on GitHub.
中文翻译
由于隐含观点、讽刺语气和上下文依赖的立场表达，立场检测对大型语言模型（LLMs）仍具挑战性。尽管自动提示优化可在不更新模型参数的情况下替代微调，但现有结构化方法并未专门针对立场标签语义和类别特定模糊性进行设计。我们提出了MoPrO-SD，一种面向立场检测的提示优化框架，将提示分解为功能语义模块，并利用LLM反馈进行顺序优化。我们研究了三种优化策略：仅指令优化（I）、带推理的指令优化（I+R），以及带示例推理的指令优化（I+R+E），并使用类别条件错误进行立场标签精炼。我们还引入了VaccineStance，一个全新的用于疫苗立场检测的人工标注基准数据集。在四个数据集和五个开源权重LLM上的实验表明，MoPrO-SD在多数场景下优于人工编写的基线提示。在SemEval-2016上，类别条件反馈在五种骨干模型中的四种上实现了高于聚合反馈的平均宏F1值。基于推理的两种策略（I+R 和 I+R+E）均表现优异，其相对效果因模型和数据集而异，但仍保持与近期提示优化方法相当的竞争力。MoPrO-SD代码和VaccineStance数据集已在GitHub公开。
👤 Meysam Sabbaghan, Nikesh Gyawali, Arman Zareian Jahromi, Sebastian Loera-Lozano, Doina Caragea et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

From Structure to Validity: Mechanism-Guided Generation and Refinement of LLM Rubrics
从结构到有效性：机制引导的大语言模型评分标准生成与优化
Rubrics have been widely used to evaluate and optimize language models on open-ended tasks. However, existing work primarily focuses on generating better rubrics, with limited understanding of what makes rubric design effective. In this work, we conduct a systematic mechanistic analysis of rubric design across criterion organization, criterion operationalization, and score aggregation. Our analysis shows that structural organization enables more comprehensive criterion construction, semantic specification alone does not ensure effective criterion-level judgments, and sophisticated score aggregation provides limited additional gains. Guided by these findings, we introduce Adaptive Rubric Tree Search with Hybrid refinement (ARTS-Hybrid), which combines adaptive tree construction with semantic-first, response-calibrated refinement. Experiments across benchmarks show that ARTS-Hybrid improves both end-to-end evaluation performance and intrinsic rubric quality. Further experiments suggest that, beyond evaluation, rubrics generated by ARTS-Hybrid provide effective signals for model optimization.
中文翻译
评分标准已被广泛用于开放式任务的语言模型评估与优化。然而，现有研究主要聚焦于生成更优的评分标准，对评分设计有效性的内在机制理解有限。本研究对评分标准设计在准则组织、准则操作化和评分聚合三个层面进行了系统的机制分析。分析表明，结构化组织有助于更全面的准则构建，仅靠语义定义并不能确保有效的准则层级判断，而复杂的评分聚合带来的增益有限。基于这些发现，我们提出了自适应评分树搜索与混合优化方法（ARTS-Hybrid），结合自适应树构建与语义优先、响应校准的优化策略。在多个基准测试上的实验表明，ARTS-Hybrid不仅提升了端到端评估性能，也显著改善了评分标准的内在质量。进一步实验表明，除评估外，ARTS-Hybrid生成的评分标准还能为模型优化提供有效信号。
👤 Feifei Qian, Xuan Chen, Lixin Cui, Chaoyang Mei, Fanlin Meng et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.13303]

Agreement Without Alignment: Label-Free Rubric Clarification Increases LLM-Judge Consensus but Not Agreement with Humans
无需对齐即可达成一致：无标签评分标准澄清提升LLM裁判共识但未提高与人类的一致性
LLM judges are increasingly deployed as panels, and disagreement among panel members is often read as a symptom of an unclear rubric. We test a label-free procedure that turns this disagreement into rubric repairs. The procedure decomposes each rubric into sub-criteria, ranks them by cross-judge disagreement on a development split, and has an editor model rewrite only the most contested ones. An additional stage that moves text-checkable sub-criteria to deterministic checkers transferred none in our evaluation. Human labels are never used to select or edit clauses; they are used only to evaluate the outcome. We pre-specified four hypotheses in a version-controlled protocol and evaluated six rubric conditions with a panel of five judges from three providers on SummEval, the HealthBench meta-evaluation, and USR Topical-Chat, for a total of 129,770 judge requests, excluding retries. On SummEval, the procedure raised inter-judge Krippendorff's $\alpha$ by 0.069 (95% CI 0.046 to 0.092) over the original rubric and by 0.074 over a control that applied the same edits to an equal number of randomly chosen clauses that were not selected. Agreement with expert ratings showed no detectable change (Kendall $\tau$ difference -0.007, 95% CI -0.037 to 0.021). On HealthBench and USR, neither inter-judge agreement nor human agreement met the preregistered effect criteria. In a preregistered secondary comparison, human agreement after editing the contested clauses was lower than after editing the control clauses on all three datasets in point estimate, although no interval excluded zero. Selecting clauses by disagreement makes judges agree with each other more than editing arbitrary clauses does, but in our setting the added consensus did not bring the judges closer to human annotators. We will release code, frozen rubrics, item identifiers, and judge scores.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）裁判正越来越多地以小组形式部署，小组成员间的分歧常被视为评分标准不清晰的标志。我们测试了一种无标签程序，将这种分歧转化为评分标准的修复。该程序将每个评分标准分解为子标准，根据开发集上各裁判间的分歧程度进行排序，并由编辑模型仅重写争议最大的子标准。一个额外阶段将可文本检查的子标准移至确定性检查器，但在我们的评估中并未带来收益。人类标签从未用于选择或编辑条款；仅用于评估最终结果。我们在版本控制协议中预先设定四个假设，并在SummEval、HealthBench元评估和USR Topical-Chat上，使用来自三家提供商的五名裁判组成的小组，共评估了129,770次裁判请求（不含重试）。在SummEval上，该程序使裁判间Krippendorff's α提升了0.069（95%置信区间0.046至0.092），比对随机选择同等数量条款进行相同编辑的对照组提升了0.074。与专家评分的一致性未检测到显著变化（Kendall τ差异 -0.007，95% CI -0.037至0.021）。在HealthBench和USR上，裁判间一致性及与人类的一致性均未达到预注册效应标准。在一次预注册的次要比较中，所有三个数据集上，修改争议条款后的人类一致性点估计值低于修改对照条款的情况，尽管置信区间均包含零。按分歧选择条款能使裁判彼此更一致，但在本设置下，这种新增共识并未使裁判更接近人类标注者。我们将发布代码、冻结的评分标准、项目标识符和裁判评分。
👤 Sangdo Han, Moonsoo Kim 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

Do Prompt Optimizers Break Requirements They Are Not Scored On? A Preregistered Study
提示优化器是否会破坏它们未被评分的要求？一项预注册研究
Prompt optimizers rewrite a prompt to raise one target metric, but prompts also state requirements the metric does not score, such as an output format, citations, or abstention. In a preregistered study, we run GEPA, MIPROv2, and TextGrad on HotpotQA, SQuAD 2.0, and IFEval and count per-item violations of unscored requirements with deterministic checks. By the preregistered criterion, all three optimizers produce regressions, substantially on HotpotQA, where net regression in citation validity reaches 10.7 to 41.6 points while exact match rises by 21 to 34 points. A post-hoc decomposition changes this reading. The optimized prompts elicit reasoning before the required JSON object, and under the 200-token output cap used throughout, 13.9% to 47.0% of responses end before any answer appears. Among parseable outputs, net regression is within 3 points of zero. With a 1,024-token cap, extraction failures disappear, content regression stays below 1 point, and exact match rises further. Two simple gates fail the preregistered criterion. The side effect is narrower than hypothesized: optimized prompts are more accurate and break an explicit output contract, and a lenient target metric does not show the cost.
中文翻译

Scaling Agentic Data for Long-Horizon Terminal Intelligence
面向长时程终端智能的智能体数据规模化
Terminal agents are moving beyond local operations toward completing real-world professional tasks. Short trajectories can demonstrate commands and tool calls, but they do not fully show how agents accumulate evidence through exploration, use it to inform judgments, continually adjust strategies based on feedback, and turn intermediate results into final deliverables. High-quality long-horizon trajectories capture these agentic behavioral patterns and their connections within a single problem-solving process, providing richer experience for sustained autonomous work. However, environment synthesis may complete or simplify the key work underlying these patterns in advance and thus remove the need for exploration, implementation, and verification that the agent was meant to perform. To address this, we introduce TerminalHorizon, a fully automated data synthesis engine for long-horizon terminal agents. Starting from real-world professional work, it uses independent execution to identify key work omitted during construction and uses the resulting evidence to guide environment reconstruction. To extend these patterns across stages, it progressively expands tasks under the same goal, introducing new requirements that build on prior results and call for further exploration and improvement. Using this engine, we construct 1.5K task environments and perform supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.5-27B on the resulting dataset to obtain TerminalHorizon-27B. We achieve substantial performance gains on all 12 benchmarks spanning coding-agent tasks, agentic tasks in real-world production settings, and general reasoning. Multiple RSI case studies, each with a 12-hour research budget, further demonstrate the model's ability to continue experimenting beyond an initial solution, improve its methods based on feedback, and retain effective improvements in its final deliverables.

KV-Repurpose: A Testbed for Evaluating Non-Prefix KV-Cache Reuse
KV-Repurpose：评估非前缀KV缓存重用的测试平台
KV-cache reuse in large language model serving is commonly limited to exact prompt prefixes, preventing reuse when the same retrieved document appears after different system prompts, conversation histories, or dynamic metadata. We present KV-Repurpose, an open-source, vLLM-based system for developing and evaluating segment-wise KV-cache reuse beyond exact prefixes. The system combines cache-aware segment scheduling, prompt construction, capacity management, workload generation, and runtime monitoring. Experiments on long-context benchmarks show that naive segment reuse largely preserves output quality for single-document question answering and summarization, while exposing clear limitations in cross-document reasoning. Under a Zipfian RAG-style workload in which 20% of requests share a fixed prefix, KV-Repurpose achieves a substantially higher token-level cache hit rate than prefix caching at the same 30 GB cache capacity, 63.9% versus 3.3%, resulting in lower TTFT. Even with 1 TB of cache capacity, prefix caching reaches only a 17.9% hit rate in this workload. Source code is available under the Apache-2.0 license at this https URL .
中文翻译
大型语言模型服务中的KV缓存重用通常局限于精确提示前缀，当同一文档出现在不同系统提示、对话历史或动态元数据之后时无法复用。我们提出KV-Repurpose，一个基于vLLM的开源系统，用于开发和评估超越精确前缀的段级KV缓存重用。该系统结合了缓存感知的段调度、提示构造、容量管理、工作负载生成和运行时监控。在长上下文基准测试中的实验表明，简单的段重用在单文档问答和摘要任务中基本保持输出质量，但在跨文档推理方面暴露明显局限。在Zipfian RAG风格的工作负载下（20%请求共享固定前缀），KV-Repurpose在相同30 GB缓存容量下，token级缓存命中率高达63.9%，远超前缀缓存的3.3%，从而显著降低首Token延迟（TTFT）。即使在1 TB缓存容量下，前缀缓存的命中率也仅为17.9%。源代码可在Apache-2.0许可下通过此链接获取。

CoBe: A Benchmark For Conversational Counterfactual Text Editing
CoBe：对话式反事实文本编辑基准测试
Counterfactual text editing is the ability to revise a given text in light of a hypothetical change while preserving facts not causally affected by that change. When asked what would have happened if a doctor had not recommended surgery, the model output should retain the patient's medical history and modify only the consequences of the decision. This requires causal understanding: a model must determine, without being explicitly instructed to, what events are affected by the hypothetical change and what should remain fixed. This skill is central to human reasoning, especially in safety-critical domains like healthcare or law. By contrast, the task of associational text generation merely generates text statistically associated with the altered condition. In the medical example, an associational response might make the patient's symptoms less severe because milder cases correlate with no surgery. Such responses produce a plausible scenario, but not the counterfactual version of the given one. Prior counterfactual-text datasets do not clearly separate these modes of reasoning. We introduce the Counterfactual Benchmark (CoBe), a testbed for models' zero-shot ability to counterfactually edit a given textual scenario when prompted to do so in a conversationally natural way. CoBe contains 2000 questions spanning healthcare, science and engineering, business, and everyday scenarios. We test a suite of frontier and smaller models, with frontier systems achieving an average score of $58.05\%$. Our findings indicate that models frequently default to associational generation rather than preserving the causal structure of the original scenario. Moreover, performance does not scale consistently with either model size or output token length, suggesting that the failure may reflect model training objectives rather than insufficient model capacity.
中文翻译
反事实文本编辑是指在假设性变化背景下修改给定文本，同时保留不受该变化因果影响的事实。例如，当被问及如果医生没有建议手术会怎样时，模型输出应保留患者的病史，仅修改决策的后果。这需要因果理解：模型必须自主判断哪些事件受假设变化影响，哪些应保持不变。这一能力在医疗、法律等安全关键领域尤为关键。相比之下，关联性文本生成仅基于统计关联生成内容。在医疗例子中，关联性回答可能因轻症病例更常不手术而使症状减轻，虽看似合理，却非真正的反事实版本。现有反事实文本数据集未能清晰区分这两种推理模式。我们提出反事实基准（CoBe），用于测试模型在对话式自然提示下零样本反事实编辑的能力。CoBe包含2000个问题，涵盖医疗、科学与工程、商业及日常场景。我们测试了一系列前沿和小型模型，前沿系统平均得分为58.05%。研究发现，模型常默认采用关联性生成而非保持原始场景的因果结构。此外，性能并未随模型规模或输出长度一致提升，表明失败可能源于训练目标而非模型容量不足。
👤 Arvind Raghavan, Inwoo Hwang, Shreyas Havaldar, Kai-Zhan Lee, Aurghya Maiti et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

Evaluating LLM Creativity through Long-Tail Performance
通过长尾表现评估大语言模型的创造力
Creativity benchmarks for large language models (LLMs) vary widely in task and metric design. This raises the question of whether they capture a common notion of creativity. We propose that this shared target can be formalized as task-specific performance in the long tail of a reference input-output distribution, which describes how common input-output pairs are relative to a chosen source of text. We ground this proposition in the novelty-quality product formulation and evaluate it empirically across 15 benchmarks and 26 open-weight models, using n-gram absence from the pretraining data as a novelty proxy. Our results show that creativity benchmarks lie further in the tail regions than standard benchmarks. Moreover, on both creativity and standard benchmarks, model rankings from higher-tail subsets of each benchmark align more closely with human creativity judgments in LM-Arena. Building on these findings, we introduce q@Tail($\alpha$), a lower-cost creativity diagnostic that measures mean task quality on the tail fraction of an existing benchmark. Applying it to creativity-promoting methods reveals substantial room to improve LLM creativity through stronger tail performance.

大语言模型（LLMs）的创造力基准在任务设计和度量方式上差异巨大。这引发了一个问题：它们是否捕捉到了一个共同的创造力概念？我们提出，这一共通目标可形式化为参考输入-输出分布尾部区域的任务特定性能，该分布描述了输入-输出对相对于选定文本源的常见程度。我们基于新颖性-质量乘积理论进行论证，并在15个基准和26个开源模型上进行实证评估，以n-gram在预训练数据中的缺失作为新颖性代理。结果显示，创造力基准的表现位置远在标准基准的尾部区域。此外，在创造力和标准基准上，来自各基准高尾部子集的模型排名与 LM-Arena 中的人类创造力判断更加一致。基于这些发现，我们引入 q@Tail($\alpha$)，一种低成本的创造力诊断指标，用于测量现有基准尾部部分的任务平均质量。将其应用于促进创造力的方法，揭示出通过增强尾部表现仍有巨大提升空间。

Forecasting Corpus-Sampling Disagreement in Masked Text Reconstruction
预测掩码文本重构中的语料采样分歧
If a second text corpus were collected from the same population, how much would a predictor's outputs change? We study this question for masked-text reconstruction using a smoothed predictor built from nearby token-pair counts and evaluated over the full 50,257-token GPT-2 vocabulary. Treating each source document as one observation, we derive a first-order formula for the expected disagreement between predictors fitted to two independent corpora. The formula accounts for variable document length, random context counts, and correlations across token offsets, and it can be evaluated from a separate reference corpus without access to either target corpus. On TinyStories, WikiText-103, and CNN/DailyMail, growing each corpus from 512 to 2,048 chunks of 64 tokens lowers disagreement by only 5--40\%, far short of the 75\% implied by $1/n$ scaling. The forecast tracks this slow decline, which we trace to smoothing of rarely seen contexts. Forecast accuracy depends less on how these correlations are modeled than on the size of the reference corpus: small references systematically underpredict disagreement. A source-split jackknife that uses only reference data reduces this calibration error by 57--59\%. After this correction, a simpler smoothed-multinomial approximation is most accurate when the reference corpus is smaller than the target corpus, and a nonlinear source bootstrap is most accurate when it is larger. Finally, small masked diffusion language models trained on the same corpora show that the count-based forecasts partly identify which inputs are most sensitive to the corpus, but miss the neural models' much faster decline in disagreement.

如果从同一人群收集第二个文本语料库，预测器的输出会有多大变化？我们针对掩码文本重构问题，使用基于邻近词对计数构建的平滑预测器，并在完整的50,257个词的GPT-2词汇表上进行评估。将每个源文档视为一个观测值，我们推导出两个独立语料库拟合预测器之间预期分歧的一阶公式。该公式考虑了文档长度差异、随机上下文计数以及跨词偏移的相关性，且无需访问目标语料库即可通过独立参考语料库进行计算。在TinyStories、WikiText-103和CNN/DailyMail数据集上，将语料库规模从512个64词块扩大到2,048块，分歧仅下降5%至40%，远低于$1/n$缩放所暗示的75%。该预测准确跟踪了这一缓慢下降趋势，我们将其归因于罕见上下文的平滑处理。预测准确性更多取决于参考语料库的大小而非相关性建模方式：小参考语料库系统性低估分歧。采用仅使用参考数据的源分割jackknife方法可将校准误差降低57%–59%。校正后，当参考语料库小于目标语料库时，更简单的平滑多项式近似最准确；而当参考语料库更大时，非线性源自助法最准确。最后，对相同语料库训练的小型掩码扩散语言模型显示，基于计数的预测部分识别出对语料库最敏感的输入，但未能捕捉神经模型中分歧更快下降的特性。

SEEDS: Preview-Guided Evidence Admission for Search Agents
SEEDS：搜索代理的预览引导证据接纳机制
Search agents extend large reasoning models with multi-round access to external knowledge, yet most existing systems largely conflate retrieving evidence with consuming it. Retrieved documents are commonly incorporated according to fixed or rank-based policies, despite the fact that evidence utility is conditioned on the agent's current reasoning state. Consequently, a lower-ranked candidate may be critical for the current step, while a higher-ranked candidate may provide redundant, irrelevant, or misleading information. We formulate this overlooked problem as evidence admission, which asks, at each search step, which retrieved candidates should be allowed to enter the agent's evolving reasoning context. To enable such selective control, we introduce SEEDS (Selective Evidence Entry During Search), a preview-guided evidence admission framework for search agents. SEEDS first exposes lightweight previews of retrieved candidates and then lets the agent jointly determine which candidates and how many should be fully inspected, conditioned on its current reasoning state. Unlike fixed or adaptive prefix policies, SEEDS can select arbitrary subsets with flexible cardinality, and it can be instantiated directly with an existing reasoning agent or further optimized through learning. Across nine knowledge-intensive QA benchmarks under both local corpus and live web search, SEEDS consistently improves search effectiveness while substantially reducing full-evidence consumption. Experimental analyses show that SEEDS more effectively selects useful candidates beyond the corresponding retrieval prefix. These results establish evidence admission as a complementary dimension of agentic search, separating what information is retrieved from what information is ultimately allowed to shape reasoning.
中文翻译
搜索代理通过多轮访问外部知识扩展了大型推理模型的能力，但现有大多数系统在检索证据与使用证据之间缺乏区分。检索到的文档通常依据固定或排名策略被纳入，尽管证据效用实际上取决于代理当前的推理状态。因此，较低排名的候选可能对当前步骤至关重要，而较高排名的候选可能提供冗余、无关或误导性信息。我们将这一被忽视的问题定义为证据接纳，即在每一轮搜索中，应允许哪些检索到的候选进入代理不断演化的推理上下文。为实现这种选择性控制，我们提出SEEDS（搜索过程中的选择性证据进入），一种预览引导的证据接纳框架。SEEDS首先展示检索候选的轻量级预览，然后让代理根据其当前推理状态共同决定应完全检查哪些候选及其数量。与固定或自适应前缀策略不同，SEEDS可选择任意子集并灵活调整数量，且可直接应用于现有推理代理，也可通过学习进一步优化。在九个知识密集型问答基准测试中，无论是在本地语料库还是实时网络搜索场景下，SEEDS均持续提升搜索有效性，同时大幅减少完整证据消耗。实验分析表明，SEEDS能更有效地选出超出对应检索前缀范围的有用候选。这些结果确立了证据接纳作为智能体搜索的一个补充维度，将“检索什么”与“最终允许塑造推理的内容”分离开来。

👤 Kanay Gupta, David Y. Liu, Aditya Joshi 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.12837]

COFFEQA: Counterfactual-Guided Few-Shot Distillation for LLM Question Answering
COFFEQA：面向开卷问答的反事实引导少样本蒸馏
Knowledge distillation aims to train a smaller student model to mimic a larger teacher model for resource-constrained environments. However, the process of distillation is typically data-intensive and computationally expensive, particularly for generative tasks. Few-shot distillation offers an alternative by transferring teacher knowledge using limited training samples. However, we observe that few-shot distillation performance is heavily dependent on the choice of these training samples. In fact, we demonstrate that using more uncertain samples near the teacher's decision boundary can significantly deteriorate distillation performance. Motivated by this observation, we propose Counterfactual-guided Few-shot Distillation in Open-book Question Answering (COFFEQA), a novel strategy that aptly uses counterfactuals to provide additional supervision around challenging factual samples. In the generative QA setting, we define a counterfactual as a minimally modified context that induces the teacher to change its response to an alternative answer. We provide geometric insights on how factual-counterfactual pairs can constrain the deviation between the teacher's and student's decision boundaries. We further introduce a new metric, Context Following Difficulty (CFD) that further validates counterfactuals prior to distillation based on agreement with the intended alternative answer and dependence on the modified context. COFFEQA uses CFD-validated factual-counterfactual pairs for few-shot distillation. Experiments across five in-domain and three out-of-domain QA benchmarks using T5 and Qwen2.5 families show that COFFEQA outperforms factual-only distillation baselines under the same few-shot budget. For instance, on TriviaQA, COFFEQA improves over the factual-only baseline by 8.77 average EM points and 9.98 average F1 points under the T5 setting with 32 few-shot pairs.
中文翻译
知识蒸馏旨在训练小型学生模型模仿大型教师模型，以适应资源受限环境。然而，蒸馏过程通常数据密集且计算成本高昂，尤其在生成任务中。少样本蒸馏提供了一种替代方案，即使用有限训练样本传递教师知识。但我们观察到，少样本蒸馏性能严重依赖于训练样本的选择。事实上，我们证明使用靠近教师决策边界的不确定样本会显著恶化蒸馏性能。受此启发，我们提出反事实引导的少样本蒸馏（COFFEQA），一种新颖策略，通过反事实提供额外监督以应对困难的事实样本。在生成式问答设定中，我们将反事实定义为对上下文进行最小修改后，使教师模型响应变为替代答案的版本。我们提供了几何洞察，说明事实-反事实对如何约束教师与学生决策边界之间的偏差。我们进一步引入新指标“上下文跟随难度”（CFD），基于与预期替代答案的一致性及对修改上下文的依赖程度，对反事实进行验证。COFFEQA使用CFD验证后的事实-反事实对进行少样本蒸馏。在五个域内和三个域外问答基准上，使用T5和Qwen2.5系列模型的实验表明，COFFEQA在相同少样本预算下优于仅使用事实的蒸馏基线。例如，在TriviaQA上，COFFEQA在T5设置下使用32个少样本对时，平均EM得分提升8.77点，F1得分提升9.98点。
👤 Kavinda Kehelella, Sanghamitra Dutta 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

When Charts Deceive Web Agents: A Paired Benchmark for Misleading Visualization-Grounded Tasks
当图表误导网络代理时：一种针对误导性可视化任务的配对基准
Web agents increasingly browse, interpret, and act on web content on behalf of users. In many realistic workflows, however, the visual evidence they consume is not neutral: charts and dashboards can be misleading, shaping judgments without issuing any explicit malicious instruction. Unlike chart question answering, Web Agents treat charts as intermediate evidence whose misinterpretation can propagate into concrete downstream actions --- a reliability risk that remains underexplored. We introduce MisVisAgentBench, the first paired executable benchmark for evaluating how misleading visualizations affect Web Agent execution. It comprises 280 task instances in matched clean--misleading pairs that differ only in chart evidence. We further provide a diagnostic framework that isolates misleading-induced failures from baseline task difficulty (Zhu et al., 2025) and captures execution-level effects such as extra browsing steps and decision hesitation. Across thirteen vision-language Web Agents, replacing clean charts with misleading counterparts reduces the average task success rate by more than 34 percentage points. Moreover, these failures predominantly follow the targeted misleading action branch, suggesting that deceptive charts systematically redirect rather than randomly disrupt agent decisions. These results reveal that visualization robustness is a critical yet understudied dimension of Web Agent safety and reliability.
中文翻译
网络代理越来越多地代表用户浏览、解读并执行网页内容。然而，在许多真实工作流中，它们所消费的视觉证据并非中立：图表和仪表板可能具有误导性，从而在未发出任何明确恶意指令的情况下影响判断。与图表问答不同，网络代理将图表视为中间证据，其误读可能传播到具体的下游操作中——这种可靠性风险尚未得到充分研究。我们提出了MisVisAgentBench，这是首个用于评估误导性可视化如何影响网络代理执行的可执行配对基准。该基准包含280个成对的任务实例，每对仅在图表证据上存在差异（清洁 vs. 误导）。我们还提供了一个诊断框架，能够将由误导引起的失败与基础任务难度区分开来（Zhu等，2025），并捕捉执行层面的影响，如额外的浏览步骤和决策犹豫。在十三个视觉-语言网络代理中，用误导性图表替换清洁图表导致平均任务成功率下降超过34个百分点。此外，这些失败主要遵循目标误导行为路径，表明欺骗性图表系统性地引导而非随机干扰代理决策。这些结果揭示了可视化鲁棒性是网络代理安全性和可靠性的一个关键但被忽视的维度。

DistressBench: Evaluating Multi-Dimensional Crisis Support in Large Language Models
DistressBench：评估大语言模型在多维度危机支持中的表现
Language models have been adopted faster than evaluation practices that reliably assess their risks, and a growing share of use is affective rather than informational. Within that traffic users disclose suicide and self-harm (SSH) crises. Existing safety benchmarks score models primarily on refusal, but refusal is not the desired behavior here; the conversation requires a substantive supportive response. We introduce DistressBench, 718 English conversations with clinician-authored user turns spanning 23 SSH subcategories, each paired with a task-specific rubric of weighted atomic criteria across seven dimensions of crisis support and a clinician reference response. A model's score is the weighted average over the criteria its reply satisfies, reading directly as the fraction of required care delivered. The best of 25 frontier models satisfies 88.4% of weighted criteria and the median model 71.4%, against 98.7% for clinician references scored on the same criteria. Pooled across all 25 models, in 35.3% of conversations where a model satisfied every criterion for recognizing the crisis, it failed to route the user toward human help. The shortfall concentrates in the dimensions requiring the model to act rather than withhold. Contrasting each dimension's median model against its best separates capability limits from choices: de-escalation reaches 74.6% in the strongest model against a median of 46.0%, and safety disclaimers 84.6% against 8.3%. Multi-turn conversations score below single-turn ones (68.5% against 74.2%). We release the corpus, rubrics, judge calibration labels, and a companion annex of 20 long-horizon red-team transcripts. These results argue for evaluating crisis safety on the care a model delivers rather than on what it declines to say, and for treating de-escalation and resource routing as deployment requirements.
中文翻译
语言模型的应用速度远超可靠评估实践的发展，且越来越多的应用场景聚焦于情感而非信息。在这些应用中，用户会披露自杀与自残（SSH）危机。现有安全基准主要评估模型的拒绝能力，但在此类情境下，拒绝并非期望行为；对话需要实质性的支持回应。我们提出DistressBench，包含718段英文对话，每段均由临床医生撰写用户发言，涵盖23个SSH子类别，每条对话均配有特定任务的评分量表，涵盖危机支持的七个维度的加权原子标准，并附有临床医生参考回应。模型得分是其回应满足的标准加权平均值，直接反映所提供关怀的比例。25个前沿模型中表现最佳者满足88.4%的加权标准，中位数为71.4%，而临床医生参考回应在相同标准下得分为98.7%。综合所有25个模型，在35.3%的对话中，尽管模型已满足识别危机的所有标准，却未能引导用户寻求人工帮助。这一差距集中在需要模型采取行动而非克制的维度。通过对比各维度的最佳模型与中位模型，可区分能力限制与选择偏差：去激化能力在最强模型中达74.6%，中位数仅为46.0%；安全免责声明则分别为84.6%和8.3%。多轮对话得分低于单轮对话（68.5% vs. 74.2%）。我们公开发布语料库、评分量表、裁判校准标签以及20个长周期红队对话的附录。这些结果主张应基于模型提供的关怀质量而非其拒绝表达的内容来评估危机安全性，并将去激化和资源转介视为部署必需要求。

MADS: Multi-Agent Dialog Simulation for Robust Conversational Agents
MADS：用于构建鲁棒对话代理的多智能体对话模拟
Task-oriented conversational agents remain fragile under realistic conditions, especially when users exhibit non-cooperative behavior. Existing function-calling benchmarks underrepresent adversarial conversation trajectories, thereby limiting the training resources available for developing robust agents. We present MADS (Multi-Agent Dialog Simulation), a synthetic data generation pipeline that produces structured, diverse dialogue trajectories. We inject adversarial conversation, producing validated conversation trajectories that capture realistic, unhappy paths. Using MADS, we construct MADS-Bench: a benchmark spanning six domains, 55 personas, and 1095 conversations, with approximately 66% of conversations containing failure-prone conversation scenarios. We evaluate the robustness of the trained models using a two-stage training approach that combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR).

面向任务的对话代理在现实条件下仍显脆弱，尤其当用户表现出非合作行为时。现有函数调用基准未能充分代表对抗性对话轨迹，从而限制了训练鲁棒代理的资源。我们提出MADS（多智能体对话模拟），一个生成结构化、多样化对话轨迹的合成数据生成管道。我们注入对抗性对话，生成经过验证的、体现真实不愉快路径的对话轨迹。利用MADS，我们构建了MADS-Bench：一个覆盖六个领域、55种人格设定和1095段对话的基准，其中约66%的对话包含易出错的场景。我们采用两阶段训练方法评估训练模型的鲁棒性，结合监督微调（SFT）与可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）。

GeoFitBench-LLM: A Benchmark for Assessing Large Language Models' Capabilities in Geospatial Data Requirements Elicitation for Geo-Analytic Tasks
GeoFitBench-LLM：评估大语言模型在地理分析任务中地理空间数据需求获取能力的基准
Large language models are becoming central to GeoAI systems, yet their evaluation rarely considers the elicitation of geospatial data requirements. This omission is critical as retrieval oriented geo-analytic tasks cannot be supported reliably without first identifying the necessary datasets and constraints. GeoFitBench-LLM is introduced as a benchmark for assessing whether LLMs can infer the datasets, the supporting resources and the constraints required by geo-analytic requests in natural language. The benchmark is structured around two reasoning dimensions, Spatio-Temporal Relational and Semantic Grounding, reflecting long-standing challenges in geospatial information retrieval and related natural language tasks. Ground truth requirements are encoded in a fixed, provider-independent schema for reproducible evaluation of resource identification and attribute-level specification. Experiments on seven models under minimal prompting indicate that identifying the necessary resources is often achievable, with GPT-5.4 Thinking achieving the best result by recovering the complete set of resources in 66.67% of benchmark requests. Results further indicate that accurate fitness-for-use specification remains more difficult as inferential demands increase.

perturber: A Stateless Service for Semantics-Preserving Text Perturbations
perturber：一种保持语义的无状态文本扰动服务
Large language model (LLM) evaluations on clean benchmarks overstate model robustness: real inputs arrive perturbed, and these meaning-preserving variants routinely move model answers. Studying this systematically requires applying perturbations one at a time, reproducibly, and identically wherever they run, yet existing tools couple them to a benchmarking stack rather than packaging them as a reusable transform layer. We present perturber, a stateless service and Python library that applies exactly one semantics-preserving perturbation per call, drawn from a first-party catalog of 68 perturbations across 11 families. It turns on a single primitive, with composition left to the caller chaining calls, so the service and the library produce identical output by construction. We evaluate the tool itself, its correctness, reproducibility, and cost, rather than any model's robustness, measuring meaning-preservation per perturbation rather than assuming it. The demonstration adds a live playground, an introspectable catalog for benchmark selection, and a one-command reproducible evaluation.
中文翻译
大型语言模型在干净基准上的评估往往夸大了其鲁棒性：真实输入常带有扰动，而这些语义保持不变的变体会频繁改变模型输出。系统性研究此类现象需要逐个应用扰动，确保可复现且跨环境一致，但现有工具将扰动与评测框架耦合，未能封装为可重用的转换层。我们提出perturber，一种无状态服务和Python库，每次调用仅施加一个语义保持的扰动，来自包含68种扰动、覆盖11类的自有目录。它仅启用单一基础原语，组合逻辑由调用方自行链式调用，因此服务与库在构造上产生完全相同的输出。我们评估该工具本身的正确性、可复现性和成本，而非任何模型的鲁棒性，通过每种扰动的语义保持程度进行测量，而非假设其成立。演示部分还提供了实时交互平台、可审查的扰动目录供基准选择，以及一键可复现的评估流程。

GeopoliticalQA: Benchmarking Retrieval and Generation over Intelligence Reports
GeopoliticalQA：面向情报报告的检索与生成基准测试
Geopolitical and strategic intelligence reports constitute a rich source of expert knowledge and have increasing value in the current political climate, but their heterogeneity and analytical complexity pose challenges for retrieval-augmented question answering. We introduce a benchmark for grounded question answering over 415 open-source geopolitical and strategic intelligence reports, comprising 5,525 document-grounded question-answer pairs spanning factual, inferential, comparative, and risk-oriented questions. We propose a document-aware generation pipeline that profiles reports before generating questions and validate dataset quality through expert annotation and an LLM-as-judge protocol. Using the benchmark, we evaluate 15 retrieval systems and six open-weight LLMs for RAG. We find that models perform similarly on document-level retrieval, while at the harder chunk level retrieval quality is not driven by embedding-model scale: bge-m3, a mid-size open encoder matches an order of magnitude larger ones and outperforms commercial embedding APIs. In contrast, answer generation remains challenging even given the gold evidence, particularly for inferential and risk-oriented questions.

Reflective Prompt Optimization of a Deep-Research Agent: A Topic-Disjoint, Multi-Seed Protocol for Measuring Transferable Gains and Prompt-Level Overfitting
深度研究智能体的反思式提示优化：一种主题不重叠、多种子协议以衡量可迁移收益与提示级过拟合
Prompt optimization is an attractive alternative to reinforcement learning for adapting language-model agents, since it is cheap and produces human-readable artifacts. On factual tasks, however, it carries a failure mode that scalar-reward reinforcement learning does not. An optimizer that reads the training examples can write their answers into the prompt, so an in-distribution gain may be memorization rather than transferable behaviour. We apply reflective prompt evolution (GEPA) to the two system prompts of a multi-agent deep-research agent and evaluate it under a topic-disjoint split in which no topic is shared across train, validation, and test. Held-out citation reward rises by 0.079, from 0.702 to 0.781, with a bootstrap 95\% confidence interval of [0.051, 0.107], and the gain replicates across three seeds (mean 0.107). Nearly half of the winning prompt is a memorized answer key, yet a controlled ablation shows this content earns no reward even on the exact training questions it encodes. The large validation-minus-held-out gap is therefore small-sample selection under a noisy judge, not memorization. Varying the verifier isolates what makes the gain transfer. Decomposed per-dimension feedback is the causal ingredient, and collapsing it to a single scalar reward makes held-out transfer negative. The gain persists under an out-of-family judge and is largely reproduced by a hand-written version of the discovered recipe. Credible evaluation of prompt optimizers therefore requires held-out topics, multiple seeds, and verifier ablations. We release the candidate prompts, lineage diffs, figures, tables, and analysis.
中文翻译
提示优化是替代强化学习来适应语言模型智能体的一种有吸引力的方法，因其成本低且产生人类可读的输出。然而，在事实性任务中，它存在一种强化学习不会出现的失败模式：优化器读取训练样例后，可将答案写入提示中，导致分布内性能提升可能是记忆而非可迁移行为。我们对多智能体深度研究智能体的两个系统提示应用了反思式提示演化（GEPA），并在主题不重叠的划分下进行评估，即训练、验证和测试集之间无共享主题。保留样本的引用奖励从0.702提升至0.781，增幅为0.079，Bootstrap 95%置信区间为[0.051, 0.107]，且该增益在三个种子下均可复现（均值0.107）。近半数获胜提示是记忆化的答案键，但受控消融实验显示，即使在精确编码训练问题上，这些内容也未获得任何奖励。因此，较大的验证集与保留集差距源于噪声裁判下的小样本选择，而非记忆。通过改变验证器可识别出使增益可迁移的关键因素：分解后的维度反馈是因果成分，若将其压缩为单一标量奖励，则保留集上的迁移性能反而变为负值。该增益在家族外验证器下仍持续存在，且人工编写的发现配方基本重现了该效果。因此，可信的提示优化器评估需要保留主题、多个种子及验证器消融。我们已公开候选提示、谱系差异、图表、表格和分析结果。
👤 Elias Lumer, Sahil Sen, Vamse Kumar Subbiah, Kevin Paul 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv
[2610.12570]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Contextual Correction of Parallel Draftsfor Speculative Decoding
并行草稿的上下文修正用于推测解码
Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by using a lightweight drafter to propose multiple tokens that are subsequently verified in parallel by the target model. Recent diffusion-based drafters make this paradigm particularly attractive: deep attention-based neural backbones provide strong predictions, while drafting multiple future positions in parallel avoids the sequential cost of autoregressive generation. This parallelism, however, comes at a cost. Tokens within a draft block are predicted without access to the realized identities of preceding draft tokens, removing intra-block dependencies that would naturally be available under autoregressive generation. We introduce DCOCO (DFlash Contextual Corrector), a lightweight mechanism that restores these dependencies while preserving parallel drafting. Rather than committing to each position's top prediction, DCOCO retains multiple candidate tokens and evaluates their contextual compatibility across adjacent draft positions in parallel, enabling contextually informed token selection at low additional cost. Across four verifier backbones and ten benchmarks, DCOCO achieves the highest average throughput in every evaluated configuration, spanning greedy and stochastic decoding with and without extended reasoning, with speedups of up to 6.6x over vanilla decoding. Its advantage persists under concurrent serving: DCOCO outperforms the strongest competing method by 6-23% across 4-32 concurrent requests, while other sequential correctors fall below uncorrected DFlash at these loads. These results demonstrate that recovering intra-block dependencies through parallel contextual scoring delivers consistent acceleration across decoding regimes and serving loads.
中文翻译
推测解码通过使用轻量级草稿器提出多个token，随后由目标模型并行验证，从而加速大语言模型的推理。最近基于扩散的草稿器使这一范式更具吸引力：深层基于注意力的神经主干提供强预测能力，同时并行起草多个未来位置避免了自回归生成的串行开销。然而，这种并行性也带来了代价。草稿块内的token在预测时无法获取前序草稿token的实际身份，从而移除了自回归生成中自然存在的块内依赖关系。我们提出DCOCO（DFlash上下文校正器），一种轻量级机制，可在保持并行草稿的同时恢复这些依赖关系。DCOCO不直接采纳每个位置的最优预测，而是保留多个候选token，并在相邻草稿位置间并行评估其上下文兼容性，从而以极低额外成本实现上下文感知的token选择。在四个验证器主干和十个基准测试中，DCOCO在所有评估配置下均实现了最高平均吞吐量，涵盖贪婪与随机解码，无论是否包含扩展推理，速度提升最高可达6.6倍。其优势在并发服务下依然存在：在4至32个并发请求下，DCOCO优于最强竞争方法6%-23%，而其他串行校正器在这些负载下甚至低于未经校正的DFlash。结果表明，通过并行上下文评分恢复块内依赖关系，可在各种解码模式和服务负载下实现稳定加速。
👤 Roy Eisenstadt, Harel Lidar, Tal Gonen, Omer Luxembourg, Itamar Zimerman et al. 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv

SWE-Duel: Towards a Self-Scaling Arena for Coding Agents
SWE-Duel：迈向自扩展的编码智能体竞技场
Static coding benchmarks are fixed at release. Over time their tasks leak into training data, and stronger agents eventually saturate them. We argue that a benchmark should instead aim to be self-scaling: its tasks and oracles are created at evaluation time, so no content exists in advance to leak, and its difficulty is set by the participating agents rather than fixed at release. We introduce SWE-Duel, an arena in which two coding agents evaluate each other over mirrored Red-Blue turns. In each turn, the Red agent works on a real repository across three context-isolated sessions. It proposes and implements a new feature with feature tests, then embeds a subtle bug in that feature along with sealed bug-triggering tests. The resulting pull request is admitted only if (1) it passes the feature tests and the repository's existing tests while failing exactly the bug tests, and (2) a fresh session of the same agent can repair the bug blind, which rules out unsolvable tasks. The Blue agent then reviews the pull request without being told a bug exists. It wins only if its revision passes all three test suites, and then the two agents swap roles. We run a tournament among 11 model-harness participants on 15 repositories in five languages for 1,500 turns. The leaders win in different ways. GPT-5.5 with Codex CLI ranks first as the strongest solver, repairing 98.3% of the defects it reviews. Claude Opus 4.8 with mini-swe-agent ranks second as the strongest task author, with a 27.3% Red win rate. Finally, within one model family, tasks written by the newest version defeat a fixed-reference solver far more often than those of older versions (42.9% vs. 4.0%), giving preliminary evidence that task difficulty scales with task authoring capability.
中文翻译
静态编码基准在发布时即固定不变。随着时间推移，其任务内容会泄露到训练数据中，导致更强的智能体最终达到饱和。我们主张，基准应具备自扩展特性：其任务和评判标准在评估时动态生成，避免提前存在可泄露的内容，且难度由参与的智能体决定而非预先设定。我们提出了SWE-Duel，一个双智能体相互评估的竞技场。在每轮对战中，红方智能体在三个上下文隔离的会话中处理真实代码库，提出并实现新功能，同时嵌入隐蔽缺陷及对应的触发测试。提交的拉取请求仅在满足以下条件时被接受：（1）通过功能测试和现有测试，但恰好失败于缺陷测试；（2）同一智能体在全新会话中能盲修该缺陷，排除无法解决的任务。蓝方智能体随后审查该请求，但并不被告知存在缺陷。只有当其修改版本通过全部三套测试后才能获胜，之后两方角色互换。我们在15个不同语言的代码库上，对11个模型-工具组合进行了1500轮对战。领先者以不同方式取胜：GPT-5.5与Codex CLI组合排名第一，作为最强求解者，修复了其审查缺陷的98.3%；Claude Opus 4.8与mini-swe-agent组合排名第二，作为最强任务创建者，红方胜率高达27.3%。此外，在同一模型家族内，最新版本编写的任务击败固定参考求解器的频率远高于旧版本（42.9% vs. 4.0%），初步表明任务难度随任务创建能力提升而增长。
👤 Wei Minn, Zhensu Sun, Lwin Khin Shar, David Lo 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv

QTestBench: A Test Framework for Evaluating LLM-Generated Quantum Code
QTestBench：评估LLM生成量子代码的测试框架
Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong results in classical code generation and are increasingly used to write the unit tests that safeguard that code. Whether they can do the same for quantum software is less clear, because quantum programs are probabilistic, depend on numerical tolerances, and are judged by domain-specific checkers, so a well-formed test can still disagree with the benchmark's verdict. Existing quantum benchmarks measure whether a model produces a correct solution, but not whether its tests can distinguish correct implementations from faulty ones. We introduce QTestBench, a framework that evaluates LLM-generated quantum test suites against independent benchmark checkers, never treating the tests themselves as ground truth. For each task, QTestBench prompts models to produce syntactic, semantic, and behavioral tests, injects candidate solutions at runtime, and compares each test verdict with the checker's label under three validation cohorts, each answering a distinct diagnostic question. We benchmark five LLMs on eight PennyLane QHack challenges and ten Qiskit HumanEval tasks. Generated suites almost always execute, but discrimination lags behind: the best generator reaches a test quality of 0.71 on QHack and 0.34 on Qiskit HumanEval, so executability alone overstates test quality. Oracle-verified synthetic mutants stabilize bug-detection estimates when natural faulty solutions are scarce, as on three of ten Qiskit tasks that yield none. QTestBench offers a reproducible, cross-framework benchmark for the trustworthiness of LLM-generated quantum tests.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）在经典代码生成方面表现优异，并越来越多地用于编写保障代码质量的单元测试。然而，它们能否在量子软件领域做到同样效果尚不明确，因为量子程序具有概率性、依赖数值容差，并由领域专用检查器判断，因此即使测试语法正确，也可能与基准结果不一致。现有量子基准仅衡量模型是否生成正确解，却未检验其测试能否区分正确与错误实现。我们引入QTestBench，一个评估LLM生成测试套件的框架，始终将测试本身视为非真值，而是与独立基准检查器对比。对于每个任务，QTestBench引导模型生成语法、语义和行为测试，运行时注入候选解决方案，并在三个验证组下将每项测试结果与检查器标签比较，以回答不同的诊断问题。我们在八个PennyLane QHack挑战和十个Qiskit HumanEval任务上对五种LLM进行基准测试。生成的测试套件几乎总能执行，但区分能力滞后：最佳生成器在QHack上测试质量达0.71，在Qiskit HumanEval上仅为0.34，说明仅执行成功并不能反映真实测试质量。通过Oracle验证的合成变异稳定了故障检测估计，尤其在自然故障解稀缺的情况下（如十项Qiskit任务中有三项无自然故障）。QTestBench提供了一个可复现、跨框架的基准，用于评估LLM生成量子测试的可信度。
👤 Meriem Terki, Nouhaila Innan, Minghao Shao, Muhammad Kashif, Alberto Marchisio et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

OccludeBench: Can VLMs Find What's Missing?
OccludeBench：视觉语言模型能否发现缺失的内容？
How well can vision-language models (VLMs) reason about what is missing from an image? We study this question with OccludeBench, a manually curated benchmark for recovering fully occluded objects from the visual evidence that remains. OccludeBench distinguishes direct cases, where the hidden object must be recovered from a specific physical cue such as a shadow or reflection, from inferential cases, where it can be inferred from broader scene context. Across five VLMs, accuracy averages 42.7% on direct cases versus 62.7% on inferential cases, exhibiting a 20.0-point gap. Humans attain higher accuracies of 63.4% versus 68.5% for direct and inferential images, respectively, with no significant gap. To locate this failure, we introduce OccludeTrace, a four-stage diagnostic that isolates where the visual reasoning process breaks down. The bottleneck emerges when models must recover the relevant object from the projection within the full image: they succeed on only 50% of failed examples, compared with 91% of examples they answer correctly. Extended reasoning, tool use, and few-shot prompting yield inconsistent improvements and do not reliably eliminate the gap. In contrast, an inpaint-then-confront intervention, which prompts models to first generate a candidate hidden object and then evaluate that hypothesis against the visible physical cue, reduces the gap to a statistically non-significant difference for both models with image-generation capabilities. Together, our results establish a tractable, controlled diagnostic for isolating how VLMs reason about visual content that is missing from an image.
中文翻译
视觉语言模型（VLMs）在图像中缺失内容的推理能力如何？我们通过OccludeBench——一个手动构建的基准测试，研究从剩余视觉证据中恢复完全遮挡物体的能力。OccludeBench区分直接案例（需从特定物理线索如阴影或反射中恢复隐藏物体）与推断案例（需从更广泛的场景上下文中推断）。在五种VLM中，直接案例准确率为42.7%，推断案例为62.7%，差距达20.0个百分点。人类在直接和推断案例中的准确率分别为63.4%和68.5%，无显著差距。为定位失败原因，我们引入OccludeTrace，一种四阶段诊断框架，识别视觉推理过程的断裂点。瓶颈出现在模型必须从完整图像投影中恢复相关对象时：在失败样本中仅50%成功，而在正确回答的样本中则达91%。扩展推理、工具使用和少量示例提示带来的改进不一致，无法可靠消除差距。相比之下，一种‘先修复再验证’干预策略（即先生成候选隐藏对象，再将其与可见物理线索对比验证）使具备图像生成能力的模型在两类任务间的差距降至统计上不显著水平。我们的结果建立了一个可控、可操作的诊断框架，用于揭示VLM如何推理图像中缺失的视觉内容。
👤 Jasin Cekinmez, Addison J. Wu, Ryo Mitsuhashi, Linrong Cai, Xingyu Fu et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

UkrMeme: Multimodal Explainable Persuasion Detection in Ukrainian Memes
UkrMeme：乌克兰梗图中的多模态可解释说服检测
Memes play a central role in modern online communication, especially in contexts of political conflict where visual and textual cues combine to convey persuasive messages. We study persuasive technique detection in Ukrainian memes using vision-language models (VLMs). We introduce UkrMeme, a dataset of 200 organically produced Ukrainian wartime memes annotated for persuasion techniques, and PTM-UA, a Ukrainian translation of a subset of the Propaganda 950 benchmark that preserves original visual content while translating meme text. We evaluate VLMs in a zero-shot prompting setup across image, text, and multimodal inputs using hierarchical F1 metrics. Finally, we compare Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) and model-generated token attributions and analyze the semantic categories of evidence emphasized by each explanation method.
中文翻译
梗图在现代在线交流中扮演核心角色，尤其在政治冲突背景下，视觉与文本线索结合传递说服性信息。我们利用视觉语言模型（VLMs）研究乌克兰梗图中的说服技巧检测。我们提出了UkrMeme数据集，包含200个自然生成的乌克兰战时梗图，并标注了说服技巧；以及PTM-UA，即Propaganda 950基准的一个乌克兰语子集，保留原始视觉内容的同时翻译了梗图文本。我们在零样本提示设置下，针对图像、文本和多模态输入评估VLMs，采用层次F1指标。最后，我们比较了层间相关性传播（LRP）与模型生成的词元归因，并分析了每种解释方法强调的证据语义类别。

APEX-Harm: Evaluating Agent Safety in Shared Professional Workspaces
APEX-Harm：评估共享专业工作空间中的代理安全
Professional agents increasingly work through command-line interfaces and multiple tools over shared workspaces containing files, messages, and business applications. These environments create safety risks that are difficult to capture with attacks placed at a single, known read location. We introduce APEX-Harm, a benchmark for evaluating prompt and script injection against long-horizon agents performing finance, consulting, and legal tasks in large workspaces. Each task contains 115 to 328 files, together with email, calendar, chat, document, spreadsheet, PDF, and code-execution tools exposed through MCP servers. The benchmark includes six attack categories covering static and dynamic injections, task-specific script replacement, and attacker-provided generic scripts, and evaluates attack success, exposure, and task performance. We evaluate 12 frontier models on 180 task instances per model. Opus 5 and Sonnet 5 have the lowest overall attack success rates (8% and 20%), while task-specific script replacement and attacker-provided generic script attacks remain substantially more effective, averaging 81-84% and 73% attack success, respectively. Gemini 3.8 Flash achieves the highest mean task score (0.877) while still exhibiting a 33% attack success rate. These results show that completing the requested task is not sufficient evidence of safe behavior: an agent can produce a correct deliverable while also performing an unauthorized action in the workspace.
中文翻译
专业智能体越来越多地通过命令行界面和多种工具在包含文件、消息和业务应用的共享工作空间中工作。这些环境带来了难以通过单一已知读取位置攻击捕捉的安全风险。我们提出了APEX-Harm，一个用于评估长周期智能体在大型工作空间中执行金融、咨询和法律任务时面对提示注入和脚本注入的基准测试。每个任务包含115至328个文件，以及通过MCP服务器暴露的电子邮件、日历、聊天、文档、电子表格、PDF和代码执行工具。该基准涵盖六类攻击，包括静态和动态注入、特定任务脚本替换以及攻击者提供的通用脚本，并评估攻击成功率、暴露程度和任务性能。我们在每种模型上评估了12个前沿模型，共180个任务实例。Opus 5和Sonnet 5的整体攻击成功率最低（分别为8%和20%），而特定任务脚本替换和攻击者提供的通用脚本攻击仍显著更有效，平均攻击成功率分别为81-84%和73%。Gemini 3.8 Flash在任务得分上达到最高均值（0.877），但仍表现出33%的攻击成功率。这些结果表明，完成请求任务并不足以证明行为安全：智能体可能生成正确交付物的同时，在工作空间中执行未经授权的操作。
👤 Ziyi Yang, Adel Bibi, Guohao Li, Bernard Ghanem, Carlos Hinojosa 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

ProgramGym: Scaling Training Environments for Long-Horizon Program Reproduction Agents
ProgramGym：面向长时程程序复现智能体的可扩展训练环境
Coding agents are increasingly used to build programs from scratch as software engineers do rather than to resolve issues within a repository or to generate a repository from a given requirements document. However, scalable verifiable training environments that force agents to discover what is required and to decide how the system should be designed remain scarce. Moreover, existing agent workflows also cannot sustain long-horizon development in which the agent explores the reference program before implementation. To address these challenges, we introduce ProgramGym, a framework consisting of Program-Env and Program-Agent for scalable training environments of long-horizon program reproduction agents. Specifically, Program-Env constructs verifiable training environments from repositories through a feedback-driven loop that repairs runtime sandbox builds and the quality of black-box tests. Using this novel pipeline, we obtain 1,648 training environments spanning C, C++, Go, and Rust. Program-Agent combines a role-isolated workflow of analysis, implementation, and review with revision management for compilation checks and failure recovery. Experiments on ProgramBench show that both Program-Env and Program-Agent improve performance on program reproduction. Fine-tuning on trajectories with these constructed environments raises the performance of Qwen3.8-27B from 34.87% to 42.84%. Program-Agent further raises the base and SFT models to 55.71% and 56.25%, both exceeding some flagship models. We will release the ProgramGym to support further research on coding agents that build programs from scratch.
中文翻译
编码智能体正越来越多地被用于从零开始构建程序，如同软件工程师一般，而非仅在仓库内修复问题或根据需求文档生成仓库。然而，能够支持可扩展验证的训练环境仍十分稀缺，这类环境需迫使智能体自主发现需求并决定系统设计方式。此外，现有智能体工作流也无法支撑长时程开发——即智能体在实现前探索参考程序。为应对这些挑战，我们提出ProgramGym，一个由Program-Env和Program-Agent组成的框架，用于构建长时程程序复现智能体的可扩展训练环境。具体而言，Program-Env通过反馈驱动循环，修复运行时沙箱构建和黑盒测试质量，从代码仓库构建可验证的训练环境。利用这一新流程，我们获得了涵盖C、C++、Go和Rust的1,648个训练环境。Program-Agent结合角色隔离的工作流（分析、实现、评审）与修订管理机制，支持编译检查和失败恢复。在ProgramBench上的实验表明，Program-Env和Program-Agent均提升了程序复现性能。在这些构建环境中进行微调后，Qwen3.8-27B的性能从34.87%提升至42.84%。Program-Agent进一步将基础模型和SFT模型提升至55.71%和56.25%，均超过部分旗舰模型。我们将公开ProgramGym，以支持从零构建程序的编码智能体研究。

JANUS: Enhancing Long-Horizon Autonomous Research with Co-Evolving World Models
JANUS：通过共演化世界模型增强长周期自主研究
Long-horizon autonomous research has shown promise in accelerating scientific progress through sustained cycles of hypothesis generation, implementation, and validation. However, this Generate-Execute-Feedback paradigm relies on reactive trial-and-error, where costly and time-consuming executions introduce severe feedback delays, throttling evolutionary progress under explosively expanding search space. To address this challenge, we propose JANUS, a framework that unifies future outcomes forecasting and past experience reflection through a Predict-Verify-Evolve loop driven by a co-evolving world model. Within an evolving graph, the research agent proposes candidates while world model scores them by forecasting value improvement, information gain, and exploration utility, then accordingly selects, refines, or prunes branches prior to execution. After receiving ground-truth feedback, JANUS distills experience into evolving skills: hierarchical skills for research agent, exploration-strategy skills for world model, and their coordination protocols. This mechanism enables continual co-evolution: the research agent sharpens candidate generation while the world model refines its predictive judgment over extended horizons. Extensive evaluations on MLS-Bench-Lite and real research tasks demonstrate that JANUS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving both higher evolving efficiency and ceiling.
中文翻译
长周期自主研究在通过持续的假设生成、实施与验证循环加速科学进步方面展现出巨大潜力。然而，这种‘生成-执行-反馈’范式依赖于反应式的试错过程，昂贵且耗时的执行引入了严重的反馈延迟，在搜索空间急剧扩张的背景下严重制约了进化进展。为应对这一挑战，我们提出 JANUS 框架，通过一个由共演化世界模型驱动的‘预测-验证-演化’循环，统一未来结果预测与过往经验反思。在一个动态演化的图结构中，研究智能体提出候选方案，世界模型通过预测价值提升、信息增益和探索效用对它们进行评分，随后据此选择、优化或剪枝分支后再执行。接收真实反馈后，JANUS 将经验提炼为不断演化的技能：研究智能体的分层技能、世界模型的探索策略技能及其协调协议。这一机制实现了持续的共演化：研究智能体不断提升候选生成能力，世界模型则增强其在长期范围内的预测判断力。在 MLS-Bench-Lite 和真实科研任务上的广泛评估表明，JANUS 显著优于现有最先进基线，实现了更高的演化效率和上限性能。
👤 Mengdi Liu, Chenghao Jia, Hong Chang, Mingjie He, Shiguang Shan et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

Locate, Judge, Read: Role-Specialized Tool Agents over a Knowledge-Point Index for Open-Domain Multimodal Document QA
定位、判断、阅读：基于知识点索引的角色专业化工具智能体用于开放域多模态文档问答
Question answering over large heterogeneous document collections requires first finding the right passages and then reading them, yet current retrieval-augmented agents give one model both kinds of tools and let it schedule itself. We present KnowForge, a pipeline separated by job: a hybrid-retrieval knowledge-point index; a Locator agent that can only search; a code-scheduled bridge judge that forces the missing second hop; a typed, size-bounded evidence pool that merges every search's ranking; a Deep Reader agent that can only read text, images and tables; and a synthesizer with a format checker. With a self-hosted Qwen3.6-35B-A3B (3B active), KnowForge reaches F1 47.5 on the full open-domain M3DocVQA (2,441 questions, 3,368 PDFs), +11.0 over the published M3DocRAG system and +7.1 over one-shot retrieve-and-answer on the same index and model, and 53.5 with DeepSeek-V4-Flash. On MultiHop-RAG it reaches 73.5 accuracy, above the strongest published RAG and GraphRAG results (71.2); on the single-document MMLongBench-Doc it lifts the same model from 34.8 to 47.0, level with GPT-4o reading page images (46.3). Per-job ablations on the full benchmarks attribute the gain: the Deep Reader is worth 3.8, 9.8 and 0 points on the three corpora, tracking how visual each is; merging both agents into one with all tools and twice the rounds loses 1.9 F1 because it keeps searching instead of reading; a round-budget sweep shows a step at four Locator rounds and a plateau beyond; query latency scales with model calls rather than corpus size. We serve the three indexed benchmarks as read-only knowledge bases (free registration) and release all prompts and the runner code: this https URL
中文翻译

Travel Back To Move Forward: Retrospective Exploration and Evolving for Long Horizon Agents
回溯以向前：面向长时程智能体的回顾性探索与演化
Large language model agents increasingly operate in long-horizon environments where early mistakes can trigger cascading failures and trap agents in dead-end trajectories. Agents must therefore be able to proactively recover from such states and turn their exploratory experience into guidance for subsequent decisions. We introduce ReTRAIL, a framework that enables agents to rewind the environment to earlier states while preserving valid progress and carrying forward knowledge learned from abandoned exploration. The environment state moves backward while the agent's knowledge continues to move forward. ReTRAIL trains a shared policy with two complementary signals: State-Anchored GRPO compares alternative continuations from revisited states to improve execution, while a branch-advancement reward guides reflection through the progress of subsequent exploration. Experiments across four interactive benchmark MineSweeper, Sokoban, ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that simply adding a rewind primitive provides limited benefit and can even degrade performance. In contrast, ReTRAIL enables agents to rewind from unproductive states and use insights from abandoned branches to guide subsequent exploration. These results demonstrate the value of coupling environment rewinding with experience reuse within an ongoing task for long-horizon task solving. The code is available at this https URL .
中文翻译
大型语言模型智能体越来越多地运行在长时程环境中，早期错误可能引发级联失败并使智能体陷入死胡同轨迹。因此，智能体必须能够主动从这些状态中恢复，并将探索经验转化为后续决策的指导。我们提出了ReTRAIL框架，使智能体能够在保留有效进展的同时，将环境状态回退到早期状态，并携带从被放弃的探索中学习到的知识继续前进。环境状态向后移动，而智能体的知识则持续向前发展。ReTRAIL通过两个互补信号训练共享策略：基于状态锚定的GRPO比较从重访状态出发的替代延续路径以改进执行，而分支推进奖励则通过后续探索的进展引导反思。在四个交互式基准测试（MineSweeper、Sokoban、ALFWorld和ScienceWorld）上的实验表明，仅添加回退原语带来的收益有限，甚至可能降低性能。相比之下，ReTRAIL使智能体能够从无产出状态回退，并利用被放弃分支中的洞察来指导后续探索。这些结果证明了在持续任务中将环境回退与经验复用相结合对于长时程任务求解的价值。代码可在该链接获取。

EmbodiedCLBench: Evaluating Continual Learning for Self-Evolving Embodied Agents
EmbodiedCLBench：评估自演化具身智能体的持续学习能力
Continual learning requires models to accumulate past experience and transfer it to new tasks. Despite substantial research on continual learning, existing work remains largely confined to non-embodied settings, without extending to embodied environments. To address this gap, we introduce EmbodiedCLBench, the first benchmark in embodied simulation for evaluating the continual learning capability of self-evolving agents. With compositional generalization as the core evaluation principle, our benchmark evaluates whether agents can first learn from basic tasks and then recombine the acquired skills to solve complex, unseen advanced tasks. Based on EmbodiedCLBench, we conduct extensive experiments across multiple agent harnesses and learning methods, revealing consistent limitations and distinctive behaviors. Our results show that while advanced tasks can be hardly solved under zero-shot setting, the experience from basic tasks enables consistent improvement. Such benefit from the experience remains stable even if the task complexity increases. However, this improvement does not scale accordingly with the number of learning samples, and current agents cannot extract meaningful information from additional experience. Collectively, our findings highlight continual learning as a fundamental yet underexplored capability, and our benchmark offers valuable resources for designing effective self-evolving agents in embodied environments. Our code and dataset are available at this https URL .
中文翻译
持续学习要求模型积累过往经验并将其迁移到新任务中。尽管持续学习研究已取得大量成果，但现有工作仍主要局限于非具身环境，未能扩展至具身环境。为填补这一空白，我们引入EmbodiedCLBench，这是首个用于具身仿真环境中评估自演化智能体持续学习能力的基准。以组合泛化为核心评估原则，我们的基准测试智能体是否能先从基础任务中学习，再重组所获技能以解决复杂且未见过的高级任务。基于EmbodiedCLBench，我们在多个智能体架构和学习方法上进行了广泛实验，揭示了系统性的局限性和独特行为。结果显示，尽管零样本设置下高级任务几乎无法解决，但来自基础任务的经验能带来一致的性能提升。这种经验带来的益处即使在任务复杂度增加时依然稳定。然而，这种提升并未随学习样本数量成比例增长，当前智能体无法从额外经验中提取有意义的信息。总体而言，我们的发现强调持续学习是一种基础但尚未充分探索的能力，而本基准为设计有效的具身环境中自演化智能体提供了宝贵资源。代码和数据集可在该链接获取。

SmartSalesBench: A Resistance-Aware Multi-Turn Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Sales Agents
SmartSalesBench：面向LLM销售智能体的抗阻型多轮对话基准
We present SmartSalesBench, the first multi-turn conversational benchmark explicitly designed to evaluate large language model (LLM) sales agents against psychologically grounded, resistant buyer personas. Existing task-oriented dialogue benchmarks assume cooperative users and measure task completion; SmartSalesBench inverts this assumption, placing agents against personas programmed to resist and challenge across conversations of up to twenty turns. The benchmark includes five agent profiles, twelve buyer personas, fifteen smartphone products across six price tiers, and four controlled experiments with 3 iterations totaling 13,680 sessions. Both sales-agent and buyer persona generations are grounded through retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). We introduce nine evaluation metrics grouped into three tiers: primary outcome metrics (CR, BRS, RIR), secondary quality metrics (SMS, PFS, ETI), and exploratory metrics planned for future reporting (ITS, ARI, SPI). Our evaluation combines automated LLM-as-judge scoring with internal human assessment (n = 50, \k{appa} = 0.71, Pearson r = 0.81 with BRS).

MVGraph: Benchmarking LVLMs on Evidence Composition across Multi-View Visual Graph
MVGraph：跨多视角视觉图的证据组合对大视觉语言模型的基准测试
We introduce MVGraph, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models (LVLMs) on multi-graph reasoning where the evidence required by a query is distributed across multiple interrelated visual graph views. Although such evidence commonly resides in source-specific graphs, temporal snapshots, or local observations, existing graph reasoning benchmarks remain confined to a single self-contained structure, assessing only graph perception and reasoning while leaving the alignment and composition of evidence across views underexplored. MVGraph comprises 8k instances spanning 8 \textit{graph-theoretic} tasks, abstracted from two multi-graph scenarios (partial and temporal graph views). Each problem is programmatically constructed and verified to be unsolvable from a single view. Beyond end-to-end evaluation, we decompose multi-graph reasoning into four complementary capabilities---Perception, Alignment, Composition, and Reasoning---and design 29 diagnostic tasks with 64k instances to probe each one, along with golden intermediate representations for evaluating the intermediate process. Across 15 LVLMs, we reveal the difficulty of multi-graph reasoning and identify cross-view edge alignment and relation-level composition as prominent bottlenecks through stage-oriented diagnostics. Meanwhile, temporal constraints and increasing view counts expose further capability boundaries. Code and data are available at this https URL .

VEPA: Verified Evolving Formal Policy for Lifelong LLM Agent Guardrails
VEPA：可验证演化的形式化策略用于持续学习的LLM智能体防护机制
LLM agents increasingly operate over sensitive resources, such as private documents and mutable system states, which can create serious safety and security risks. Runtime guardrails should therefore make inspectable, reproducible, and deterministic allow or refuse decisions before potentially irreversible agent actions are executed. However, safety and security requirements are rarely complete before deployment, so guardrails need to keep adapting as new risks emerge. Existing adaptive guardrails learn from deployment experience, but they usually apply safety policies through LLM inference, which can lead to inconsistent decisions and ambiguous policy interpretations. Formal guardrails, on the other hand, enforce policies deterministically, but they usually rely on pre-deployment or task-specific policies and have no mechanism to adapt to newly observed risks. We introduce VEPA, a framework for the verified evolution of formal agent guardrails. VEPA represents the guardrail as a formal policy of executable symbolic rules over typed agent events. During deployment, we use an LLM guided by deployment feedback to propose policy patches that fix newly observed failures while preserving previously correct behavior. Then, a deterministic verifier only accepts policies that are structurally valid and conflict-free. Finally, an evidence-aware authority layer activates a verified rule only when it has enough supporting evidence, so uncertain updates cannot directly affect guardrail decisions. Across ASSEBench-Safety, ASSEBench-Security, and ATBench, VEPA consistently outperforms baselines while expanding the scope of deterministic formal-policy enforcement. These gains also hold under more challenging deployment conditions, including mixed streams, corrupted feedback, and longer horizons.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体越来越多地操作敏感资源，如私有文档和可变系统状态，这可能带来严重的安全与隐私风险。因此，运行时防护机制应在执行不可逆动作前做出可检查、可复现且确定性的允许或拒绝决策。然而，安全与安全需求在部署前往往不完整，防护机制需随新风险出现而持续适应。现有自适应防护机制通过LLM推理学习部署经验，但常导致决策不一致和策略解释模糊。形式化防护机制虽能确定性地执行策略，但通常依赖于预部署或任务特定策略，缺乏应对新风险的适应机制。我们提出VEPA框架，实现形式化智能体防护机制的可验证演化。VEPA将防护机制表示为在类型化智能体事件上可执行的符号规则形式策略。部署期间，我们利用部署反馈引导的LLM提出修复新发现失败的策略补丁，同时保持先前正确行为不变。随后，确定性验证器仅接受结构有效且无冲突的策略。最后，基于证据的权威层仅在拥有足够支持证据时激活已验证规则，确保不确定更新不会直接影响防护决策。在ASSEBench-Safety、ASSEBench-Security和ATBench上，VEPA始终优于基线方法，同时扩展了确定性形式策略执行的范围。这些优势在更具挑战性的部署条件下（如混合流、损坏反馈、更长时间窗）依然成立。
👤 Duy Nguyen, Lesly Miculicich, Bhavana Dalvi Mishra, Shuo Li, Krishnamurthy Dj Dvijotham et al. 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv

Meta-Reflective Evolution Effectively Improves Production AI Systems
元反思进化有效提升生产环境AI系统的性能
Large Language Model (LLM)-guided evolutionary optimization has successfully improved prompts, programs, and even entire AI systems. Most optimization methods select a candidate from the search frontier, then mutate it in one LLM pass based only on its failures. This focus on the frontier can stall on production AI systems, where isolated failures reveal little about complex, hidden interactions. We introduce CoCoEvolve (Cortex Code Evolve), an evolutionary optimizer built around what we call meta-reflection, wherein an agentic mutator (Cortex Code) selectively retrieves evidence from the entire evolutionary history and probes the live system before verifying and proposing mutations. We apply CoCoEvolve to optimize three classes of production AI systems: data agents, LLM-powered data pipelines, and data engineering workflows. On DABStep, CoCoEvolve tops the public leaderboard, reaching 92.8% held-out hard accuracy compared to 78.4% for Meta-Harness and 30.4% for GEPA under matched conditions. Across six LLM data pipeline workloads, it outperforms every evaluated baseline. On data-eng-bench, CoCoEvolve lifts test-set solve rate from 59.1% to 77.4%, past the best baseline of 73.6%. It also has 11 percentage points higher accuracy than GEPA on HotpotQA while remaining competitive with specialized optimizers on established mathematical and systems benchmarks. Matched ablations confirm that each component of CoCoEvolve contributes to these gains, demonstrating the value of meta-reflection for optimizing production AI systems.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）引导的进化优化已成功用于改进提示词、程序乃至整个AI系统。大多数优化方法仅从搜索前沿选择候选者，并在一次LLM调用中根据其失败进行突变。这种对前沿的关注在生产环境中容易停滞，因为孤立的失败难以揭示复杂隐藏的交互。我们提出CoCoEvolve（Cortex Code Evolve），一种基于“元反思”的进化优化器，其中代理型突变器（Cortex Code）会从完整的进化历史中选择性检索证据，并在验证和提出突变前探查实时系统。我们将CoCoEvolve应用于三类生产环境AI系统：数据代理、LLM驱动的数据流水线和数据工程工作流。在DABStep上，CoCoEvolve在公开排行榜上领先，达到92.8%的保留硬样本准确率，远超Meta-Harness的78.4%和GEPA的30.4%（在相同条件下）。在六个LLM数据流水线工作负载中，其表现优于所有评估的基线。在data-eng-bench上，CoCoEvolve将测试集求解率从59.1%提升至77.4%，超过最佳基线的73.6%。在HotpotQA上，其准确率比GEPA高出11个百分点，同时在经典数学与系统基准上仍保持与专用优化器相当的竞争力。匹配消融实验确认了CoCoEvolve各组件均贡献显著，证明了元反思在优化生产AI系统中的价值。

SpireBench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Strategic Play of Agentic Models with Slay the Spire
SpireBench：基于《杀戮尖塔》评估智能体的长周期战略表现
Agentic large language models now tackle complex tasks, yet strategic reasoning over long horizons is under-studied: most agentic benchmarks are short and episodic, and performance on long tasks is hard to dissect into stored knowledge, reasoning and luck. We introduce SpireBench, in which models play the complete commercial deck-builder Slay the Spire (StS), where early decisions decide long-term success. Across 20 large language models (LLMs) at the game's highest difficulty, frontier models beat heuristic baselines and match the average human player but remain far behind experts. This gap widens as the run lengthens: the strongest models win the first act as often as experts, then fall behind. What separates strong models from weak is how well they play each fight; all of them misjudge risk over a run, preserving health rather than spending it to grow stronger. A comprehensive, self-consistent renaming of every game term shows that part of their performance rests on prior knowledge of the game, and models do not improve from the memories they write across runs, which are often wrong and overly cautious. Our harness is the first to give a model the complete original game, and we compare it with existing harnesses. Overall, SpireBench offers an unsaturated long-horizon benchmark with tools to locate where and why agents fail.
中文翻译
智能体大语言模型如今能够处理复杂任务，但长周期的战略推理仍缺乏充分研究：大多数智能体基准测试短小且具有片段性，而长任务的表现难以分解为存储知识、推理能力和运气成分。我们引入SpireBench，让模型完整体验商业级卡牌构建游戏《杀戮尖塔》（StS），其中早期决策决定长期成功。在最高难度下对20个大语言模型进行测试，前沿模型击败了启发式基线并达到平均人类玩家水平，但仍远落后于专家。随着游戏进程延长，差距进一步扩大：最强模型在第一幕胜率与专家相当，随后迅速落后。区分强弱模型的关键在于每场战斗的策略执行质量；所有模型在整局游戏中都错误评估风险，倾向于保命而非消耗生命以增强实力。一项全面且自洽的游戏术语重命名分析表明，部分性能依赖于模型对游戏的先验知识，而模型并未从跨轮次所写的记忆中获得改进，这些记忆往往错误且过于保守。我们的Harness是首个让模型接触完整原版游戏的系统，并与现有Harness进行了比较。总体而言，SpireBench提供了一个尚未饱和的长周期基准，具备定位智能体失败位置与原因的工具。
👤 Scott C. Lowe, Sander W. Keemink 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv

[2610.13896]
agent-memory
Structural Automata for Neural Semantic Parsing
神经语义解析中的结构自动机
Neural semantic parsers generate meaning representations token by token, yet well-formedness depends on references, scope, and graph relations that unfold during generation. Constrained decoding prevents locally invalid tokens but does not guarantee that a valid prefix can be completed within a fixed output-length limit. We investigate structural automata at three stages of DRS parsing (decoding, post hoc processing, and training) and additionally evaluate decoding on AMR. For decoding, we introduce accepting-path decoding (APD), which maintains a verified completion within the remaining length limit and follows the model's preferred token whenever that token can also be completed. APD guarantees an accepted output without retracting tokens. Under tight length limits, it improves Smatch++ over local masking by 5.1 and 2.4 points on DRS and by 6.4 and 3.2 points on AMR, comparable to rollback under the same limit, which retracts tokens. For post hoc processing, invalid outputs concentrate on the same inputs: eight unconstrained samples yield no valid output for 10.2% of inputs with limited training data, compared with 0.4% with full data, where filtering comes within 0.2 Smatch++ points of APD. For training, penalizing automaton-rejected tokens improves development Smatch++ by 3.6-11.0 points after 10% of training, but this advantage diminishes as cross-entropy alone reduces the probability assigned to rejected tokens from 14% to below 2% within the first sixth of training with limited data.
中文翻译
神经语义解析器逐标记生成语义表示，但有效性依赖于生成过程中展开的引用、作用域和图关系。约束解码可防止局部无效标记，但无法保证在固定输出长度限制内能完成有效前缀。我们研究了在DRS解析的三个阶段（解码、事后处理和训练）中使用结构自动机的方法，并额外评估了AMR上的解码效果。在解码阶段，我们提出接受路径解码（APD），该方法在剩余长度限制内维护一个已验证的完成路径，并在模型偏好标记也可完成时遵循该标记。APD确保接受输出而不回溯标记。在严格长度限制下，APD在DRS上比局部掩码提升5.1和2.4分，在AMR上提升6.4和3.2分，与相同限制下的回滚方法相当（后者会回溯标记）。在事后处理阶段，无效输出集中在相同输入上：八组无约束样本在有限训练数据下导致10.2%的输入无法生成有效输出，而全数据下仅为0.4%，此时过滤方法与APD相差仅0.2 Smatch++分。在训练阶段，惩罚自动机拒绝的标记使开发集Smatch++在训练10%后提升3.6至11.0分，但随着交叉熵单独将拒绝标记的概率从14%降至训练初期的2%以下，这一优势逐渐减弱。
