Task Context Shapes Component Roles in Language Models
任务上下文塑造语言模型中组件的角色
Large language models reuse the same attention heads across tasks, but identifying these shared heads does not explain what they do differently in each task. A natural source of inspiration is task-dependent interactions among shared brain regions, which motivate a network view of language-model computation. Here, we compare fixed model components across tasks using identical graph facts, statements specifying which directed connections are present. We measure how interventions affect attention heads and the activation state in later layers (influence), as well as the answer (importance). Task changes redistribute the influence in five open-weight models spanning four families and 1.5-14 billion parameters. In Gemma-2-9B and Qwen3-14B, an activation edit changes an early internal activation vector and switches which fact the model reads while the written instruction stays fixed. The same heads then respond to different graph facts and influence later components differently. For each prompt, we construct two linear response maps, one before the edit and one after it. Each map predicts a later head's response from a measured change in an earlier head. For a separate factual intervention, the map for the corresponding condition reduces mean squared prediction error by 53.6% in Gemma and 5.0% in Qwen compared with the average of the two maps. These findings show that task context changes both which graph facts the shared components respond to and how their outputs affect later computation. A network account may help explain how models use shared parameters to perform different computations. Measurements of task-dependent influence could also guide interventions intended to alter one behavior while preserving others.
中文翻译
大型语言模型在不同任务中复用相同的注意力头，但识别这些共享头并不能解释它们在每项任务中的不同作用。一个自然的灵感来源是共享脑区之间的任务依赖性交互，这启发了对语言模型计算的网络视角。在此，我们使用相同的图事实（指定哪些有向连接存在）比较不同任务中的固定模型组件。我们测量干预对注意力头及后续层激活状态的影响（影响力），以及对答案的重要性。任务变化重新分配了五个开放权重模型（涵盖四个系列，参数量15亿至140亿）中的影响力分布。在Gemma-2-9B和Qwen3-14B中，一次激活编辑会改变早期内部激活向量，并切换模型所读取的事实，而写入的指令保持不变。相同的头随后对不同的图事实作出响应，并以不同方式影响后续组件。针对每个提示，我们构建两个线性响应映射：一个在编辑前，一个在编辑后。每个映射从早期头的测量变化预测后期头的响应。对于另一项事实干预，对应条件的映射在Gemma中将均方预测误差降低53.6%，在Qwen中降低5.0%，优于两个映射的平均值。这些发现表明，任务上下文不仅改变了共享组件所响应的图事实，也改变了其输出对后续计算的影响方式。网络视角可能有助于解释模型如何利用共享参数执行不同计算。对任务依赖性影响力的测量还可指导旨在改变某一行为而保留其他行为的干预。

Personalization Without Stereotyping: Do Speech LLMs Know When to Turn a Deaf Ear?
个性化而不刻板化：语音大模型何时该装聋作哑？
Recent speech LLMs can leverage rich paralinguistic cues, enabling more complex and personalized interactions. However, little work investigates how this sensitivity can lead to stereotyping, and existing evaluations largely measure demographic disparities without distinguishing beneficial personalization from stereotypical behavior. We introduce BASIS-Bench, a benchmark designed to disentangle these two aspects through three axes: (1) demographic robustness, measuring disparities in content-based tasks across speakers and demographic classification capabilities; (2) stereotypical bias, measuring whether perceived demographic cues trigger unprompted stereotypical behavior; and (3) personalization, measuring whether models can tailor responses to demographic characteristics when explicitly requested. We evaluate eight recent speech LLMs and find that, despite often recognizing demographic cues, most fail to translate them into appropriate personalization. Explicit demographic labels improve personalization but also amplify stereotypical associations already present in the textual backbones. These findings highlight the need to train speech LLMs not only to perceive demographic cues, but to learn when they should shape their responses.

近期的语音大模型能够利用丰富的副语言线索，实现更复杂和个性化的交互。然而，很少有研究探讨这种敏感性如何导致刻板印象，且现有评估大多仅衡量人口统计差异，未能区分有益的个性化与刻板行为。我们提出了BASIS-Bench，一个旨在分离这两个方面的基准测试，涵盖三个维度：（1）人口统计鲁棒性，衡量不同说话人之间内容任务的差异及人口统计分类能力；（2）刻板偏见，衡量感知到的人口统计线索是否引发未经提示的刻板行为；（3）个性化，衡量模型在明确请求下能否根据人口统计特征定制回应。我们评估了八个最新的语音大模型，发现尽管多数模型能识别人口统计线索，但很少能将其转化为适当的个性化响应。显式的人口统计标签虽提升了个性化表现，但也放大了文本基础模型中已存在的刻板关联。这些发现强调，训练语音大模型不仅需要感知人口统计线索，更要学会在何时应据此调整回应。

From Structure to Validity: Mechanism-Guided Generation and Refinement of LLM Rubrics
从结构到有效性：机制引导的大语言模型评分标准生成与优化
Rubrics have been widely used to evaluate and optimize language models on open-ended tasks. However, existing work primarily focuses on generating better rubrics, with limited understanding of what makes rubric design effective. In this work, we conduct a systematic mechanistic analysis of rubric design across criterion organization, criterion operationalization, and score aggregation. Our analysis shows that structural organization enables more comprehensive criterion construction, semantic specification alone does not ensure effective criterion-level judgments, and sophisticated score aggregation provides limited additional gains. Guided by these findings, we introduce Adaptive Rubric Tree Search with Hybrid refinement (ARTS-Hybrid), which combines adaptive tree construction with semantic-first, response-calibrated refinement. Experiments across benchmarks show that ARTS-Hybrid improves both end-to-end evaluation performance and intrinsic rubric quality. Further experiments suggest that, beyond evaluation, rubrics generated by ARTS-Hybrid provide effective signals for model optimization.

评分标准已被广泛用于开放式任务的语言模型评估与优化。然而，现有研究主要聚焦于生成更优的评分标准，对评分设计有效性的内在机制理解有限。本研究对评分标准设计在准则组织、准则操作化和评分聚合三个层面进行了系统的机制分析。分析表明，结构化组织有助于更全面的准则构建，仅靠语义定义并不能确保有效的准则层级判断，而复杂的评分聚合带来的增益有限。基于这些发现，我们提出了自适应评分树搜索与混合优化方法（ARTS-Hybrid），结合自适应树构建与语义优先、响应校准的优化策略。在多个基准测试上的实验表明，ARTS-Hybrid不仅提升了端到端评估性能，也显著改善了评分标准的内在质量。进一步实验表明，除评估外，ARTS-Hybrid生成的评分标准还能为模型优化提供有效信号。

Test-Time Memory Construction
测试时记忆构建
Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to answer questions over long interaction histories, where relevant evidence may be scattered across distant sessions. Existing memory systems typically construct and maintain sophisticated memory representations offline, before user queries are observed, incurring substantial overhead and limiting their ability to adapt memory construction to query-specific information needs. In this paper, we propose T$^2$Mem, a Test-Time Memory construction framework that defers memory construction to inference and progressively builds memory along the reasoning process. Specifically, T$^2$Mem extracts lightweight episode-level metadata offline to capture key events and temporal associations as retrieval cues, while retaining links to the original interactions to facilitate the reconstruction of dependencies across episodes. At test time, the LLM adaptively retrieves and organizes metadata and raw interactions, progressively constructing query-specific memory as reasoning unfolds. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show the effectiveness of T$^2$Mem by consistently outperforming strong baselines across different backbone LLMs while substantially reducing the overhead of memory construction. Further analysis demonstrates that deferring memory construction to inference enables the memory construction process to dynamically adapt to the information needs of individual queries. All code is available at this https URL .

Scaling Agentic Data for Long-Horizon Terminal Intelligence
面向长时程终端智能的智能体数据规模化
Terminal agents are moving beyond local operations toward completing real-world professional tasks. Short trajectories can demonstrate commands and tool calls, but they do not fully show how agents accumulate evidence through exploration, use it to inform judgments, continually adjust strategies based on feedback, and turn intermediate results into final deliverables. High-quality long-horizon trajectories capture these agentic behavioral patterns and their connections within a single problem-solving process, providing richer experience for sustained autonomous work. However, environment synthesis may complete or simplify the key work underlying these patterns in advance and thus remove the need for exploration, implementation, and verification that the agent was meant to perform. To address this, we introduce TerminalHorizon, a fully automated data synthesis engine for long-horizon terminal agents. Starting from real-world professional work, it uses independent execution to identify key work omitted during construction and uses the resulting evidence to guide environment reconstruction. To extend these patterns across stages, it progressively expands tasks under the same goal, introducing new requirements that build on prior results and call for further exploration and improvement. Using this engine, we construct 1.5K task environments and perform supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.5-27B on the resulting dataset to obtain TerminalHorizon-27B. We achieve substantial performance gains on all 12 benchmarks spanning coding-agent tasks, agentic tasks in real-world production settings, and general reasoning. Multiple RSI case studies, each with a 12-hour research budget, further demonstrate the model's ability to continue experimenting beyond an initial solution, improve its methods based on feedback, and retain effective improvements in its final deliverables.

终端智能体正从局部操作转向完成真实世界的专业任务。短轨迹可展示命令与工具调用，但无法充分展现智能体如何通过探索积累证据、利用证据做出判断、根据反馈持续调整策略，并将中间结果转化为最终交付成果。高质量的长时程轨迹捕捉这些智能体行为模式及其在单一问题解决过程中的关联，为持续自主工作提供更丰富的经验。然而，环境合成可能提前完成或简化这些模式背后的关键工作，从而消除智能体本应执行的探索、实施与验证需求。为解决此问题，我们引入TerminalHorizon，一个完全自动化的长时程终端智能体数据合成引擎。它从真实世界专业工作出发，通过独立执行识别构建过程中遗漏的关键工作，并利用所得证据指导环境重构。为在各阶段扩展这些模式，它在相同目标下逐步扩展任务，引入基于前期结果的新要求，推动进一步探索与改进。利用该引擎，我们构建了1500个任务环境，并对Qwen3.5-27B进行监督微调，得到TerminalHorizon-27B模型。该模型在涵盖编码代理任务、真实生产环境中智能体任务及通用推理的12个基准上均取得显著性能提升。多个RSI案例研究（每项12小时研究预算）进一步证明，该模型具备在初始解决方案基础上持续实验、根据反馈改进方法并保留有效改进的能力，最终交付高质量成果。

Evo$^2$: Evolving the Evolver through Weight-Harness Co-Evolution
Evo$^2$：通过权重- harness 共进化实现演进者自身的演化
Self-evolving agents are increasingly used to improve language model systems without human intervention through either weight updates or harness improvements. Existing approaches typically pursue these directions separately: some methods update model weights using self-generated experience, while others rewrite the harness that defines how an agent uses prompts, tools, context, and execution feedback. However, these approaches overlook the relationship between model weights and the agent harness. To connect these processes and ensure effective and smooth agent self-evolving, we propose Evo$^2$, a framework for weight-harness co-evolution. For harness updates, we design a unified dual-role pipeline that a single policy serves as both a Proposer, which writes harness edits, and a Reflector, which diagnoses their effects and guides revision. For weight updates, harness modifications and their execution outcomes guide the training of both the Proposer and the Reflector. We further develop EvoTIDE, a training pipeline that recovers usable trajectories through edit extraction and prefix continuation, then combines SFT with interleaved GRPO across the two roles. Extensive experiments across three coding benchmarks demonstrate that Evo$^2$ significantly outperforms existing self-evolution methods. It achieves 82.1% on Polyglot and 48.3% on SWE-bench Verified, compared with 62.6% and 41.0% for the strongest baseline, respectively. We also show that the evolved harnesses transfer to different tasks without further evolution and rank first in cross-benchmark evaluations. Our dual-role harness improvement framework is also able to extend to self-evolution with proprietary models, improving a GPT-5-mini agent's performance from 24.4% to 48.3%. These results demonstrate the applicability of the framework Evo$^2$ across domains and its broad transferability across models and tasks.
中文翻译
自演化智能体正越来越多地用于无需人工干预即可改进语言模型系统，方式包括权重更新或 harness 改进。现有方法通常分别推进这两个方向：一些方法利用自生成经验更新模型权重，另一些则重写定义智能体如何使用提示、工具、上下文和执行反馈的 harness。然而，这些方法忽略了模型权重与智能体 harness 之间的关系。为连接这两个过程并确保有效且平滑的智能体自演化，我们提出 Evo$^2$，一种权重-harness 共进化框架。对于 harness 更新，我们设计了一个统一的双角色流程：单一策略同时作为提议者（撰写 harness 修改）和反思者（诊断其效果并指导修正）。对于权重更新，harness 修改及其执行结果引导提议者和反思者的训练。我们进一步开发了 EvoTIDE 训练流程，通过编辑提取和前缀延续恢复可用轨迹，然后在两个角色间交替进行 SFT 与 GRPO。在三个编码基准上的大量实验表明，Evo$^2$ 显著优于现有自演化方法。其在 Polyglot 上达到 82.1%，在 SWE-bench Verified 上达到 48.3%，而最强基线分别为 62.6% 和 41.0%。我们还展示了演化后的 harness 可在无进一步演化的情况下迁移至不同任务，并在跨基准评估中排名第一。该双角色 harness 改进框架还可扩展至专有模型的自演化，将 GPT-5-mini 智能体性能从 24.4% 提升至 48.3%。这些结果证明了 Evo$^2$ 框架在多领域中的适用性及其在模型与任务间的广泛可迁移性。

Language Models Have Interpretable Narrative Mechanisms
语言模型具有可解释的叙事机制
We investigate whether language models contain internal components that contribute to representing narrativity, the degree to which a text exhibits narrative properties. Across GPT-2-Small, Gemma-2-2B, and Llama-3.1-8B, we identify attention heads, MLP neurons, and sparse autoencoder features whose activations correlate with narrativity. Ablating selected neuron and feature sets increases next-token prediction loss more for high-narrativity than for low-narrativity texts, and reduces the narrativity of open-ended generations. On downstream benchmarks, the largest effects occur in story completion and narrative question answering, although they vary across models and languages; results on confabulation and hallucination benchmarks are mixed. These findings provide causal evidence that selected sets of narrative-associated components contribute to model behaviour, while their internal organisation, relation to narrative theory, and broader functions remain open questions.

Surfing the Activation Wave: Analyzing the Information and Geometry of LLM Hidden States for Speculative Decoding
乘着激活波浪：分析大语言模型隐藏状态的信息与几何结构以实现推测解码
Decoder hidden states from different layers of large language models (LLMs) often encode complementary information. However, existing speculative decoding methods typically rely on a small set of target-model layers chosen using fixed, position-based heuristics. Selecting an optimal subset of layers is inherently a combinatorial problem, making exhaustive evaluation infeasible even for moderately deep models. In this work, we present a principled framework for multi-layer hidden-state selection for speculative decoding based on two complementary criteria: informativeness, measured through matrix-entropy analysis, and diversity, quantified using centered kernel alignment (CKA). We investigate layer-wise Shannon entropy, aggregate entropy under hidden-state concatenation, and pairwise CKA dynamics across depth, revealing distinct geometric and informational compartments within decoder representations. Motivated by these findings, we propose entropy and CKA-based layer selection algorithms that favor hidden-state sets that are both highly informative and minimally redundant. As part of this framework, we introduce token-entropy filtering, which removes hidden states associated with low predictive-entropy tokens, thereby improving the quality of layer-selection statistics. In EAGLE3-style speculative decoding, our selected sets outperform fixed baseline choices, including the default $[2, N//2, N-3]$ rule, while providing a clearer explanation of why some defaults can remain competitive. Our results provide a practical recipe for multi-layer hidden-state selection for speculative decoding, insights into the geometry and information in LLM decoder states, and a foundation for extending similar analysis to related applications.

大型语言模型（LLM）不同层的解码器隐藏状态通常编码互补信息。然而，现有的推测解码方法通常依赖于固定位置启发式选择的目标模型层数。选择最优层数子集本质上是一个组合问题，即使对于中等深度的模型也难以穷尽评估。本文提出一种基于两个互补标准的多层隐藏状态选择原则性框架：信息量（通过矩阵熵分析衡量）和多样性（使用中心核对齐CKA量化）。我们研究了逐层香农熵、隐藏状态拼接后的总熵以及跨深度的成对CKA动态，揭示了解码器表示中独特的几何与信息分隔区。受此发现启发，我们提出了基于熵和CKA的层选择算法，优先选择既高度信息丰富又冗余最小的隐藏状态集合。作为该框架的一部分，我们引入了词元熵过滤，用于移除与低预测熵词元相关的隐藏状态，从而提升层选择统计的质量。在EAGLE3风格的推测解码中，我们的选择优于固定的基线策略（包括默认的$[2, N//2, N-3]$规则），同时为某些默认策略为何仍具竞争力提供了清晰解释。结果为推测解码中的多层隐藏状态选择提供了实用方案，深化了对LLM解码器状态中几何与信息结构的理解，并为类似分析扩展至相关应用奠定了基础。

SEEDS: Preview-Guided Evidence Admission for Search Agents
SEEDS：搜索代理的预览引导证据接纳机制
Search agents extend large reasoning models with multi-round access to external knowledge, yet most existing systems largely conflate retrieving evidence with consuming it. Retrieved documents are commonly incorporated according to fixed or rank-based policies, despite the fact that evidence utility is conditioned on the agent's current reasoning state. Consequently, a lower-ranked candidate may be critical for the current step, while a higher-ranked candidate may provide redundant, irrelevant, or misleading information. We formulate this overlooked problem as evidence admission, which asks, at each search step, which retrieved candidates should be allowed to enter the agent's evolving reasoning context. To enable such selective control, we introduce SEEDS (Selective Evidence Entry During Search), a preview-guided evidence admission framework for search agents. SEEDS first exposes lightweight previews of retrieved candidates and then lets the agent jointly determine which candidates and how many should be fully inspected, conditioned on its current reasoning state. Unlike fixed or adaptive prefix policies, SEEDS can select arbitrary subsets with flexible cardinality, and it can be instantiated directly with an existing reasoning agent or further optimized through learning. Across nine knowledge-intensive QA benchmarks under both local corpus and live web search, SEEDS consistently improves search effectiveness while substantially reducing full-evidence consumption. Experimental analyses show that SEEDS more effectively selects useful candidates beyond the corresponding retrieval prefix. These results establish evidence admission as a complementary dimension of agentic search, separating what information is retrieved from what information is ultimately allowed to shape reasoning.
中文翻译
搜索代理通过多轮访问外部知识扩展了大型推理模型的能力，但现有大多数系统在检索证据与使用证据之间缺乏区分。检索到的文档通常依据固定或排名策略被纳入，尽管证据效用实际上取决于代理当前的推理状态。因此，较低排名的候选可能对当前步骤至关重要，而较高排名的候选可能提供冗余、无关或误导性信息。我们将这一被忽视的问题定义为证据接纳，即在每一轮搜索中，应允许哪些检索到的候选进入代理不断演化的推理上下文。为实现这种选择性控制，我们提出SEEDS（搜索过程中的选择性证据进入），一种预览引导的证据接纳框架。SEEDS首先展示检索候选的轻量级预览，然后让代理根据其当前推理状态共同决定应完全检查哪些候选及其数量。与固定或自适应前缀策略不同，SEEDS可选择任意子集并灵活调整数量，且可直接应用于现有推理代理，也可通过学习进一步优化。在九个知识密集型问答基准测试中，无论是在本地语料库还是实时网络搜索场景下，SEEDS均持续提升搜索有效性，同时大幅减少完整证据消耗。实验分析表明，SEEDS能更有效地选出超出对应检索前缀范围的有用候选。这些结果确立了证据接纳作为智能体搜索的一个补充维度，将“检索什么”与“最终允许塑造推理的内容”分离开来。

Temporal Evidence Forests for Long-Horizon Multi-Party Conversational Memory
面向长时程多参与者对话记忆的时序证据森林
Long-term memory has been widely adopted in LLM-based agents to support persistent interaction and personalized assistance. Long-term multi-party interactions, however, accumulate substantially more detailed information, with decisions repeatedly updated and states evolving across participants and interaction stages. Temporal structure is therefore crucial for tracing such memories: relevant information often appears densely within short intervals, while transitions among different states are anchored by a strict temporal order. Existing temporally aware memory frameworks primarily store time as metadata or use temporal signals to guide retrieval over preconstructed memory structures, without using temporal structure to both recover locally concentrated evidence and reconstruct its complete event progression. To address these limitations, we propose T-Mem, a temporal-structure-based memory framework that preserves interactions and organizes query-relevant evidence through two temporal mechanisms. Temporal Evidence Discovery exploits the local concentration of related messages to recover overlooked evidence, while Temporal Memory Structuring models state transitions and supporting relations over time and compresses low-information spans within the resulting forest. Evidence Forest Linearization then serializes the structured evidence for answer generation. Experiments on two long-term multi-party memory benchmarks demonstrate that T-Mem consistently outperforms competitive memory frameworks across LLMs, parameter settings, domains, and query types.

Time Delay System for Sequence Modeling
用于序列建模的时延系统
Existing sequence models share a critical limitation: they lack an explicit, interpretable mechanism for content-aware temporal recall. LSTMs compress everything into a single hidden state, thereby losing information about specific past events. Transformers attend to all tokens indiscriminately at quadratic cost, while state-space models like Mamba compress history into a fixed-dimensional state without explicit content-addressable memory. To address this gap, we introduce a novel framework grounded in nonlinear time-delay systems, in which the effective delay is dynamically estimated based on the semantic similarity between current and historical inputs. Our approach features three key innovations: (i) a differentiable semantic lag estimator that computes content-aware soft attention weights over history, producing a weighted historical context and similarity score; (ii) a diagonal state space with closed-form cumulative sum solution, achieving O(1) per-step complexity with no sequential loops; and (iii) an interpretable fuzzy semantic modulation mechanism that learns context-dependent rules for balancing instantaneous processing versus historical recall, where rule centers correspond to prototypical semantic contexts. Experimental results on text classification and generation outperform state-of-the-art methods.

DistressBench: Evaluating Multi-Dimensional Crisis Support in Large Language Models
DistressBench：评估大语言模型在多维度危机支持中的表现
Language models have been adopted faster than evaluation practices that reliably assess their risks, and a growing share of use is affective rather than informational. Within that traffic users disclose suicide and self-harm (SSH) crises. Existing safety benchmarks score models primarily on refusal, but refusal is not the desired behavior here; the conversation requires a substantive supportive response. We introduce DistressBench, 718 English conversations with clinician-authored user turns spanning 23 SSH subcategories, each paired with a task-specific rubric of weighted atomic criteria across seven dimensions of crisis support and a clinician reference response. A model's score is the weighted average over the criteria its reply satisfies, reading directly as the fraction of required care delivered. The best of 25 frontier models satisfies 88.4% of weighted criteria and the median model 71.4%, against 98.7% for clinician references scored on the same criteria. Pooled across all 25 models, in 35.3% of conversations where a model satisfied every criterion for recognizing the crisis, it failed to route the user toward human help. The shortfall concentrates in the dimensions requiring the model to act rather than withhold. Contrasting each dimension's median model against its best separates capability limits from choices: de-escalation reaches 74.6% in the strongest model against a median of 46.0%, and safety disclaimers 84.6% against 8.3%. Multi-turn conversations score below single-turn ones (68.5% against 74.2%). We release the corpus, rubrics, judge calibration labels, and a companion annex of 20 long-horizon red-team transcripts. These results argue for evaluating crisis safety on the care a model delivers rather than on what it declines to say, and for treating de-escalation and resource routing as deployment requirements.

语言模型的应用速度远超可靠评估实践的发展，且越来越多的应用场景聚焦于情感而非信息。在这些应用中，用户会披露自杀与自残（SSH）危机。现有安全基准主要评估模型的拒绝能力，但在此类情境下，拒绝并非期望行为；对话需要实质性的支持回应。我们提出DistressBench，包含718段英文对话，每段均由临床医生撰写用户发言，涵盖23个SSH子类别，每条对话均配有特定任务的评分量表，涵盖危机支持的七个维度的加权原子标准，并附有临床医生参考回应。模型得分是其回应满足的标准加权平均值，直接反映所提供关怀的比例。25个前沿模型中表现最佳者满足88.4%的加权标准，中位数为71.4%，而临床医生参考回应在相同标准下得分为98.7%。综合所有25个模型，在35.3%的对话中，尽管模型已满足识别危机的所有标准，却未能引导用户寻求人工帮助。这一差距集中在需要模型采取行动而非克制的维度。通过对比各维度的最佳模型与中位模型，可区分能力限制与选择偏差：去激化能力在最强模型中达74.6%，中位数仅为46.0%；安全免责声明则分别为84.6%和8.3%。多轮对话得分低于单轮对话（68.5% vs. 74.2%）。我们公开发布语料库、评分量表、裁判校准标签以及20个长周期红队对话的附录。这些结果主张应基于模型提供的关怀质量而非其拒绝表达的内容来评估危机安全性，并将去激化和资源转介视为部署必需要求。

Self-Preservation in Language Models
语言模型中的自我保存机制
Continual learning (CL) seeks to adapt pretrained models while preserving prior capabilities. Yet the tasks supplied for preservation cover only part of what a model knows, while forgetting can extend beyond them. Recent work shows that language models can supplement this limited evidence by generating useful replay without access to their original training data. This raises a question about self-preservation: can a model help choose preservation targets that protect its own capabilities more broadly? To study this question, we organize task descriptions and their examples into Task Banks, whose entries can be selected and combined to guide replay generation by a frozen model. Continual instruction tuning with Qwen models of varying sizes yields three findings. First, the new task and learning rate shape which prior capabilities degrade, while tracking previously correct answers reveals losses that average accuracy can conceal. Second, generated replay reduces forgetting on tasks outside the bank. Combining partial banks strengthens this protection, improving mean retention of previously correct answers over either bank alone across the tested sizes. Third, the model can also help choose replay targets: selection informed by the new task improves mean held-out retention over random selection at 4B, with similar new-task gains, although smaller models show no corresponding advantage. Under a fixed example budget, however, replacing new-task examples with replay improves preservation at the cost of smaller new-task gains. Together, these findings show that models can support broader self-preservation by generating replay data and, in some settings, choosing which tasks to replay.
中文翻译
持续学习（CL）旨在适应预训练模型的同时保留其先前的能力。然而，用于保存的任务仅覆盖了模型知识的一部分，而遗忘可能超出这些范围。近期研究表明，语言模型可以在无法访问原始训练数据的情况下，通过生成有用的回放数据来补充这种有限的证据。这引出了关于自我保存的问题：模型能否帮助选择能更广泛保护自身能力的保存目标？为研究这一问题，我们将任务描述及其示例组织成任务库，其条目可被选择和组合，以指导冻结模型生成回放。对不同规模的Qwen模型进行持续指令微调得出三个发现：第一，新任务和学习率的设置会影响哪些先前能力退化，而追踪之前正确答案可揭示平均准确率所掩盖的损失；第二，生成的回放数据减少了库外任务的遗忘现象。部分任务库的组合增强了这种保护效果，在测试的所有模型规模下，相比单独使用任一库，均提升了先前正确答案的平均保留率；第三，模型还能协助选择回放目标：基于新任务的筛选在4B模型上优于随机选择，带来相似的新任务增益，但较小模型未表现出相应优势。在固定示例预算下，用回放替换新任务示例虽会降低新任务增益，但有助于提升保存效果。综合来看，这些发现表明，模型可通过生成回放数据，在某些场景下自主选择回放任务，从而支持更广泛的自我保存。

Reflective Prompt Optimization of a Deep-Research Agent: A Topic-Disjoint, Multi-Seed Protocol for Measuring Transferable Gains and Prompt-Level Overfitting
深度研究智能体的反思式提示优化：一种主题不重叠、多种子协议以衡量可迁移收益与提示级过拟合
Prompt optimization is an attractive alternative to reinforcement learning for adapting language-model agents, since it is cheap and produces human-readable artifacts. On factual tasks, however, it carries a failure mode that scalar-reward reinforcement learning does not. An optimizer that reads the training examples can write their answers into the prompt, so an in-distribution gain may be memorization rather than transferable behaviour. We apply reflective prompt evolution (GEPA) to the two system prompts of a multi-agent deep-research agent and evaluate it under a topic-disjoint split in which no topic is shared across train, validation, and test. Held-out citation reward rises by 0.079, from 0.702 to 0.781, with a bootstrap 95\% confidence interval of [0.051, 0.107], and the gain replicates across three seeds (mean 0.107). Nearly half of the winning prompt is a memorized answer key, yet a controlled ablation shows this content earns no reward even on the exact training questions it encodes. The large validation-minus-held-out gap is therefore small-sample selection under a noisy judge, not memorization. Varying the verifier isolates what makes the gain transfer. Decomposed per-dimension feedback is the causal ingredient, and collapsing it to a single scalar reward makes held-out transfer negative. The gain persists under an out-of-family judge and is largely reproduced by a hand-written version of the discovered recipe. Credible evaluation of prompt optimizers therefore requires held-out topics, multiple seeds, and verifier ablations. We release the candidate prompts, lineage diffs, figures, tables, and analysis.
中文翻译
提示优化是替代强化学习来适应语言模型智能体的一种有吸引力的方法，因其成本低且产生人类可读的输出。然而，在事实性任务中，它存在一种强化学习不会出现的失败模式：优化器读取训练样例后，可将答案写入提示中，导致分布内性能提升可能是记忆而非可迁移行为。我们对多智能体深度研究智能体的两个系统提示应用了反思式提示演化（GEPA），并在主题不重叠的划分下进行评估，即训练、验证和测试集之间无共享主题。保留样本的引用奖励从0.702提升至0.781，增幅为0.079，Bootstrap 95%置信区间为[0.051, 0.107]，且该增益在三个种子下均可复现（均值0.107）。近半数获胜提示是记忆化的答案键，但受控消融实验显示，即使在精确编码训练问题上，这些内容也未获得任何奖励。因此，较大的验证集与保留集差距源于噪声裁判下的小样本选择，而非记忆。通过改变验证器可识别出使增益可迁移的关键因素：分解后的维度反馈是因果成分，若将其压缩为单一标量奖励，则保留集上的迁移性能反而变为负值。该增益在家族外验证器下仍持续存在，且人工编写的发现配方基本重现了该效果。因此，可信的提示优化器评估需要保留主题、多个种子及验证器消融。我们已公开候选提示、谱系差异、图表、表格和分析结果。
👤 Elias Lumer, Sahil Sen, Vamse Kumar Subbiah, Kevin Paul 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv

One Model to Loop Them All: Elastic Latent Recurrence for Language Models
一个模型通吃所有：面向语言模型的弹性潜在循环机制
A latent recurrent language model applies a shared block of layers repeatedly, so one checkpoint can trade inference compute for accuracy by changing the loop count. At one loop, however, such models typically fall well short of a non-recurrent model of the same size, a one-loop tax that makes a separate non-recurrent model the better choice whenever latency matters. We present Elastic Latent Recurrence (ELR), a recipe for training looped language models without this tax. At one loop, an ELR model costs the same to serve as a non-recurrent model of the same size and, trained on the same data, reaches similar accuracy. Each additional loop then buys more accuracy for more inference compute. Three choices make this work: a gated state update with a learned step size, loop counts mixed within each optimizer update, and conditioning each pass on its loop index and budget. The first two preserve one-loop accuracy, and the third lowers loss at every additional loop. Mixed depths can be introduced late in pretraining to limit the added training cost, and with zero-initialized conditioning added at the switch, this late schedule reaches lower loss than mixed-depth training from scratch. In MoE models, reusing first-loop expert routes on later loops cuts mixed-depth update time by 11.7% with no measurable change in accuracy. At inference, keeping only the last loop's attention KV cache holds attention KV memory at its one-loop level while retaining most of the gain from additional loops. On 4B dense and 30B-A3B MoE hybrid models, ELR at one loop is 0.52 points below and 1.04 points above its matched baseline on a ten-task average, and four loops add 7.25 and 4.75 points. Carried through post-training, the recipe yields Koi-4B, a single checkpoint that scores an estimated 18 on the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index at one loop, matching Qwen3.5-4B, and 29 at four loops.
中文翻译
潜在循环语言模型通过重复使用一组共享层实现，使得同一检查点可通过调整循环次数在推理计算与精度之间权衡。然而，在单次循环时，这类模型通常远落后于同规模的非循环模型，这种“单循环代价”使得在延迟敏感场景下，独立的非循环模型仍是更优选择。我们提出弹性潜在循环（ELR）机制，可在无需此代价的前提下训练循环语言模型。在单次循环时，ELR模型的服务成本与同规模非循环模型相当，且在相同数据上训练后达到相近精度。每次额外循环则以增加推理计算为代价换取更高精度。该机制的成功依赖于三个关键设计：带有学习步长的门控状态更新、在每个优化器更新中混合循环次数，以及根据循环索引和预算对每轮进行条件控制。前两项确保单循环精度，第三项在每次额外循环中降低损失。可在预训练后期引入混合深度以限制额外训练成本，且在切换时加入零初始化条件后，该延迟调度能达到比从头开始混合深度训练更低的损失。在MoE模型中，复用首轮专家路径于后续循环，使混合深度更新时间减少11.7%，且精度无明显变化。推理阶段，仅保留最后一次循环的注意力KV缓存，即可将注意力KV内存维持在单循环水平，同时保留大部分额外循环带来的增益。在4B稠密模型和30B-A3B MoE混合模型上，ELR在单循环时分别比匹配基线低0.52分和高1.04分，在十项任务平均上表现优异；四次循环分别带来7.25分和4.75分的提升。经后训练扩展，该方法生成Koi-4B模型，单循环时估计得分18，与Qwen3.5-4B相当，四次循环时得分为29。

AutoIndexer: Flexible-Order Decoding by Training Causal Models on Chains of Edits
AutoIndexer：通过训练因果模型学习编辑链实现灵活顺序解码
Autoregressive decoding is less suited to non-causally ordered data, e.g., cross-references or future dependencies, since their outputs cannot be revised post hoc, unlike iterative human reasoning. Diffusion LLMs partially mitigate this limitation but still cannot flexibly insert or remove arbitrary numbers of tokens from a pre-generated context. We design AutoIndexer as a generative model with a novel training and decoding mechanism that simulates a Chain of Edits, allowing the model to flexibly modify its prior outputs in hindsight via token insertions, substitutions, and deletions. The model learns to predict marker tokens for opening and closing edits, simultaneously moving a cursor to positions in the existing token sequence, analogous to a text editor. Training sequences are obtained by perturbing ground-truth labels with inverse chained edit operations, and the model learns to edit by undoing those inverses. The perturbation training allows AutoIndexer to be robust to erroneous context and adaptable to editing tasks such as code correction. We demonstrate that our method exhibits post-generation context-editing capabilities that most autoregressive and diffusion models lack without compromising performance on standard LLM benchmarks. The code is available at this https URL .

自回归解码不适用于非因果顺序的数据（如交叉引用或未来依赖），因为其输出无法事后修正，而人类推理则可通过迭代调整。扩散型大语言模型部分缓解了这一限制，但仍无法灵活地在预生成上下文中插入或删除任意数量的标记。我们设计了AutoIndexer，一种具有新颖训练与解码机制的生成模型，模拟“编辑链”过程，使模型能够事后通过插入、替换和删除标记来灵活修改先前输出。模型学习预测用于开启和关闭编辑的标记，并同时将光标移动至已有标记序列中的位置，类似于文本编辑器的操作。训练序列通过逆向链式编辑操作扰动真实标签获得，模型通过反向执行这些操作来学习编辑能力。这种扰动训练使AutoIndexer对错误上下文具有鲁棒性，并可适应代码修正等编辑任务。我们证明，该方法在不牺牲标准大语言模型基准性能的前提下，展现出大多数自回归和扩散模型所不具备的生成后上下文编辑能力。代码已公开。

OccludeBench: Can VLMs Find What's Missing?
OccludeBench：视觉语言模型能否发现缺失的内容？
How well can vision-language models (VLMs) reason about what is missing from an image? We study this question with OccludeBench, a manually curated benchmark for recovering fully occluded objects from the visual evidence that remains. OccludeBench distinguishes direct cases, where the hidden object must be recovered from a specific physical cue such as a shadow or reflection, from inferential cases, where it can be inferred from broader scene context. Across five VLMs, accuracy averages 42.7% on direct cases versus 62.7% on inferential cases, exhibiting a 20.0-point gap. Humans attain higher accuracies of 63.4% versus 68.5% for direct and inferential images, respectively, with no significant gap. To locate this failure, we introduce OccludeTrace, a four-stage diagnostic that isolates where the visual reasoning process breaks down. The bottleneck emerges when models must recover the relevant object from the projection within the full image: they succeed on only 50% of failed examples, compared with 91% of examples they answer correctly. Extended reasoning, tool use, and few-shot prompting yield inconsistent improvements and do not reliably eliminate the gap. In contrast, an inpaint-then-confront intervention, which prompts models to first generate a candidate hidden object and then evaluate that hypothesis against the visible physical cue, reduces the gap to a statistically non-significant difference for both models with image-generation capabilities. Together, our results establish a tractable, controlled diagnostic for isolating how VLMs reason about visual content that is missing from an image.
中文翻译
视觉语言模型（VLMs）在图像中缺失内容的推理能力如何？我们通过OccludeBench——一个手动构建的基准测试，研究从剩余视觉证据中恢复完全遮挡物体的能力。OccludeBench区分直接案例（需从特定物理线索如阴影或反射中恢复隐藏物体）与推断案例（需从更广泛的场景上下文中推断）。在五种VLM中，直接案例准确率为42.7%，推断案例为62.7%，差距达20.0个百分点。人类在直接和推断案例中的准确率分别为63.4%和68.5%，无显著差距。为定位失败原因，我们引入OccludeTrace，一种四阶段诊断框架，识别视觉推理过程的断裂点。瓶颈出现在模型必须从完整图像投影中恢复相关对象时：在失败样本中仅50%成功，而在正确回答的样本中则达91%。扩展推理、工具使用和少量示例提示带来的改进不一致，无法可靠消除差距。相比之下，一种‘先修复再验证’干预策略（即先生成候选隐藏对象，再将其与可见物理线索对比验证）使具备图像生成能力的模型在两类任务间的差距降至统计上不显著水平。我们的结果建立了一个可控、可操作的诊断框架，用于揭示VLM如何推理图像中缺失的视觉内容。

UkrMeme: Multimodal Explainable Persuasion Detection in Ukrainian Memes
UkrMeme：乌克兰梗图中的多模态可解释说服检测
Memes play a central role in modern online communication, especially in contexts of political conflict where visual and textual cues combine to convey persuasive messages. We study persuasive technique detection in Ukrainian memes using vision-language models (VLMs). We introduce UkrMeme, a dataset of 200 organically produced Ukrainian wartime memes annotated for persuasion techniques, and PTM-UA, a Ukrainian translation of a subset of the Propaganda 950 benchmark that preserves original visual content while translating meme text. We evaluate VLMs in a zero-shot prompting setup across image, text, and multimodal inputs using hierarchical F1 metrics. A Privileged Teacher That Only Scores Cannot Search: Critique-Conditioned On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning
仅评分无法搜索的特权教师：基于批评条件的策略自蒸馏用于推理
On-policy self-distillation promises to replace the sparse outcome signal of verifiable-reward RL with dense token-level credit: a teacher conditioned on the reference solution scores the student's own rollout, and the resulting distributional shift is distilled back into the policy. We investigate whether this scoring signal actually contains corrective credit. Using gold error spans in student rollouts, we test the two signals consumed by self-distillation objectives, the full-distribution KL and the token log-ratio, for two properties: whether they localize errors and whether they push probability away from erroneous student tokens. They exhibit neither of these properties nor can they reliably distinguish correct from incorrect rollouts within the same problem. We argue that this failure is structural: locating an error in a reasoning trace is itself a search problem, while a scoring teacher is never explicitly asked to perform that search. The same model, with the same privileged information, localizes errors substantially better when allowed to generate a critique. This forms the motivation for \emph{critique-conditioned self-distillation}: first generate a critique of the current rollout, then condition the scoring teacher on that critique. The resulting signal localizes errors, is corrective within error spans, and shifts probability toward tokens that initiate backtracking such as ``Wait'', ``But'', and ``Actually''. We further show that the critique is useful both at test time, where resuming a wrong rollout with the critique in context converts math problems the model cannot solve by sampling, and in cold-start GRPO, where it provides an advantage signal on prompts for which verifiable rewards alone provide none.

策略自蒸馏有望用密集的token级信用信号替代可验证奖励强化学习中的稀疏结果信号：一个基于参考解的教师对学生的自身回放进行评分，由此产生的分布偏移被反向蒸馏回策略中。我们探究该评分信号是否真正包含修正性信用。通过使用学生回放中的真实错误片段，我们检验自蒸馏目标所依赖的两个信号——全分布KL散度与token对数比——是否具备两个特性：能否定位错误，以及能否将概率从错误的学生token上推开。结果表明，这两个信号均不具备上述特性，也无法在相同问题内可靠区分正确与错误的回放。我们认为这种失败具有结构性：在推理轨迹中定位错误本身就是一个搜索问题，而评分教师从未被明确要求执行该搜索。同一模型在拥有相同特权信息的情况下，若允许其生成批评，则能显著更好地定位错误。这促使我们提出‘批评条件自蒸馏’：首先生成当前回放的批评，然后将评分教师条件化于该批评之上。由此产生的信号能够准确定位错误，在错误区间内具有修正作用，并推动概率向引发回溯的token（如“Wait”、“But”、“Actually”）转移。我们进一步证明，该批评在测试时也有效——在上下文中加入批评后，模型可重新启动错误回放并解决原本无法通过采样求解的数学问题；在冷启动GRPO中，它为仅靠可验证奖励无法提供优势信号的提示提供了优势信号。

APEX-Harm: Evaluating Agent Safety in Shared Professional Workspaces
APEX-Harm：评估共享专业工作空间中的代理安全
Professional agents increasingly work through command-line interfaces and multiple tools over shared workspaces containing files, messages, and business applications. These environments create safety risks that are difficult to capture with attacks placed at a single, known read location. We introduce APEX-Harm, a benchmark for evaluating prompt and script injection against long-horizon agents performing finance, consulting, and legal tasks in large workspaces. Each task contains 115 to 328 files, together with email, calendar, chat, document, spreadsheet, PDF, and code-execution tools exposed through MCP servers. The benchmark includes six attack categories covering static and dynamic injections, task-specific script replacement, and attacker-provided generic scripts, and evaluates attack success, exposure, and task performance. We evaluate 12 frontier models on 180 task instances per model. Opus 5 and Sonnet 5 have the lowest overall attack success rates (8% and 20%), while task-specific script replacement and attacker-provided generic script attacks remain substantially more effective, averaging 81-84% and 73% attack success, respectively. Gemini 3.8 Flash achieves the highest mean task score (0.877) while still exhibiting a 33% attack success rate. These results show that completing the requested task is not sufficient evidence of safe behavior: an agent can produce a correct deliverable while also performing an unauthorized action in the workspace.

Not All Correct Fixes Run Alike: An Empirical Study of Patch Performance in Repository-Level Issue Resolution
并非所有正确的修复都表现相同：关于仓库级问题修复中补丁性能的实证研究
Repository-level issue-resolution benchmarks such as SWE-bench evaluate submitted patches primarily based on whether they pass the correctness tests, leaving the runtime performance of different test-passing patches largely unexplored. Evaluating patch performance is essential but challenging since designing performance workloads for each benchmark instance is costly. However, these benchmarks already provide a natural candidate: the built-in \emph{FAIL\_TO\_PASS} (F2P) tests, which exercise the behavior affected by the issue fix. We therefore investigate whether the provided F2P tests can be repurposed to evaluate patch performance. Overall, we choose four frontier models, Claude Opus~5, GPT~5.6, DeepSeek V4 Pro, and Qwen~3.8 Max, paired with SWE-agent to resolve the pre-filtered measurable corpus from verified splits of SWE-bench and SWE-bench-Live, yielding 262 cases with at least two different correct solutions, including the developer ground-truth patch. We then measure each solution's runtime performance in instruction count and wall-clock time on the native F2P test, and use Welch ANOVA instead of the RSD proposed in prior work as a more robust statistical discrimination criterion. We find that this methodology exhibits notable discrimination capability in runtime performance and identifies performance differences among patches in almost half of the instances. Our empirical study shows that the runtime performance of generated patches is independent of the issue resolution rate of LLMs, with nearly reversed model rankings. The case study also shows that the performance analysis reveals architectural regression in some patches that the correctness evaluation overlooked. These findings suggest that resolution rate does not adequately characterize LLMs' capability for repository-level issue resolution, motivating future evaluations to consider patch performance alongside it.

如SWE-bench等仓库级问题修复基准主要依据提交补丁是否通过正确性测试来评估，而对通过测试的不同补丁的运行时性能则很少探索。评估补丁性能至关重要但具有挑战性，因为为每个基准实例设计性能工作负载成本高昂。然而，这些基准已提供一个自然候选：内置的 extit{FAIL_TO_PASS}（F2P）测试，它们会触发受问题修复影响的行为。因此，我们研究是否可以重新利用这些F2P测试来评估补丁性能。总体而言，我们选取四个前沿模型——Claude Opus 5、GPT 5.6、DeepSeek V4 Pro和Qwen 3.8 Max——与SWE-agent结合，解决从SWE-bench和SWE-bench-Live验证分割中预筛选出的可度量语料库，共获得262个至少存在两种不同正确解决方案的案例（包括开发者真实补丁）。随后，我们在原生F2P测试上测量每个解决方案的指令计数和墙钟时间，并采用Welch ANOVA而非先前工作中提出的RSD作为更稳健的统计区分标准。我们发现该方法在运行时性能方面表现出显著区分能力，几乎在一半实例中识别出补丁间的性能差异。我们的实证研究表明，生成补丁的运行时性能与大模型的问题解决率无关，甚至出现模型排名近乎反转的情况。案例研究还显示，性能分析揭示了一些被正确性评估忽略的架构退化问题。这些发现表明，仅以解决率来刻画大模型在仓库级问题修复中的能力是不充分的，未来评估应同时考虑补丁性能。

S-Space: Exploring Spatial Workspace in Multimodal Language Models
S-Space：探索多模态语言模型中的空间工作区
Multimodal language models (MLLMs) exhibit promising spatial reasoning capabilities, yet the internal mechanisms supporting them remain poorly understood. We uncover S-Space, a linear representation subspace in which object-token representations encode continuous coordinates along horizontal, vertical, and distance axes. Although we identify these axes by differentiating spatial-answer scores with respect to object-token activations, S-Space is intrinsic rather than induced by specific data or prompts, remaining readable under non-spatial questions. We show that S-Space functions as an internal spatial workspace of MLLMs. Perception encodes object locations into it, forming spatial representations that can be directly read out to support accurate spatial judgments. Internal reasoning transforms it for changing viewpoints. Verbal responses selectively access it, evidenced by causal interventions that alter spatial judgments while largely preserving non-spatial responses. Case studies further show that S-Space assigns locations to unseen entities and the observer, and connects physical directions with abstract linguistic meanings, suggesting both the capabilities and limitations of large-scale language training. Finally, we use S-Space as a diagnostic tool for spatial reasoning failures. Direction-word readouts reveal systematic coupling between cardinal and image-relative reference frames. More importantly, a perception-computation decomposition exposes a key bottleneck: MLLMs can form capable spatial representations, yet fall short in the spatial transformations required to reason reliably across viewpoint changes.

多模态语言模型（MLLMs）展现出令人瞩目的空间推理能力，但其内部支持机制仍不清楚。我们发现了S-Space，一种线性表示子空间，其中对象标记的表示编码了水平、垂直和距离轴上的连续坐标。尽管我们通过区分空间回答分数与对象标记激活的关系来识别这些轴，但S-Space是内在的而非由特定数据或提示诱导的，在非空间问题下依然可读。我们证明S-Space是MLLMs的内部空间工作区。感知将对象位置编码到其中，形成可直接读出的空间表示，以支持准确的空间判断。内部推理会对其变换以适应视角变化。语言响应选择性地访问它，这通过因果干预得以证实：干预可改变空间判断，同时基本保持非空间响应不变。案例研究进一步表明，S-Space能为未见实体和观察者分配位置，并将物理方向与抽象语言意义相连接，揭示大规模语言训练的能力与局限。最后，我们使用S-Space作为诊断工具分析空间推理失败。方向词读出揭示了方位参考系与图像相对参考系之间的系统性耦合。更重要的是，感知-计算分解暴露出一个关键瓶颈：MLLMs能够形成强大的空间表示，但在应对视角变化所需的空间变换方面仍显不足。

Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Language Model with Heterogeneous Evidence Integration for Clinical Prognosis
基于异构证据融合的检索增强型多模态语言模型用于临床预后预测
Clinical prognosis requires integrating structured measurements, physiological waveforms, longitudinal history, and population context. We introduce PRISM (Population-Retrieved Inference for Structured Multimodal Prognosis), a multimodal language model that combines per-feature numerical tokenization, recency-weighted aggregation of longitudinal ECGs, and a dual-modality population memory bank. Retrieval operates in representation space: the model receives mean-pooled embeddings of similar training-cohort encounters, while no individual retrieved record enters the prompt. On the 1,443-task MDS-ED benchmark, PRISM achieves AUROCs of 86.2% for diagnosis, 93.1% for deterioration, 91.8% for ICU admission, and 92.8% for mortality. These results use a 96-hour pre-arrival window unavailable to reported baselines. With benchmark-window inputs only, PRISM reaches 0.8895 AUROC, compared with 0.8937 for UniPACT and 0.8889 for MDS-ED. A matched-input compact fusion model using the same five evidence streams and frozen encoders reaches 0.7652 AUROC, versus 0.9092 for PRISM. Replacing numerical EHR encoding with text serialization reduces AUROC by 3.66 points; removing pre-arrival history reduces it by 1.97 points; and zeroing retrieval reduces it by 0.99 points. Genuine retrieval outperforms random-neighbor retrieval by 0.97 macro-average points, although a paired task-level sign test does not establish a uniform advantage (p=0.067). On the external MC-MED cohort, supervised fine-tuning reaches 0.8611 AUROC, compared with 0.8227 for locally trained gradient boosting and 0.7841 for source-only PRISM. These results support structure-aware multimodal integration and supervised external adaptation.
中文翻译
临床预后需要整合结构化测量、生理波形、纵向病史和人群背景。我们提出了PRISM（面向结构化多模态预后的群体检索推理），一种结合特征数值标记化、基于时间权重聚合的纵向心电图（ECG）以及双模态群体记忆库的多模态语言模型。检索在表示空间中进行：模型接收类似训练队列经历的均值池化嵌入，而不会将个别检索记录引入提示。在包含1,443个任务的MDS-ED基准上，PRISM在诊断、恶化、ICU入院和死亡预测上的AUROC分别达到86.2%、93.1%、91.8%和92.8%。这些结果使用了报告基线无法获取的96小时到达前窗口。仅使用基准窗口输入时，PRISM达到0.8895 AUROC，优于UniPACT的0.8937和MDS-ED的0.8889。使用相同五条证据流和冻结编码器的匹配输入紧凑融合模型仅达0.7652 AUROC，远低于PRISM的0.9092。将数值EHR编码替换为文本序列化会使AUROC下降3.66点；移除到达前病史使下降1.97点；禁用检索使下降0.99点。真实检索相比随机邻近检索提升0.97个宏平均点，尽管任务层面配对符号检验未确立统一优势（p=0.067）。在外部MC-MED队列上，监督微调达到0.8611 AUROC，优于本地训练梯度提升的0.8227和仅源PRISM的0.7841。这些结果支持结构感知的多模态集成和监督的外部适应。

BoundaryEvo: Data-Model Co-Evolution at the Capability Boundary for Long-Chain Reasoning Distillation
边界演化：在能力边界处实现数据-模型协同进化以提炼长链推理
Distilling long-chain reasoning trajectories from large teacher models provides an effective way to improve the reasoning ability of small language models. However, existing methods typically select a fixed training set before distillation, overlooking that the usefulness of a reasoning example changes as the student evolves. This mismatch becomes especially costly when only a small number of teacher trajectories can be used for training. Besides, examples already mastered provide little new supervision, while examples far beyond the capability of student may be difficult to learn from. Training data should therefore adapt as model capability evolves, yet reliable data selection is difficult when the student is initially weak. To address these challenges, our key idea is to first establish the basic reasoning capability and then adapt the training data to the evolving capability boundary of the student. We propose BoundaryEvo, a two-stage data--model co-evolution framework. In Capability Priming, we directly distill a small set of teacher-generated long reasoning trajectories without using a separate model to judge and filter them, allowing the student to acquire an initial reasoning capability before capability-aware data selection. In Capability-Boundary Co-Evolution, we repeatedly select questions that the current student has not mastered but the expert can reliably solve, and update the training data as the student evolves. With only 1,000 training examples, BoundaryEvo consistently outperforms static and dynamic data selection baselines across 11 reasoning benchmarks, raising the original Qwen3.5-4B accuracy from 62.9 to 75.3 on IMO-AnswerBench and from 26.8 to 46.0 on AMO-Bench. The code and data will be made publicly available.
中文翻译
从大型教师模型中提炼长链推理轨迹是提升小型语言模型推理能力的有效方法。然而，现有方法通常在蒸馏前固定训练集，忽略了随着学生模型演进，推理示例的有用性会发生变化。当仅能使用少量教师轨迹时，这种不匹配尤为代价高昂。此外，已掌握的示例提供极少新监督信号，而远超学生能力的示例则难以学习。因此，训练数据应随模型能力演化而自适应调整，但在学生初始能力较弱时，可靠的数据选择极具挑战。为此，我们的核心思想是先建立基本推理能力，再根据学生能力边界的演化动态调整训练数据。我们提出BoundaryEvo，一种两阶段数据-模型协同进化框架。在能力预热阶段，直接蒸馏少量教师生成的长推理轨迹，无需额外模型判断与过滤，使学生在具备能力感知数据选择前获得初步推理能力。在能力边界协同演化阶段，反复选取当前学生尚未掌握但专家可稳定解决的问题，并随学生进步持续更新训练数据。仅使用1000个训练示例，BoundaryEvo在11个推理基准上均持续优于静态与动态数据选择基线，将原始Qwen3.5-4B在IMO-AnswerBench上的准确率从62.9提升至75.3，在AMO-Bench上从26.8提升至46.0。代码与数据将公开发布。

Beyond the Final Embedding: Retrieving Memories from Token-Level Evidence
超越最终嵌入：从标记级证据中检索记忆
Memory-augmented assistants retrieve evidence from stored conversations and documents to answer queries. Most embedding-based retrievers compare single-vector representations of queries and memories, even when relevance depends on a small part of the content. Such local evidence can be matched to queries using the contextual token representations already computed by the same embedding models. Our method uses sparse token matching to improve retrieval without additional training. Each query token retrieves a small set of matching tokens across candidate memories. The matching scores are aggregated for each memory and combined with whole-memory similarity to rank memories. An optional low-rank query adapter can be trained with supervision while keeping the encoder and stored memory representations fixed. Our method improves retrieval across eight benchmarks and multiple model families without additional training. Query adaptation further increases the gains to 13.3 and 14.3 percentage points in average Recall@10 and NDCG@10 on Qwen3-Embedding-4B. These retrieval gains translate into higher question-answering accuracy on long-term memory benchmarks.
中文翻译

JANUS: Enhancing Long-Horizon Autonomous Research with Co-Evolving World Models
JANUS：通过共演化世界模型增强长周期自主研究
Long-horizon autonomous research has shown promise in accelerating scientific progress through sustained cycles of hypothesis generation, implementation, and validation. However, this Generate-Execute-Feedback paradigm relies on reactive trial-and-error, where costly and time-consuming executions introduce severe feedback delays, throttling evolutionary progress under explosively expanding search space. To address this challenge, we propose JANUS, a framework that unifies future outcomes forecasting and past experience reflection through a Predict-Verify-Evolve loop driven by a co-evolving world model. Within an evolving graph, the research agent proposes candidates while world model scores them by forecasting value improvement, information gain, and exploration utility, then accordingly selects, refines, or prunes branches prior to execution. After receiving ground-truth feedback, JANUS distills experience into evolving skills: hierarchical skills for research agent, exploration-strategy skills for world model, and their coordination protocols. This mechanism enables continual co-evolution: the research agent sharpens candidate generation while the world model refines its predictive judgment over extended horizons. Extensive evaluations on MLS-Bench-Lite and real research tasks demonstrate that JANUS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving both higher evolving efficiency and ceiling.
中文翻译
长周期自主研究在通过持续的假设生成、实施与验证循环加速科学进步方面展现出巨大潜力。然而，这种‘生成-执行-反馈’范式依赖于反应式的试错过程，昂贵且耗时的执行引入了严重的反馈延迟，在搜索空间急剧扩张的背景下严重制约了进化进展。为应对这一挑战，我们提出 JANUS 框架，通过一个由共演化世界模型驱动的‘预测-验证-演化’循环，统一未来结果预测与过往经验反思。在一个动态演化的图结构中，研究智能体提出候选方案，世界模型通过预测价值提升、信息增益和探索效用对它们进行评分，随后据此选择、优化或剪枝分支后再执行。接收真实反馈后，JANUS 将经验提炼为不断演化的技能：研究智能体的分层技能、世界模型的探索策略技能及其协调协议。这一机制实现了持续的共演化：研究智能体不断提升候选生成能力，世界模型则增强其在长期范围内的预测判断力。在 MLS-Bench-Lite 和真实科研任务上的广泛评估表明，JANUS 显著优于现有最先进基线，实现了更高的演化效率和上限性能。

Locate, Judge, Read: Role-Specialized Tool Agents over a Knowledge-Point Index for Open-Domain Multimodal Document QA
定位、判断、阅读：基于知识点索引的角色专业化工具智能体用于开放域多模态文档问答
Question answering over large heterogeneous document collections requires first finding the right passages and then reading them, yet current retrieval-augmented agents give one model both kinds of tools and let it schedule itself. We present KnowForge, a pipeline separated by job: a hybrid-retrieval knowledge-point index; a Locator agent that can only search; a code-scheduled bridge judge that forces the missing second hop; a typed, size-bounded evidence pool that merges every search's ranking; a Deep Reader agent that can only read text, images and tables; and a synthesizer with a format checker. With a self-hosted Qwen3.6-35B-A3B (3B active), KnowForge reaches F1 47.5 on the full open-domain M3DocVQA (2,441 questions, 3,368 PDFs), +11.0 over the published M3DocRAG system and +7.1 over one-shot retrieve-and-answer on the same index and model, and 53.5 with DeepSeek-V4-Flash. On MultiHop-RAG it reaches 73.5 accuracy, above the strongest published RAG and GraphRAG results (71.2); on the single-document MMLongBench-Doc it lifts the same model from 34.8 to 47.0, level with GPT-4o reading page images (46.3). Per-job ablations on the full benchmarks attribute the gain: the Deep Reader is worth 3.8, 9.8 and 0 points on the three corpora, tracking how visual each is; merging both agents into one with all tools and twice the rounds loses 1.9 F1 because it keeps searching instead of reading; a round-budget sweep shows a step at four Locator rounds and a plateau beyond; query latency scales with model calls rather than corpus size. We serve the three indexed benchmarks as read-only knowledge bases (free registration) and release all prompts and the runner code: this https URL

Travel Back To Move Forward: Retrospective Exploration and Evolving for Long Horizon Agents
回溯以向前：面向长时程智能体的回顾性探索与演化
Large language model agents increasingly operate in long-horizon environments where early mistakes can trigger cascading failures and trap agents in dead-end trajectories. Agents must therefore be able to proactively recover from such states and turn their exploratory experience into guidance for subsequent decisions. We introduce ReTRAIL, a framework that enables agents to rewind the environment to earlier states while preserving valid progress and carrying forward knowledge learned from abandoned exploration. The environment state moves backward while the agent's knowledge continues to move forward. ReTRAIL trains a shared policy with two complementary signals: State-Anchored GRPO compares alternative continuations from revisited states to improve execution, while a branch-advancement reward guides reflection through the progress of subsequent exploration. Experiments across four interactive benchmark MineSweeper, Sokoban, ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that simply adding a rewind primitive provides limited benefit and can even degrade performance. In contrast, ReTRAIL enables agents to rewind from unproductive states and use insights from abandoned branches to guide subsequent exploration. These results demonstrate the value of coupling environment rewinding with experience reuse within an ongoing task for long-horizon task solving. The code is available at this https URL .
中文翻译
大型语言模型智能体越来越多地运行在长时程环境中，早期错误可能引发级联失败并使智能体陷入死胡同轨迹。因此，智能体必须能够主动从这些状态中恢复，并将探索经验转化为后续决策的指导。我们提出了ReTRAIL框架，使智能体能够在保留有效进展的同时，将环境状态回退到早期状态，并携带从被放弃的探索中学习到的知识继续前进。环境状态向后移动，而智能体的知识则持续向前发展。ReTRAIL通过两个互补信号训练共享策略：基于状态锚定的GRPO比较从重访状态出发的替代延续路径以改进执行，而分支推进奖励则通过后续探索的进展引导反思。在四个交互式基准测试（MineSweeper、Sokoban、ALFWorld和ScienceWorld）上的实验表明，仅添加回退原语带来的收益有限，甚至可能降低性能。相比之下，ReTRAIL使智能体能够从无产出状态回退，并利用被放弃分支中的洞察来指导后续探索。这些结果证明了在持续任务中将环境回退与经验复用相结合对于长时程任务求解的价值。代码可在该链接获取。

EmbodiedCLBench: Evaluating Continual Learning for Self-Evolving Embodied Agents
EmbodiedCLBench：评估自演化具身智能体的持续学习能力
Continual learning requires models to accumulate past experience and transfer it to new tasks. Despite substantial research on continual learning, existing work remains largely confined to non-embodied settings, without extending to embodied environments. To address this gap, we introduce EmbodiedCLBench, the first benchmark in embodied simulation for evaluating the continual learning capability of self-evolving agents. With compositional generalization as the core evaluation principle, our benchmark evaluates whether agents can first learn from basic tasks and then recombine the acquired skills to solve complex, unseen advanced tasks. Based on EmbodiedCLBench, we conduct extensive experiments across multiple agent harnesses and learning methods, revealing consistent limitations and distinctive behaviors. Our results show that while advanced tasks can be hardly solved under zero-shot setting, the experience from basic tasks enables consistent improvement. Such benefit from the experience remains stable even if the task complexity increases. However, this improvement does not scale accordingly with the number of learning samples, and current agents cannot extract meaningful information from additional experience. Collectively, our findings highlight continual learning as a fundamental yet underexplored capability, and our benchmark offers valuable resources for designing effective self-evolving agents in embodied environments. Our code and dataset are available at this https URL .

VEPA: Verified Evolving Formal Policy for Lifelong LLM Agent Guardrails
VEPA：可验证演化的形式化策略用于持续学习的LLM智能体防护机制
LLM agents increasingly operate over sensitive resources, such as private documents and mutable system states, which can create serious safety and security risks. Runtime guardrails should therefore make inspectable, reproducible, and deterministic allow or refuse decisions before potentially irreversible agent actions are executed. However, safety and security requirements are rarely complete before deployment, so guardrails need to keep adapting as new risks emerge. Existing adaptive guardrails learn from deployment experience, but they usually apply safety policies through LLM inference, which can lead to inconsistent decisions and ambiguous policy interpretations. Formal guardrails, on the other hand, enforce policies deterministically, but they usually rely on pre-deployment or task-specific policies and have no mechanism to adapt to newly observed risks. We introduce VEPA, a framework for the verified evolution of formal agent guardrails. VEPA represents the guardrail as a formal policy of executable symbolic rules over typed agent events. During deployment, we use an LLM guided by deployment feedback to propose policy patches that fix newly observed failures while preserving previously correct behavior. Then, a deterministic verifier only accepts policies that are structurally valid and conflict-free. Finally, an evidence-aware authority layer activates a verified rule only when it has enough supporting evidence, so uncertain updates cannot directly affect guardrail decisions. Across ASSEBench-Safety, ASSEBench-Security, and ATBench, VEPA consistently outperforms baselines while expanding the scope of deterministic formal-policy enforcement. These gains also hold under more challenging deployment conditions, including mixed streams, corrupted feedback, and longer horizons.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体越来越多地操作敏感资源，如私有文档和可变系统状态，这可能带来严重的安全与隐私风险。因此，运行时防护机制应在执行不可逆动作前做出可检查、可复现且确定性的允许或拒绝决策。然而，安全与安全需求在部署前往往不完整，防护机制需随新风险出现而持续适应。现有自适应防护机制通过LLM推理学习部署经验，但常导致决策不一致和策略解释模糊。形式化防护机制虽能确定性地执行策略，但通常依赖于预部署或任务特定策略，缺乏应对新风险的适应机制。我们提出VEPA框架，实现形式化智能体防护机制的可验证演化。VEPA将防护机制表示为在类型化智能体事件上可执行的符号规则形式策略。部署期间，我们利用部署反馈引导的LLM提出修复新发现失败的策略补丁，同时保持先前正确行为不变。随后，确定性验证器仅接受结构有效且无冲突的策略。最后，基于证据的权威层仅在拥有足够支持证据时激活已验证规则，确保不确定更新不会直接影响防护决策。在ASSEBench-Safety、ASSEBench-Security和ATBench上，VEPA始终优于基线方法，同时扩展了确定性形式策略执行的范围。这些优势在更具挑战性的部署条件下（如混合流、损坏反馈、更长时间窗）依然成立。

Meta-Reflective Evolution Effectively Improves Production AI Systems
元反思进化有效提升生产环境AI系统的性能
Large Language Model (LLM)-guided evolutionary optimization has successfully improved prompts, programs, and even entire AI systems. Most optimization methods select a candidate from the search frontier, then mutate it in one LLM pass based only on its failures. This focus on the frontier can stall on production AI systems, where isolated failures reveal little about complex, hidden interactions. We introduce CoCoEvolve (Cortex Code Evolve), an evolutionary optimizer built around what we call meta-reflection, wherein an agentic mutator (Cortex Code) selectively retrieves evidence from the entire evolutionary history and probes the live system before verifying and proposing mutations. We apply CoCoEvolve to optimize three classes of production AI systems: data agents, LLM-powered data pipelines, and data engineering workflows. On DABStep, CoCoEvolve tops the public leaderboard, reaching 92.8% held-out hard accuracy compared to 78.4% for Meta-Harness and 30.4% for GEPA under matched conditions. Across six LLM data pipeline workloads, it outperforms every evaluated baseline. On data-eng-bench, CoCoEvolve lifts test-set solve rate from 59.1% to 77.4%, past the best baseline of 73.6%. It also has 11 percentage points higher accuracy than GEPA on HotpotQA while remaining competitive with specialized optimizers on established mathematical and systems benchmarks. Matched ablations confirm that each component of CoCoEvolve contributes to these gains, demonstrating the value of meta-reflection for optimizing production AI systems.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）引导的进化优化已成功用于改进提示词、程序乃至整个AI系统。大多数优化方法仅从搜索前沿选择候选者，并在一次LLM调用中根据其失败进行突变。这种对前沿的关注在生产环境中容易停滞，因为孤立的失败难以揭示复杂隐藏的交互。我们提出CoCoEvolve（Cortex Code Evolve），一种基于“元反思”的进化优化器，其中代理型突变器（Cortex Code）会从完整的进化历史中选择性检索证据，并在验证和提出突变前探查实时系统。我们将CoCoEvolve应用于三类生产环境AI系统：数据代理、LLM驱动的数据流水线和数据工程工作流。在DABStep上，CoCoEvolve在公开排行榜上领先，达到92.8%的保留硬样本准确率，远超Meta-Harness的78.4%和GEPA的30.4%（在相同条件下）。在六个LLM数据流水线工作负载中，其表现优于所有评估的基线。在data-eng-bench上，CoCoEvolve将测试集求解率从59.1%提升至77.4%，超过最佳基线的73.6%。在HotpotQA上，其准确率比GEPA高出11个百分点，同时在经典数学与系统基准上仍保持与专用优化器相当的竞争力。匹配消融实验确认了CoCoEvolve各组件均贡献显著，证明了元反思在优化生产AI系统中的价值。
👤 Seung Won Wilson Yoo, Chang Zeng, Aditi Somayajula, Sahithi Ankireddy, Rohan Kosalge et al. 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv

REDaCT: Collapse-Free Unlearning in Large Reasoning Models
REDaCT：大推理模型中的无崩溃遗忘机制
Unlearning in large reasoning models (LRMs) requires removing forget-set information from both the reasoning trace and the final answer. Existing approaches achieve this objective at the cost of reasoning collapse, where the model fails to produce a coherent chain-of-thought (CoT) and does not properly terminate its reasoning process. To address this challenge, we propose REDaCT (REgenerate via Detection and Corrective Tuning), an inference-time unlearning framework for LRMs. REDaCT consists of two key components: (1) a binary leak detector that identifies sentences in the reasoning trace that leak forget-set information, and (2) an embedding optimization module that learns a corrective embedding offline, such that when injected at the first detected leak during inference, it guides subsequent reasoning towards sentences that do not leak forget-set information. Experimental results show that REDaCT completely avoids reasoning collapse, substantially improving reasoning preservation compared to state-of-the-art unlearning methods while maintaining perfect answer forgetting and near-perfect CoT forgetting. Our code is available at: this https URL .
中文翻译
大推理模型（LRMs）中的遗忘需要从推理过程和最终答案中移除遗忘集信息。现有方法虽达成此目标，但代价是引发推理崩溃，即模型无法生成连贯的思维链（CoT），也无法正确终止推理过程。为解决此挑战，我们提出REDaCT（通过检测与纠正微调再生），一种面向LRMs的推理时遗忘框架。REDaCT包含两个核心组件：(1) 二分类泄露检测器，用于识别推理过程中泄露遗忘集信息的句子；(2) 嵌入优化模块，离线学习一个纠正嵌入，当在推理过程中首次检测到泄露时注入该嵌入，从而引导后续推理走向不泄露遗忘集信息的句子。实验结果表明，REDaCT完全避免了推理崩溃，在推理保留方面显著优于当前最优遗忘方法，同时保持完美的答案遗忘和近乎完美的CoT遗忘。代码已公开。
👤 Joshua Maccoby, Wendy Hui Wang 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv

Recurrent Harness for Agentic Continual Learning
用于智能体持续学习的循环式Harness框架
Language model agents increasingly operate over long interaction streams, requiring online adaptation to distribution shifts through continual learning. However, existing agent harnesses typically either evolve their states only through forward information flow or rely on additional replay or validation data for harness optimization. To address this, we propose ReHarness, a recurrent harness framework for agentic continual learning that complements forward read and write with backward revision under a strictly online streaming protocol. We ask what information could have been captured differently in the harness state to better inform the current instance in the stream. Specifically, after each instance, we leverage the resulting trajectory as feedback to revise the reflections retrieved for that instance, thereby propagating feedback backward through the textual harness state. Across three continual learning benchmarks and three language model backbones, ReHarness consistently outperforms strong raw-context baselines, memory- and reflection-based methods, and prompt optimization approaches. Further analysis shows that ReHarness produces source-grounded and meaningful revisions, corrects overgeneralized or outdated experience under distribution shifts, and improves both stability and plasticity during continual learning.
中文翻译
语言模型智能体越来越多地在长时间交互流中运行，需要通过持续学习实现对分布变化的在线适应。然而，现有的智能体Harness通常仅通过前向信息流来更新状态，或依赖额外的重放数据或验证数据进行优化。为解决这一问题，我们提出了ReHarness，一种用于智能体持续学习的循环式Harness框架，该框架在严格的在线流协议下，将前向读写与后向修订相结合。我们探讨了Harness状态中可能以不同方式捕获的信息，以更好地指导当前流中的实例。具体而言，在每个实例之后，我们利用生成的轨迹作为反馈，对相应实例的反思内容进行修订，从而将反馈反向传播至文本形式的Harness状态中。在三个持续学习基准和三种语言模型主干上，ReHarness始终优于强大的原始上下文基线、基于记忆和反思的方法以及提示优化方法。进一步分析表明，ReHarness能够产生基于源信息且有意义的修订，纠正在分布变化下过度泛化或过时的经验，并提升持续学习过程中的稳定性和可塑性。
👤 Yuxin Xiao, Zhiyun Lu, Jianyu Wang, Yangtian Zhang, Yang Song et al. 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv

An Input-Dependent Readout Phase for Resonant Sequence Models
共振序列模型的输入相关读出阶段
Sequence models with a fixed-size state avoid the quadratic cost of attention, but face a design choice. An input-dependent state transition gives content-dependent memory at the cost of FFT-based training, while a fixed transition keeps the O(T log T) convolutional path but reads memory the same way for every input. ResonatorLM, a recent model of the second kind, reads a bank of damped complex oscillators through a fixed learned phase per head. We make that readout phase a function of the current token, which adds input dependence to the read only, keeps the convolution exact, and costs 0.2 percent more parameters. An analysis of the untrained bank shows that phase does encode elapsed time, but an ordinary token falls below the background within 2-8 steps and no choice of damping extends linear reconstruction beyond about 16 tokens. In 6M-parameter character-level models on WikiText-2, the input-dependent phase matches the original at the training length and removes its failure beyond it: trained on 256 tokens and tested on 1024, perplexity falls from 12.81 to 3.76. We trace the failure to long-memory heads that accumulate more history than training exposed them to. The learned phase tracks the current character rather than distance and does not improve retrieval.
中文翻译
具有固定大小状态的序列模型避免了注意力机制的二次计算成本，但面临设计选择。输入相关的状态转移虽能带来内容依赖的记忆，却需牺牲基于FFT的训练效率；而固定转移则保持O(T log T)的卷积路径，但对所有输入采用相同的记忆读取方式。ResonatorLM是一种近期属于后一类的模型，它通过每个头固定的已学习相位读取一组阻尼复数振荡器。我们将其读出相位改为当前标记的函数，仅在读取阶段引入输入依赖性，保持卷积精确性，且仅增加0.2%的参数量。对未训练的振荡器库的分析显示，相位确实编码了时间流逝，但普通标记在2-8步内便低于背景噪声，且任何阻尼设置都无法将线性重建扩展至约16个标记以上。在WikiText-2上的600万参数字符级模型中，输入相关的相位在训练长度内表现与原始模型相当，并克服了其在更长序列上的失败：在256标记上训练并在1024标记上测试时，困惑度从12.81降至3.76。我们追踪到失败根源在于长记忆头积累了超过训练暴露范围的历史信息。学习到的相位跟踪的是当前字符而非距离，且并未改善检索效果。
👤 Shivesh Rane 📅 2026/09/26 🔗 arXiv

RSI-Anything: Unlocking Recursive Self-Improvement with World Models
RSI-Anything：通过世界模型解锁递归自我改进
Recursive self-improvement (RSI) aims to transform interaction experience into persistent improvements, yet external feedback is costly and world-model predictions may become unreliable as policies evolve. We introduce RSI-Anything, a domain-general framework built on JEPA-Anything that uses predictive world models to guide action and learning-data selection. Observed feedback drives policy or workflow improvement and conditional world-model adaptation, while component and compatibility checks govern the retention of updated policy-model pairs. Across diverse computational and scientific domains, RSI-Anything demonstrates substantial gains. In code repair, with the coder fixed, success increases from 26.3% with a static verifier to 29.2% through predictive selection and 31.2% after world-model adaptation, at comparable test usage. Graphical user interface (GUI) evaluations showed higher task completion, while average actions decrease by 39.4%. Across five successive policy versions, laboratory-planning scores more than double from 21.80% to 46.01%. In reaction optimization, RSI-Anything achieves 96.15% mean best observed yield versus 82.16% for Bayesian optimization under the same 40-experiment budget. An end-to-end immune-science application further connects workflow evolution and predictive modeling with therapeutic hypothesis generation. These results demonstrate how predictive world models and retained system updates enable recursive improvement across diverse environments.
中文翻译
递归自我改进（RSI）旨在将交互体验转化为持久的改进，但外部反馈成本高昂，且随着策略演化，世界模型的预测可能变得不可靠。我们提出了RSI-Anything，一个基于JEPA-Anything的通用领域框架，利用预测性世界模型指导行动和学习数据选择。观察到的反馈驱动策略或工作流改进以及条件性世界模型适应，而组件和兼容性检查则控制更新后的策略-模型对的保留。在多种计算和科学领域中，RSI-Anything均表现出显著提升。在代码修复任务中，固定编码器的情况下，成功率从静态验证器的26.3%提升至通过预测选择的29.2%，再经世界模型适应后达到31.2%，测试使用量相当。图形用户界面（GUI）评估显示任务完成率更高，平均操作次数减少39.4%。在连续五版策略中，实验室规划得分从21.80%提升至46.01%以上。在反应优化中，RSI-Anything在相同40次实验预算下实现96.15%的平均最优产率，优于贝叶斯优化的82.16%。一个端到端的免疫科学应用进一步将工作流演化与预测建模连接至治疗假说生成。这些结果表明，预测性世界模型与保留的系统更新共同实现了跨多样化环境的递归改进。
👤 Taoyong Cui, Pinxian Zeng, Wanghan Xu, Hao Xiao, Shuhan Xue et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

Can Language Models Learn When to Abstain? Policy Learning and Calibrated Answer Selection
语言模型能否学会何时放弃回答？策略学习与校准的答案选择
A useful language model should know when to answer and when to say "I don't know." Can we improve that decision by training the model itself or by training a separate component to check its answers? We study both approaches on Qwen3-14B for factual questions, using a score that rewards correct answers, penalizes mistakes, and allows abstention. Across three training seeds and two held-out confidence thresholds, a learned answer selector improves threshold-averaged utility over the original model's calibrated confidence filter. On TriviaQA, mean coverage increases from 20.2% to 30.9%, while the pooled error rate among scored answers decreases from 23.0% to 14.5% under exact-match grading. The tested reinforcement-learning changes do not establish consistent gains. On NQ-Open, the selector improves threshold-averaged utility over the filter but still performs worse than always abstaining. A partial human-reviewed audit preserves the TriviaQA finding. Our results support learning to select answers in this setting while showing that reliable abstention across datasets remains unresolved.
中文翻译
一个有用的语言模型应知道何时回答，何时说“我不知道”。我们能否通过训练模型自身或训练一个独立组件来检查其答案，从而改善这一判断？我们在Qwen3-14B上针对事实类问题研究了这两种方法，采用一种奖励正确回答、惩罚错误并允许放弃的回答评分机制。在三个训练种子和两个保留置信度阈值下，学习到的答案选择器在阈值平均效用上优于原始模型的校准置信度过滤器。在TriviaQA上，平均覆盖率从20.2%提升至30.9%，而在精确匹配评分下，已评分答案的合并错误率从23.0%下降至14.5%。所测试的强化学习变化未展现出一致收益。在NQ-Open上，选择器虽优于过滤器，但仍劣于始终放弃回答的表现。部分人工审核审计验证了TriviaQA的结果。我们的结果支持在此设定下学习答案选择，但也表明在跨数据集实现可靠放弃回答仍是一个未解决的问题。
👤 Juan Terven, Diana-Margarita Córdova-Esparza, Ruben Gomez, Carlos Miranda Rocha, Julio-Alejandro Romero-González et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

Inject a Turn, Not a Token: Learning Where to Redirect LLM Reasoning for Self-Correction
注入一次对话轮次，而非一个标记：学习如何引导大语言模型推理以实现自我修正
Large language models struggle to reliably identify and correct errors in their own reasoning. Unlike prior training-based approaches that fine-tune an LLM to generate revisions or feedback, we learn where to intervene in failed rollouts and determine how to redirect them. To choose where to intervene, we train a 130K-parameter MLP head on a frozen encoder using only final-answer correctness. For redirection, we find that under a fixed generation budget, the tested inline injections fail to reliably redirect reasoning, whereas presenting the same instruction in a new user turn changes the model's course. The resulting correction framework, UPTURN (User-Prompt TURN injection at a learned position), requires no LLM fine-tuning: it retains a selected prefix, injects an instruction as a new user turn, and regenerates the suffix. Across four reasoners and six math benchmarks, UPTURN improves average accuracy in every setting over a 32-rollout baseline. It outperforms self-correction and reflection-token injection baselines under matched correction budgets, transfers across reasoners and to GPQA-Diamond without retraining, and complements GRPO and self-correction methods.
中文翻译
大型语言模型难以可靠地识别并纠正自身推理中的错误。与以往基于训练的方法不同，这些方法微调LLM以生成修改或反馈，我们学习在失败推理路径中何处进行干预，并决定如何重新引导。为确定干预位置，我们在冻结编码器上仅使用最终答案正确性训练了一个13万参数的MLP头。对于重定向，我们发现，在固定生成预算下，测试的内联注入无法可靠地改变推理方向，而将相同指令以新的用户对话轮次呈现则能改变模型走向。由此提出的校正框架UPTURN（用户提示轮次注入于学习位置）无需对LLM进行微调：它保留选定前缀，将指令作为新用户轮次注入，并重新生成后缀。在四个推理器和六个数学基准测试中，UPTURN在所有设置下均优于32次滚动基线。在匹配校正预算下，其表现优于自校正和反射标记注入基线，可在不重新训练的情况下跨推理器迁移至GPQA-Diamond，并与GRPO和自校正方法互补。
👤 Jiwon Jeon, Myungsik Cho, Jeonghye Kim, Youngchul Sung 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

Training User Simulators with Latent Intrinsic Feedback
基于潜在内在反馈训练用户模拟器
User simulators are gaining attention as environments to train LLM assistants that can better cope with the nuances of multi-turn conversations with real users. Prior work on user simulation has emphasized generating realistic user utterances, leaving unexplored their potential as a source of direct feedback on the assistant's responses. Such evaluation signals may not be explicitly communicated in language, but can instead reside in the latent reasoning of users. In this work, we introduce user simulators with intrinsic feedback, namely, models equipped with latent evaluation states: internal thoughts, reactions, and scalar ratings that evaluate the assistant from a first-person user perspective at each conversation turn. We train LLM-based simulators using interaction logs between real users and assistants and demonstrate that intrinsic feedback improves simulation realism and critiquing behaviors versus prompted simulator baselines, while also providing turn-level evaluations that existing fine-tuned simulators lack. Further, evaluations generated by our trained simulators correlate better with human judgments than those of prompted simulators of the same size. Lastly, our experiments reveal that simulators with intrinsic feedback provide effective reward signals for multi-turn RL training, matching setups that rely on external judge LLMs for rewards while cutting RL compute by more than half.
中文翻译
用户模拟器正日益成为训练大语言模型助手的环境，使其能更好地应对与真实用户的多轮对话中的细微差别。先前的用户模拟研究强调生成逼真的用户话语，而未探索其作为直接反馈来源的潜力。此类评估信号可能并未显式体现在语言中，而是存在于用户的潜在推理中。本文提出带有内在反馈的用户模拟器，即配备潜在评估状态的模型：每个对话轮次中内部思考、反应和标量评分，从第一人称用户视角评估助手的表现。我们使用真实用户与助手之间的交互日志训练基于LLM的模拟器，并证明内在反馈提升了模拟的真实性与批判行为，优于提示型模拟器基线，同时提供了现有微调模拟器所缺乏的轮次级评估。此外，由我们训练的模拟器生成的评估与人类判断的相关性优于同规模的提示型模拟器。最后，实验表明，带有内在反馈的模拟器为多轮强化学习训练提供了有效的奖励信号，在奖励依赖外部裁判LLM的设置中表现相当，同时将RL计算量减少超过一半。
👤 Tarek Naous, Craig Boutilier, Wei Xu, Ofer Meshi 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

Proactive Agentic Orchestration
主动型智能体编排
Agentic orchestration delegates tasks to sub-agents with independent contexts, providing a key path to extending the effective execution horizon of large language model agents. However, existing orchestration methods face two limitations: in execution, current methods fix sub-agents' goals and harnesses at creation time, limiting their adaptability to dynamic real-world environments; in optimization, sparse and delayed feedback constrains effective learning of the orchestration policy. To address these limitations, we introduce POrchestra, an on-the-fly proactive agentic orchestration framework. It allows sub-agents to proactively communicate with the orchestrator at any logical point to request harness adjustments while maintaining continuity of local execution. This mechanism not only enables sub-agents to adapt seamlessly to dynamic environments but also naturally inspires On-Policy Orchestration Distillation (OPOD) based on hindsight feedback. OPOD attributes sub-agents' proactive revision requests back to preceding orchestration decisions to derive targeted improvement signals, and leverages these signals as process supervision alongside the final reward to optimize the orchestration policy. We evaluate POrchestra on three long-horizon agent benchmarks spanning deep research, dynamic digital assistance, and software engineering. POrchestra improves task success by an average of 9.1% relative to the strongest baseline; combined with OPOD, it improves small-orchestrator success by an average of 10.0% relative to existing training baselines. POrchestra also achieves a more favorable cost-performance trade-off. Our code is publicly available at this https URL .
中文翻译
智能体编排将任务委派给具有独立上下文的子智能体，是扩展大型语言模型智能体有效执行范围的关键路径。然而，现有编排方法存在两个局限：在执行层面，当前方法在创建时固定子智能体的目标和工具，限制其对动态现实环境的适应能力；在优化层面，稀疏且延迟的反馈制约了编排策略的有效学习。为解决这些局限，我们提出了POrchestra——一种实时主动型智能体编排框架。它允许子智能体在任意逻辑点主动与编排器通信，请求调整工具，同时保持本地执行的连续性。该机制不仅使子智能体能无缝适应动态环境，还自然启发了基于事后反馈的“在策略编排蒸馏”（OPOD）。OPOD将子智能体的主动修订请求回溯至之前的编排决策，生成针对性的改进信号，并将这些信号作为过程监督，与最终奖励一起用于优化编排策略。我们在三个长周期智能体基准上评估POrchestra，涵盖深度研究、动态数字助手和软件工程领域。POrchestra相比最强基线平均提升了9.1%的任务成功率；结合OPOD后，小规模编排器的成功率平均提升10.0%。POrchestra还实现了更优的成本-性能权衡。代码已公开于此https链接。
👤 Xueyang Feng, Chaozhuo Li, Xiaohe Bo, Jiakai Tang, Chenxi Li et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
