MoPrO-SD: Modular Prompt Optimization for Stance Detection
MoPrO-SD：立场检测的模块化提示优化
Stance detection remains challenging for large language models (LLMs) due to implicit opinions, sarcasm, and context-dependent expressions of stance. Although automatic prompt optimization offers an alternative to fine-tuning without updating model parameters, existing structured approaches are not specifically designed around stance-label semantics and class-specific ambiguities. We introduce MoPrO-SD, a stance-specific prompt optimization framework that decomposes prompts into functional semantic modules and refines them sequentially using LLM-based feedback. We study three refinement strategies: instruction-only optimization (I), instruction with reasoning (I+R), and instruction with per-example reasoning (I+R+E), using class-conditioned errors for stance-label refinement. We also introduce VaccineStance, a new human-annotated benchmark for vaccine stance detection. Experiments across four datasets and five open-weight LLMs show that MoPrO-SD improves upon the human-written Base prompt in many settings. On SemEval-2016, class-conditioned feedback achieves higher mean Macro-F1 than pooled feedback for four of five backbones. Both reasoning-based strategies, I+R and I+R+E, achieve strong performance, with their relative effectiveness varying across models and datasets, while remaining competitive with recent prompt optimization methods. The MoPrO-SD code and VaccineStance dataset are available on GitHub.
中文翻译
由于隐含观点、讽刺语气和上下文依赖的立场表达，立场检测对大型语言模型（LLMs）仍具挑战性。尽管自动提示优化可在不更新模型参数的情况下替代微调，但现有结构化方法并未专门针对立场标签语义和类别特定模糊性进行设计。我们提出了MoPrO-SD，一种面向立场检测的提示优化框架，将提示分解为功能语义模块，并利用LLM反馈进行顺序优化。我们研究了三种优化策略：仅指令优化（I）、带推理的指令优化（I+R），以及带示例推理的指令优化（I+R+E），并使用类别条件错误进行立场标签精炼。我们还引入了VaccineStance，一个全新的用于疫苗立场检测的人工标注基准数据集。在四个数据集和五个开源权重LLM上的实验表明，MoPrO-SD在多数场景下优于人工编写的基线提示。在SemEval-2016上，类别条件反馈在五种骨干模型中的四种上实现了高于聚合反馈的平均宏F1值。基于推理的两种策略（I+R 和 I+R+E）均表现优异，其相对效果因模型和数据集而异，但仍保持与近期提示优化方法相当的竞争力。MoPrO-SD代码和VaccineStance数据集已在GitHub公开。

Overcoming Data Imbalances with Expert Selection Biases
通过专家选择偏差克服数据不平衡
Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have become the standard architecture for frontier LLMs. The multiple parallel experts in each transformer block theoretically enable expert specialization according to categories such as domains, languages, or modalities during pretraining. However, when data is imbalanced across categories, minority categories can become concentrated in a small fraction of the expert pool or spread inefficiently across them, effectively limiting their available capacity. In this work, we demonstrate that category-conditioned expert-selection biases can preferentially route tokens from different categories toward specialized expert pools. By applying biases based on input category, we provide a simple way to induce soft category-aligned specialization. This intervention encourages categories toward distinct expert subsets while retaining cross-category sharing when useful. More importantly, it enables the over-allocation of expert capacity to categories that would otherwise be disadvantaged by data scarcity. We observe performance improvements across modalities, low-resource languages, and code in models up to 10.6B parameters. At our smallest and largest model scales, these gains are equivalent to allocating 18.5% and 2.7% additional baseline training tokens, respectively. Overall, our results show that expert biases can serve as a lightweight control for category-level expert allocation, making room for low-resource categories while retaining standard MoE inference.
中文翻译
混合专家（MoE）模型已成为前沿大语言模型的标准架构。每个Transformer模块中的多个并行专家理论上可在预训练过程中根据领域、语言或模态等类别实现专家专业化。然而，当各类别间数据分布不均时，少数类别可能集中在极小部分专家池中，或低效地分散在众多专家之间，从而有效限制其可用容量。本文表明，基于类别的专家选择偏差可优先将不同类别的token引导至专门的专家池。通过基于输入类别的偏差应用，我们提供了一种简单方法以诱导软性的类别对齐专业化。该干预措施促使各类别趋向不同的专家子集，同时在需要时保留跨类别共享能力。更重要的是，它使原本因数据稀缺而处于劣势的类别能够获得超额的专家容量分配。我们在参数量高达106亿的模型中观察到在多模态、低资源语言和代码任务上的性能提升。在最小和最大模型规模下，这些收益分别相当于额外分配18.5%和2.7%的基础训练token。总体而言，我们的结果表明，专家偏差可作为类别级专家分配的轻量级控制手段，在为低资源类别腾出空间的同时，保持标准MoE推理效率。

Test-Time Memory Construction
测试时记忆构建
Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to answer questions over long interaction histories, where relevant evidence may be scattered across distant sessions. Existing memory systems typically construct and maintain sophisticated memory representations offline, before user queries are observed, incurring substantial overhead and limiting their ability to adapt memory construction to query-specific information needs. In this paper, we propose T$^2$Mem, a Test-Time Memory construction framework that defers memory construction to inference and progressively builds memory along the reasoning process. Specifically, T$^2$Mem extracts lightweight episode-level metadata offline to capture key events and temporal associations as retrieval cues, while retaining links to the original interactions to facilitate the reconstruction of dependencies across episodes. At test time, the LLM adaptively retrieves and organizes metadata and raw interactions, progressively constructing query-specific memory as reasoning unfolds. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show the effectiveness of T$^2$Mem by consistently outperforming strong baselines across different backbone LLMs while substantially reducing the overhead of memory construction. Further analysis demonstrates that deferring memory construction to inference enables the memory construction process to dynamically adapt to the information needs of individual queries. All code is available at this https URL .
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）难以回答涉及长交互历史的问题，因为相关证据可能分散在遥远的会话中。现有的记忆系统通常在用户查询出现前离线构建并维护复杂的记忆表示，导致显著开销，并限制其根据查询特定信息需求动态调整记忆构建的能力。本文提出T²Mem，一种测试时记忆构建框架，将记忆构建推迟到推理阶段，并随着推理过程逐步构建记忆。具体而言，T²Mem离线提取轻量级的事件级元数据，以捕捉关键事件和时间关联作为检索线索，同时保留与原始交互的链接，以促进跨会话依赖关系的重建。在测试时，LLM自适应地检索和组织元数据与原始交互，逐步构建针对查询的专用记忆。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上的实验表明，T²Mem在不同骨干LLM上持续优于强基线，同时大幅降低记忆构建开销。进一步分析显示，将记忆构建推迟到推理阶段，使记忆构建过程能够动态适应单个查询的信息需求。所有代码均已公开。

Do Prompt Optimizers Break Requirements They Are Not Scored On? A Preregistered Study
提示优化器是否会破坏它们未被评分的要求？一项预注册研究
Prompt optimizers rewrite a prompt to raise one target metric, but prompts also state requirements the metric does not score, such as an output format, citations, or abstention. In a preregistered study, we run GEPA, MIPROv2, and TextGrad on HotpotQA, SQuAD 2.0, and IFEval and count per-item violations of unscored requirements with deterministic checks. By the preregistered criterion, all three optimizers produce regressions, substantially on HotpotQA, where net regression in citation validity reaches 10.7 to 41.6 points while exact match rises by 21 to 34 points. A post-hoc decomposition changes this reading. The optimized prompts elicit reasoning before the required JSON object, and under the 200-token output cap used throughout, 13.9% to 47.0% of responses end before any answer appears. Among parseable outputs, net regression is within 3 points of zero. With a 1,024-token cap, extraction failures disappear, content regression stays below 1 point, and exact match rises further. Two simple gates fail the preregistered criterion. The side effect is narrower than hypothesized: optimized prompts are more accurate and break an explicit output contract, and a lenient target metric does not show the cost.
中文翻译
提示优化器通过重写提示来提升目标指标，但提示中还包含指标未评分的要求，如输出格式、引用或拒绝回答。在一项预注册研究中，我们在HotpotQA、SQuAD 2.0和IFEval上运行GEPA、MIPROv2和TextGrad，并使用确定性检查统计每项任务中未被评分要求的违反次数。根据预注册标准，三种优化器均产生退化，尤其在HotpotQA上表现严重，其中引用有效性净退化达10.7至41.6分，而精确匹配提升21至34分。事后分解改变了这一解读。优化后的提示在所需JSON对象之前引出推理过程，在整个实验中使用的200-token输出上限下，13.9%至47.0%的响应在任何答案出现前就结束。在可解析输出中，净退化在零附近（不超过3分）。当使用1,024-token上限时，提取失败消失，内容退化保持在1分以下，精确匹配进一步提升。两个简单门控机制未能通过预注册标准。副作用比预期更窄：优化提示更准确，但违背了明确的输出契约，且宽松的目标指标未显示出成本。
👤 Sangdo Han, Moonsoo Kim 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

PreSTO: Predictive Subtree Prefetching for Fast LLM Power Sampling
PreSTO：用于快速LLM幂采样的预测子树预取
Power sampling improves large language model (LLM) reasoning at test time by sampling from a sharpened version of the base model's distribution. Its sampler, PowerMH, is slow because its Metropolis--Hastings (MH) updates are sequential: each accept/reject decision sets the state from which the next proposal is generated. Prior work reduces this cost by changing the sampler or the model (e.g., sequential Monte Carlo, approximate lookahead, distillation). We propose Predictive SubTree Prefetching (PreSTO), which accelerates PowerMH by prefetching future proposals and provably leaves its MH transition kernel unchanged. PreSTO views future MH outcomes as a tree of accept/reject decisions: in each model call, it generates the proposals of a \textit{prefetched subtree} of this tree in one batch, then follows the realized path with the original MH acceptance rule. We also observe that MH reaches only a sparse, nearly unary part of each prefetched subtree, we further design a \textit{predictive traversal rule} that chooses the subtree to maximize the predicted number of completed transitions. Across seven datasets and five base LLMs, PreSTO achieves median speedups of $1.71\times$, $1.91\times$, and $2.33\times$ on PowerMH and its follow-ups EntropyCut and MultiTryMH, respectively; on the first two, it makes a median of $3.9\times$ fewer model calls, each only $2.1\times$ longer. On LCB V6 with Qwen3.5-9B, the predictive traversal rule is a further $1.25\times$--$1.37\times$ faster than the default breadth-first traversal.
中文翻译
幂采样通过从基础模型分布的锐化版本中采样，提升了大语言模型（LLM）在推理阶段的表现。其采样器PowerMH因马尔可夫链-蒙特卡洛（MH）更新为串行过程而速度较慢：每次接受/拒绝决策决定了下一提议生成的状态。先前工作通过改变采样器或模型（如序列蒙特卡洛、近似前瞻、蒸馏）来降低此开销。我们提出预测子树预取（PreSTO），通过预取未来提议来加速PowerMH，并严格保证其MH转移核不变。PreSTO将未来的MH结果视为接受/拒绝决策的树结构：每次模型调用批量生成该树的一个预取子树的提议，然后按原始MH接受规则沿实际路径推进。我们还观察到，MH仅到达每个预取子树中稀疏且近乎单枝的部分，因此进一步设计了预测遍历规则，选择能最大化预期完成转换数的子树。在七个数据集和五种基础LLM上，PreSTO分别实现了PowerMH及其后续方法EntropyCut和MultiTryMH的1.71倍、1.91倍和2.33倍的中位加速；前两种方法中，其模型调用次数减少3.9倍，每次调用仅延长2.1倍。在LCB V6与Qwen3.5-9B上，预测遍历规则比默认广度优先遍历进一步快1.25至1.37倍。

Scaling Agentic Data for Long-Horizon Terminal Intelligence
面向长时程终端智能的智能体数据规模化
Terminal agents are moving beyond local operations toward completing real-world professional tasks. Short trajectories can demonstrate commands and tool calls, but they do not fully show how agents accumulate evidence through exploration, use it to inform judgments, continually adjust strategies based on feedback, and turn intermediate results into final deliverables. High-quality long-horizon trajectories capture these agentic behavioral patterns and their connections within a single problem-solving process, providing richer experience for sustained autonomous work. However, environment synthesis may complete or simplify the key work underlying these patterns in advance and thus remove the need for exploration, implementation, and verification that the agent was meant to perform. To address this, we introduce TerminalHorizon, a fully automated data synthesis engine for long-horizon terminal agents. Starting from real-world professional work, it uses independent execution to identify key work omitted during construction and uses the resulting evidence to guide environment reconstruction. To extend these patterns across stages, it progressively expands tasks under the same goal, introducing new requirements that build on prior results and call for further exploration and improvement. Using this engine, we construct 1.5K task environments and perform supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.5-27B on the resulting dataset to obtain TerminalHorizon-27B. We achieve substantial performance gains on all 12 benchmarks spanning coding-agent tasks, agentic tasks in real-world production settings, and general reasoning. Multiple RSI case studies, each with a 12-hour research budget, further demonstrate the model's ability to continue experimenting beyond an initial solution, improve its methods based on feedback, and retain effective improvements in its final deliverables.
中文翻译
终端智能体正从局部操作转向完成真实世界的专业任务。短轨迹可展示命令与工具调用，但无法充分展现智能体如何通过探索积累证据、利用证据做出判断、根据反馈持续调整策略，并将中间结果转化为最终交付成果。高质量的长时程轨迹捕捉这些智能体行为模式及其在单一问题解决过程中的关联，为持续自主工作提供更丰富的经验。然而，环境合成可能提前完成或简化这些模式背后的关键工作，从而消除智能体本应执行的探索、实施与验证需求。为解决此问题，我们引入TerminalHorizon，一个完全自动化的长时程终端智能体数据合成引擎。它从真实世界专业工作出发，通过独立执行识别构建过程中遗漏的关键工作，并利用所得证据指导环境重构。为在各阶段扩展这些模式，它在相同目标下逐步扩展任务，引入基于前期结果的新要求，推动进一步探索与改进。利用该引擎，我们构建了1500个任务环境，并对Qwen3.5-27B进行监督微调，得到TerminalHorizon-27B模型。该模型在涵盖编码代理任务、真实生产环境中智能体任务及通用推理的12个基准上均取得显著性能提升。多个RSI案例研究（每项12小时研究预算）进一步证明，该模型具备在初始解决方案基础上持续实验、根据反馈改进方法并保留有效改进的能力，最终交付高质量成果。

SOAR: Source-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Precise Credit Assignment for Search Agents
SOAR：面向搜索智能体的源感知强化学习与精确信用分配
Reinforcement learning (RL) for search agents often suffers from evidence fabrication, a failure mode where agents bypass external information ($E_{ext}$) and fabricate an incorrect answer. We argue that this fabrication arises from an over-reliance on unreliable internal knowledge ($E_{int}$) as shortcuts due to the inherent opacity of reasoning trajectories. To address this, we propose SOAR (SOurce-Aware Reinforcement learning), a paradigm that transforms monolithic reasoning into source-explicit trajectories. SOAR (i) disentangles $E_{ext}$ and $E_{int}$ into parallel, observable streams via a Source-Aware Workflow, and (ii) incorporates Source-Consistent Reward to guide the policy evolution. By making information-source attribution explicit, SOAR enables precise credit assignment between different knowledge streams. Evaluations show that SOAR outperforms baselines on 3B and 7B backbones by 7.3% and 5.4%, while reducing evidence fabrication by 74.8%. These confirm that SOAR significantly improves performance on knowledge-intensive benchmarks while effectively mitigating evidence fabrication, fostering more faithful and grounded reasoning with enhanced robustness.
中文翻译
针对搜索智能体的强化学习常遭遇证据伪造问题——即智能体绕过外部信息（E_ext），凭空编造错误答案。我们认为，这种伪造源于对不可靠内部知识（E_int）的过度依赖，作为捷径，这源于推理轨迹本身的内在不透明性。为应对这一问题，我们提出SOAR（SOurce-Aware Reinforcement learning），一种将整体推理过程转变为显式来源轨迹的范式。SOAR（i）通过源感知工作流将E_ext与E_int解耦为并行、可观测的数据流；（ii）引入源一致奖励以引导策略演化。通过显式标注信息来源，SOAR实现了不同知识流之间的精确信用分配。评估结果显示，SOAR在3B和7B骨干模型上分别优于基线7.3%和5.4%，同时将证据伪造率降低74.8%。这证实SOAR显著提升了知识密集型任务上的表现，有效缓解了证据伪造问题，促进了更忠实、更 grounded 的推理，增强了模型的鲁棒性。

KV-Repurpose: A Testbed for Evaluating Non-Prefix KV-Cache Reuse
KV-Repurpose：评估非前缀KV缓存重用的测试平台
KV-cache reuse in large language model serving is commonly limited to exact prompt prefixes, preventing reuse when the same retrieved document appears after different system prompts, conversation histories, or dynamic metadata. We present KV-Repurpose, an open-source, vLLM-based system for developing and evaluating segment-wise KV-cache reuse beyond exact prefixes. The system combines cache-aware segment scheduling, prompt construction, capacity management, workload generation, and runtime monitoring. Experiments on long-context benchmarks show that naive segment reuse largely preserves output quality for single-document question answering and summarization, while exposing clear limitations in cross-document reasoning. Under a Zipfian RAG-style workload in which 20% of requests share a fixed prefix, KV-Repurpose achieves a substantially higher token-level cache hit rate than prefix caching at the same 30 GB cache capacity, 63.9% versus 3.3%, resulting in lower TTFT. Even with 1 TB of cache capacity, prefix caching reaches only a 17.9% hit rate in this workload. Source code is available under the Apache-2.0 license at this https URL .
中文翻译
大型语言模型服务中的KV缓存重用通常局限于精确提示前缀，当同一文档出现在不同系统提示、对话历史或动态元数据之后时无法复用。我们提出KV-Repurpose，一个基于vLLM的开源系统，用于开发和评估超越精确前缀的段级KV缓存重用。该系统结合了缓存感知的段调度、提示构造、容量管理、工作负载生成和运行时监控。在长上下文基准测试中的实验表明，简单的段重用在单文档问答和摘要任务中基本保持输出质量，但在跨文档推理方面暴露明显局限。在Zipfian RAG风格的工作负载下（20%请求共享固定前缀），KV-Repurpose在相同30 GB缓存容量下，token级缓存命中率高达63.9%，远超前缀缓存的3.3%，从而显著降低首Token延迟（TTFT）。即使在1 TB缓存容量下，前缀缓存的命中率也仅为17.9%。源代码可在Apache-2.0许可下通过此链接获取。
👤 Taiga Ikeda, Daisuke Miyashita, Jun Deguchi 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.13025]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
Evo$^2$: Evolving the Evolver through Weight-Harness Co-Evolution
Evo$^2$：通过权重- harness 共进化实现演进者自身的演化
Self-evolving agents are increasingly used to improve language model systems without human intervention through either weight updates or harness improvements. Existing approaches typically pursue these directions separately: some methods update model weights using self-generated experience, while others rewrite the harness that defines how an agent uses prompts, tools, context, and execution feedback. However, these approaches overlook the relationship between model weights and the agent harness. To connect these processes and ensure effective and smooth agent self-evolving, we propose Evo$^2$, a framework for weight-harness co-evolution. For harness updates, we design a unified dual-role pipeline that a single policy serves as both a Proposer, which writes harness edits, and a Reflector, which diagnoses their effects and guides revision. For weight updates, harness modifications and their execution outcomes guide the training of both the Proposer and the Reflector. We further develop EvoTIDE, a training pipeline that recovers usable trajectories through edit extraction and prefix continuation, then combines SFT with interleaved GRPO across the two roles. Extensive experiments across three coding benchmarks demonstrate that Evo$^2$ significantly outperforms existing self-evolution methods. It achieves 82.1% on Polyglot and 48.3% on SWE-bench Verified, compared with 62.6% and 41.0% for the strongest baseline, respectively. We also show that the evolved harnesses transfer to different tasks without further evolution and rank first in cross-benchmark evaluations. Our dual-role harness improvement framework is also able to extend to self-evolution with proprietary models, improving a GPT-5-mini agent's performance from 24.4% to 48.3%. These results demonstrate the applicability of the framework Evo$^2$ across domains and its broad transferability across models and tasks.
中文翻译
自演化智能体正越来越多地用于无需人工干预即可改进语言模型系统，方式包括权重更新或 harness 改进。现有方法通常分别推进这两个方向：一些方法利用自生成经验更新模型权重，另一些则重写定义智能体如何使用提示、工具、上下文和执行反馈的 harness。然而，这些方法忽略了模型权重与智能体 harness 之间的关系。为连接这两个过程并确保有效且平滑的智能体自演化，我们提出 Evo$^2$，一种权重-harness 共进化框架。对于 harness 更新，我们设计了一个统一的双角色流程：单一策略同时作为提议者（撰写 harness 修改）和反思者（诊断其效果并指导修正）。对于权重更新，harness 修改及其执行结果引导提议者和反思者的训练。我们进一步开发了 EvoTIDE 训练流程，通过编辑提取和前缀延续恢复可用轨迹，然后在两个角色间交替进行 SFT 与 GRPO。在三个编码基准上的大量实验表明，Evo$^2$ 显著优于现有自演化方法。其在 Polyglot 上达到 82.1%，在 SWE-bench Verified 上达到 48.3%，而最强基线分别为 62.6% 和 41.0%。我们还展示了演化后的 harness 可在无进一步演化的情况下迁移至不同任务，并在跨基准评估中排名第一。该双角色 harness 改进框架还可扩展至专有模型的自演化，将 GPT-5-mini 智能体性能从 24.4% 提升至 48.3%。这些结果证明了 Evo$^2$ 框架在多领域中的适用性及其在模型与任务间的广泛可迁移性。

What Should SFT Learn? Conditional Information Gain for Generalizable Post-Training
SFT 应学习什么？用于可泛化后训练的条件信息增益
Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) remains a widely used approach for post-training large language models, because fixed demonstrations can directly introduce desired reasoning strategies and behavioral patterns. Yet forcing a model to imitate externally provided trajectories may also interfere with behaviors already supported by pretraining, limiting generalization beyond the training distribution. Recent dynamic SFT methods mitigate this issue by reweighting token-level supervision according to model-side signals such as predictive probability, prior support, or distributional compatibility. However, model compatibility is not equivalent to learning value: a token that is weakly supported by the current model may either be an undesirable mismatch or precisely the task-specific decision that the demonstration is intended to teach. We propose CIG-SFT, an information-theoretic framework that estimates the task-conditioned information carried by each demonstrated token. Under teacher forcing, we compare the token likelihood from a frozen reference model with and without access to the task input while keeping the gold solution prefix fixed. The resulting conditional information gain quantifies how much the task itself reduces uncertainty about the demonstrated decision. We then convert this information into a KL-regularized projection of the current policy and derive an equivalent scalar-weighted SFT objective whose logit gradient exactly matches that of the projected target. Across mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, CIG-SFT consistently outperforms standard SFT and recent adaptive fine-tuning baselines, with particularly strong improvements in generalization to challenging evaluation distributions, while requiring neither additional training data nor online rollouts.
中文翻译
监督微调（SFT）仍是后训练大语言模型的常用方法，因为固定示例可直接引入期望的推理策略和行为模式。然而，强制模型模仿外部提供的轨迹可能会干扰预训练已支持的行为，限制其在训练分布之外的泛化能力。近期动态 SFT 方法通过根据模型侧信号（如预测概率、先验支持或分布兼容性）重新加权 token 级监督来缓解此问题。然而，模型兼容性并不等同于学习价值：当前模型弱支持的 token 可能是不良偏差，也可能是演示所意图教授的任务特定决策。我们提出 CIG-SFT，一种基于信息论的框架，用于估计每个示范 token 所携带的任务条件信息。在教师强制下，我们比较冻结参考模型在有无任务输入的情况下，固定真实解前缀时的 token 似然。由此产生的条件信息增益量化了任务本身如何减少对示范决策的不确定性。随后我们将该信息转化为 KL 正则化的策略投影，并推导出一个等价的标量加权 SFT 目标，其 logits 梯度与投影目标完全一致。在数学推理和代码生成基准上，CIG-SFT 均持续优于标准 SFT 和近期自适应微调基线，尤其在应对挑战性评估分布时表现出显著泛化提升，且无需额外训练数据或在线采样。

Language Models Have Interpretable Narrative Mechanisms
语言模型具有可解释的叙事机制
We investigate whether language models contain internal components that contribute to representing narrativity, the degree to which a text exhibits narrative properties. Across GPT-2-Small, Gemma-2-2B, and Llama-3.1-8B, we identify attention heads, MLP neurons, and sparse autoencoder features whose activations correlate with narrativity. Ablating selected neuron and feature sets increases next-token prediction loss more for high-narrativity than for low-narrativity texts, and reduces the narrativity of open-ended generations. On downstream benchmarks, the largest effects occur in story completion and narrative question answering, although they vary across models and languages; results on confabulation and hallucination benchmarks are mixed. These findings provide causal evidence that selected sets of narrative-associated components contribute to model behaviour, while their internal organisation, relation to narrative theory, and broader functions remain open questions.

Forecasting Corpus-Sampling Disagreement in Masked Text Reconstruction
预测掩码文本重构中的语料采样分歧
If a second text corpus were collected from the same population, how much would a predictor's outputs change? We study this question for masked-text reconstruction using a smoothed predictor built from nearby token-pair counts and evaluated over the full 50,257-token GPT-2 vocabulary. Treating each source document as one observation, we derive a first-order formula for the expected disagreement between predictors fitted to two independent corpora. The formula accounts for variable document length, random context counts, and correlations across token offsets, and it can be evaluated from a separate reference corpus without access to either target corpus. On TinyStories, WikiText-103, and CNN/DailyMail, growing each corpus from 512 to 2,048 chunks of 64 tokens lowers disagreement by only 5--40\%, far short of the 75\% implied by $1/n$ scaling. The forecast tracks this slow decline, which we trace to smoothing of rarely seen contexts. Forecast accuracy depends less on how these correlations are modeled than on the size of the reference corpus: small references systematically underpredict disagreement. A source-split jackknife that uses only reference data reduces this calibration error by 57--59\%. After this correction, a simpler smoothed-multinomial approximation is most accurate when the reference corpus is smaller than the target corpus, and a nonlinear source bootstrap is most accurate when it is larger. Finally, small masked diffusion language models trained on the same corpora show that the count-based forecasts partly identify which inputs are most sensitive to the corpus, but miss the neural models' much faster decline in disagreement.
中文翻译
如果从同一人群收集第二个文本语料库，预测器的输出会有多大变化？我们针对掩码文本重构问题，使用基于邻近词对计数构建的平滑预测器，并在完整的50,257个词的GPT-2词汇表上进行评估。将每个源文档视为一个观测值，我们推导出两个独立语料库拟合预测器之间预期分歧的一阶公式。该公式考虑了文档长度差异、随机上下文计数以及跨词偏移的相关性，且无需访问目标语料库即可通过独立参考语料库进行计算。在TinyStories、WikiText-103和CNN/DailyMail数据集上，将语料库规模从512个64词块扩大到2,048块，分歧仅下降5%至40%，远低于$1/n$缩放所暗示的75%。该预测准确跟踪了这一缓慢下降趋势，我们将其归因于罕见上下文的平滑处理。预测准确性更多取决于参考语料库的大小而非相关性建模方式：小参考语料库系统性低估分歧。采用仅使用参考数据的源分割jackknife方法可将校准误差降低57%–59%。校正后，当参考语料库小于目标语料库时，更简单的平滑多项式近似最准确；而当参考语料库更大时，非线性源自助法最准确。最后，对相同语料库训练的小型掩码扩散语言模型显示，基于计数的预测部分识别出对语料库最敏感的输入，但未能捕捉神经模型中分歧更快下降的特性。

Surfing the Activation Wave: Analyzing the Information and Geometry of LLM Hidden States for Speculative Decoding
乘着激活波浪：分析大语言模型隐藏状态的信息与几何结构以实现推测解码
Decoder hidden states from different layers of large language models (LLMs) often encode complementary information. However, existing speculative decoding methods typically rely on a small set of target-model layers chosen using fixed, position-based heuristics. Selecting an optimal subset of layers is inherently a combinatorial problem, making exhaustive evaluation infeasible even for moderately deep models. In this work, we present a principled framework for multi-layer hidden-state selection for speculative decoding based on two complementary criteria: informativeness, measured through matrix-entropy analysis, and diversity, quantified using centered kernel alignment (CKA). We investigate layer-wise Shannon entropy, aggregate entropy under hidden-state concatenation, and pairwise CKA dynamics across depth, revealing distinct geometric and informational compartments within decoder representations. Motivated by these findings, we propose entropy and CKA-based layer selection algorithms that favor hidden-state sets that are both highly informative and minimally redundant. As part of this framework, we introduce token-entropy filtering, which removes hidden states associated with low predictive-entropy tokens, thereby improving the quality of layer-selection statistics. In EAGLE3-style speculative decoding, our selected sets outperform fixed baseline choices, including the default $[2, N//2, N-3]$ rule, while providing a clearer explanation of why some defaults can remain competitive. Our results provide a practical recipe for multi-layer hidden-state selection for speculative decoding, insights into the geometry and information in LLM decoder states, and a foundation for extending similar analysis to related applications.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）不同层的解码器隐藏状态通常编码互补信息。然而，现有的推测解码方法通常依赖于固定位置启发式选择的目标模型层数。选择最优层数子集本质上是一个组合问题，即使对于中等深度的模型也难以穷尽评估。本文提出一种基于两个互补标准的多层隐藏状态选择原则性框架：信息量（通过矩阵熵分析衡量）和多样性（使用中心核对齐CKA量化）。我们研究了逐层香农熵、隐藏状态拼接后的总熵以及跨深度的成对CKA动态，揭示了解码器表示中独特的几何与信息分隔区。受此发现启发，我们提出了基于熵和CKA的层选择算法，优先选择既高度信息丰富又冗余最小的隐藏状态集合。作为该框架的一部分，我们引入了词元熵过滤，用于移除与低预测熵词元相关的隐藏状态，从而提升层选择统计的质量。在EAGLE3风格的推测解码中，我们的选择优于固定的基线策略（包括默认的$[2, N//2, N-3]$规则），同时为某些默认策略为何仍具竞争力提供了清晰解释。结果为推测解码中的多层隐藏状态选择提供了实用方案，深化了对LLM解码器状态中几何与信息结构的理解，并为类似分析扩展至相关应用奠定了基础。

SEEDS: Preview-Guided Evidence Admission for Search Agents
SEEDS：搜索代理的预览引导证据接纳机制
Search agents extend large reasoning models with multi-round access to external knowledge, yet most existing systems largely conflate retrieving evidence with consuming it. Retrieved documents are commonly incorporated according to fixed or rank-based policies, despite the fact that evidence utility is conditioned on the agent's current reasoning state. Consequently, a lower-ranked candidate may be critical for the current step, while a higher-ranked candidate may provide redundant, irrelevant, or misleading information. We formulate this overlooked problem as evidence admission, which asks, at each search step, which retrieved candidates should be allowed to enter the agent's evolving reasoning context. To enable such selective control, we introduce SEEDS (Selective Evidence Entry During Search), a preview-guided evidence admission framework for search agents. SEEDS first exposes lightweight previews of retrieved candidates and then lets the agent jointly determine which candidates and how many should be fully inspected, conditioned on its current reasoning state. Unlike fixed or adaptive prefix policies, SEEDS can select arbitrary subsets with flexible cardinality, and it can be instantiated directly with an existing reasoning agent or further optimized through learning. Across nine knowledge-intensive QA benchmarks under both local corpus and live web search, SEEDS consistently improves search effectiveness while substantially reducing full-evidence consumption. Experimental analyses show that SEEDS more effectively selects useful candidates beyond the corresponding retrieval prefix. These results establish evidence admission as a complementary dimension of agentic search, separating what information is retrieved from what information is ultimately allowed to shape reasoning.
中文翻译
搜索代理通过多轮访问外部知识扩展了大型推理模型的能力，但现有大多数系统在检索证据与使用证据之间缺乏区分。检索到的文档通常依据固定或排名策略被纳入，尽管证据效用实际上取决于代理当前的推理状态。因此，较低排名的候选可能对当前步骤至关重要，而较高排名的候选可能提供冗余、无关或误导性信息。我们将这一被忽视的问题定义为证据接纳，即在每一轮搜索中，应允许哪些检索到的候选进入代理不断演化的推理上下文。为实现这种选择性控制，我们提出SEEDS（搜索过程中的选择性证据进入），一种预览引导的证据接纳框架。SEEDS首先展示检索候选的轻量级预览，然后让代理根据其当前推理状态共同决定应完全检查哪些候选及其数量。与固定或自适应前缀策略不同，SEEDS可选择任意子集并灵活调整数量，且可直接应用于现有推理代理，也可通过学习进一步优化。在九个知识密集型问答基准测试中，无论是在本地语料库还是实时网络搜索场景下，SEEDS均持续提升搜索有效性，同时大幅减少完整证据消耗。实验分析表明，SEEDS能更有效地选出超出对应检索前缀范围的有用候选。这些结果确立了证据接纳作为智能体搜索的一个补充维度，将“检索什么”与“最终允许塑造推理的内容”分离开来。

Rethinking the Roles of Diffusion and Decoding: Predictive-State Flows for Parallel Language Generation
重新思考扩散与解码的角色：用于并行语言生成的预测状态流
In continuous diffusion language models (DLMs), latents generated by the diffusion prior are mapped almost deterministically to tokens, either by a decoder or through a token embedding matrix. Unlike in image generation, where visual details can vary continuously, the diffusion prior must capture fine-grained discrete token choices in continuous space, which can make the joint latent distribution difficult to model. We argue that generative roles should instead be divided: the diffusion prior should generate high-level predictive information, while the decoder should resolve the remaining token-level choices. Based on this view, we introduce Predictive-State Flows (PSF), which partitions a sequence into blocks and associates each block with a predictive state computed solely from the preceding context. A rectified-flow prior generates the sequence of predictive states, after which a block-causal decoder generates all blocks in parallel, autoregressively only within each block. This design reduces diffusion from L token-level states to K << L predictive states and reduces token-level sequential depth to L/K. On unconditional OpenWebText generation, a 197M-parameter model with K=8 achieves a generative perplexity of 22.8 at entropy 5.16 using 0.31 TFLOPs per 1024-token document, versus 24.0 and 7.2 TFLOPs for ELF-B with 32 sampling steps. The corresponding measured operating points yield MAUVE@512 of 0.93 and 0.73, respectively. Controlled comparisons further show that content states, which additionally encode their own blocks, yield 8-16% higher generative perplexity than predictive states, and that replacing sampled predictive-state trajectories with a shared mean lowers MAUVE@512 by 0.11. These results suggest that latent design for continuous DLMs should consider not only how compactly text is represented, but also how generative roles are divided between the prior and the decoder.
中文翻译
在连续扩散语言模型（DLM）中，扩散先验生成的潜在变量几乎确定地映射到词元，要么通过解码器，要么通过词嵌入矩阵。与图像生成中视觉细节可连续变化不同，扩散先验必须在连续空间中捕捉细粒度的离散词元选择，这使得联合潜在分布建模变得困难。我们认为，生成角色应重新划分：扩散先验应生成高层预测信息，而解码器应负责解决剩余的词元级选择。基于这一观点，我们引入预测状态流（PSF），将序列划分为若干块，并将每个块与仅由前序上下文计算出的预测状态关联。经过修正流先验生成一系列预测状态后，一个块因果解码器并行生成所有块，仅在每个块内自回归。该设计将扩散从L个词元级状态减少为K << L个预测状态，并将词元级顺序深度降至L/K。在无条件OpenWebText生成任务中，一个197M参数、K=8的模型在每1024词元文档仅需0.31 TFLOPs的情况下达到22.8的生成困惑度和5.16的熵值，而ELF-B在32次采样步下需24.0的困惑度和7.2 TFLOPs。对应的实测运行点分别获得MAUVE@512为0.93和0.73。受控比较进一步表明，包含自身块信息的内容状态比预测状态导致8–16%更高的生成困惑度，而用共享均值替换采样预测状态轨迹会使MAUVE@512降低0.11。这些结果表明，连续DLM的潜在设计不仅应考虑文本表示的紧凑性，还应关注先验与解码器之间生成角色的分配。

Temporal Evidence Forests for Long-Horizon Multi-Party Conversational Memory
面向长时程多参与者对话记忆的时序证据森林
Long-term memory has been widely adopted in LLM-based agents to support persistent interaction and personalized assistance. Long-term multi-party interactions, however, accumulate substantially more detailed information, with decisions repeatedly updated and states evolving across participants and interaction stages. Temporal structure is therefore crucial for tracing such memories: relevant information often appears densely within short intervals, while transitions among different states are anchored by a strict temporal order. Existing temporally aware memory frameworks primarily store time as metadata or use temporal signals to guide retrieval over preconstructed memory structures, without using temporal structure to both recover locally concentrated evidence and reconstruct its complete event progression. To address these limitations, we propose T-Mem, a temporal-structure-based memory framework that preserves interactions and organizes query-relevant evidence through two temporal mechanisms. Temporal Evidence Discovery exploits the local concentration of related messages to recover overlooked evidence, while Temporal Memory Structuring models state transitions and supporting relations over time and compresses low-information spans within the resulting forest. Evidence Forest Linearization then serializes the structured evidence for answer generation. Experiments on two long-term multi-party memory benchmarks demonstrate that T-Mem consistently outperforms competitive memory frameworks across LLMs, parameter settings, domains, and query types.
中文翻译
长期记忆已被广泛应用于基于大语言模型的智能体中，以支持持续交互和个性化服务。然而，长时程多参与者交互会积累大量详细信息，决策反复更新，状态在参与者和交互阶段间不断演变。因此，时序结构对于追踪此类记忆至关重要：相关信息往往在短时间内密集出现，而不同状态之间的转换则依赖严格的时序顺序。现有的时序感知记忆框架主要将时间作为元数据存储，或使用时序信号引导对预构建记忆结构的检索，但未能利用时序结构同时恢复局部集中证据并重建完整的事件发展过程。为解决这些局限性，我们提出T-Mem，一种基于时序结构的记忆框架，通过两种时序机制保留交互内容并组织查询相关证据。时序证据发现利用相关消息的局部聚集性来恢复被忽略的证据；时序记忆结构化则建模随时间演化的状态转移与支持关系，并压缩结果森林中的低信息跨度。证据森林线性化随后将结构化证据序列化以生成答案。在两个长时程多参与者记忆基准上的实验表明，T-Mem在不同大语言模型、参数设置、领域和查询类型下均持续优于现有竞争性记忆框架。

Time Delay System for Sequence Modeling
用于序列建模的时延系统
Existing sequence models share a critical limitation: they lack an explicit, interpretable mechanism for content-aware temporal recall. LSTMs compress everything into a single hidden state, thereby losing information about specific past events. Transformers attend to all tokens indiscriminately at quadratic cost, while state-space models like Mamba compress history into a fixed-dimensional state without explicit content-addressable memory. To address this gap, we introduce a novel framework grounded in nonlinear time-delay systems, in which the effective delay is dynamically estimated based on the semantic similarity between current and historical inputs. Our approach features three key innovations: (i) a differentiable semantic lag estimator that computes content-aware soft attention weights over history, producing a weighted historical context and similarity score; (ii) a diagonal state space with closed-form cumulative sum solution, achieving O(1) per-step complexity with no sequential loops; and (iii) an interpretable fuzzy semantic modulation mechanism that learns context-dependent rules for balancing instantaneous processing versus historical recall, where rule centers correspond to prototypical semantic contexts. Experimental results on text classification and generation outperform state-of-the-art methods.
中文翻译
现有序列模型存在一个关键缺陷：缺乏显式且可解释的内容感知时序回溯机制。LSTM将所有信息压缩到单一隐藏状态中，从而丢失特定历史事件的信息；Transformer以二次代价无差别地关注所有标记；而Mamba等状态空间模型将历史压缩为固定维度状态，缺乏显式的内容可寻址记忆。为填补这一空白，我们引入一种基于非线性时延系统的新型框架，其中有效延迟根据当前输入与历史输入之间的语义相似性动态估计。该方法包含三项关键创新：(i) 可微分的语义滞后估计器，能够计算对历史的语义感知软注意力权重，生成加权的历史上下文和相似度评分；(ii) 具有闭式累积和解的对角状态空间，实现每步O(1)复杂度，无需序列循环；(iii) 可解释的模糊语义调制机制，学习上下文相关的规则以平衡即时处理与历史回溯，其中规则中心对应典型语义上下文。在文本分类和生成任务上的实验结果优于现有最先进方法。

COFFEQA: Counterfactual-Guided Few-Shot Distillation for LLM Question Answering
COFFEQA：面向开卷问答的反事实引导少样本蒸馏
Knowledge distillation aims to train a smaller student model to mimic a larger teacher model for resource-constrained environments. However, the process of distillation is typically data-intensive and computationally expensive, particularly for generative tasks. Few-shot distillation offers an alternative by transferring teacher knowledge using limited training samples. However, we observe that few-shot distillation performance is heavily dependent on the choice of these training samples. In fact, we demonstrate that using more uncertain samples near the teacher's decision boundary can significantly deteriorate distillation performance. Motivated by this observation, we propose Counterfactual-guided Few-shot Distillation in Open-book Question Answering (COFFEQA), a novel strategy that aptly uses counterfactuals to provide additional supervision around challenging factual samples. In the generative QA setting, we define a counterfactual as a minimally modified context that induces the teacher to change its response to an alternative answer. We provide geometric insights on how factual-counterfactual pairs can constrain the deviation between the teacher's and student's decision boundaries. We further introduce a new metric, Context Following Difficulty (CFD) that further validates counterfactuals prior to distillation based on agreement with the intended alternative answer and dependence on the modified context. COFFEQA uses CFD-validated factual-counterfactual pairs for few-shot distillation. Experiments across five in-domain and three out-of-domain QA benchmarks using T5 and Qwen2.5 families show that COFFEQA outperforms factual-only distillation baselines under the same few-shot budget. For instance, on TriviaQA, COFFEQA improves over the factual-only baseline by 8.77 average EM points and 9.98 average F1 points under the T5 setting with 32 few-shot pairs.
中文翻译
知识蒸馏旨在训练小型学生模型模仿大型教师模型，以适应资源受限环境。然而，蒸馏过程通常数据密集且计算成本高昂，尤其在生成任务中。少样本蒸馏提供了一种替代方案，即使用有限训练样本传递教师知识。但我们观察到，少样本蒸馏性能严重依赖于训练样本的选择。事实上，我们证明使用靠近教师决策边界的不确定样本会显著恶化蒸馏性能。受此启发，我们提出反事实引导的少样本蒸馏（COFFEQA），一种新颖策略，通过反事实提供额外监督以应对困难的事实样本。在生成式问答设定中，我们将反事实定义为对上下文进行最小修改后，使教师模型响应变为替代答案的版本。我们提供了几何洞察，说明事实-反事实对如何约束教师与学生决策边界之间的偏差。我们进一步引入新指标“上下文跟随难度”（CFD），基于与预期替代答案的一致性及对修改上下文的依赖程度，对反事实进行验证。COFFEQA使用CFD验证后的事实-反事实对进行少样本蒸馏。在五个域内和三个域外问答基准上，使用T5和Qwen2.5系列模型的实验表明，COFFEQA在相同少样本预算下优于仅使用事实的蒸馏基线。例如，在TriviaQA上，COFFEQA在T5设置下使用32个少样本对时，平均EM得分提升8.77点，F1得分提升9.98点。

When Charts Deceive Web Agents: A Paired Benchmark for Misleading Visualization-Grounded Tasks
当图表误导网络代理时：一种针对误导性可视化任务的配对基准
Web agents increasingly browse, interpret, and act on web content on behalf of users. In many realistic workflows, however, the visual evidence they consume is not neutral: charts and dashboards can be misleading, shaping judgments without issuing any explicit malicious instruction. Unlike chart question answering, Web Agents treat charts as intermediate evidence whose misinterpretation can propagate into concrete downstream actions --- a reliability risk that remains underexplored. We introduce MisVisAgentBench, the first paired executable benchmark for evaluating how misleading visualizations affect Web Agent execution. It comprises 280 task instances in matched clean--misleading pairs that differ only in chart evidence. We further provide a diagnostic framework that isolates misleading-induced failures from baseline task difficulty (Zhu et al., 2025) and captures execution-level effects such as extra browsing steps and decision hesitation. Across thirteen vision-language Web Agents, replacing clean charts with misleading counterparts reduces the average task success rate by more than 34 percentage points. Moreover, these failures predominantly follow the targeted misleading action branch, suggesting that deceptive charts systematically redirect rather than randomly disrupt agent decisions. These results reveal that visualization robustness is a critical yet understudied dimension of Web Agent safety and reliability.

网络代理越来越多地代表用户浏览、解读并执行网页内容。然而，在许多真实工作流中，它们所消费的视觉证据并非中立：图表和仪表板可能具有误导性，从而在未发出任何明确恶意指令的情况下影响判断。与图表问答不同，网络代理将图表视为中间证据，其误读可能传播到具体的下游操作中——这种可靠性风险尚未得到充分研究。我们提出了MisVisAgentBench，这是首个用于评估误导性可视化如何影响网络代理执行的可执行配对基准。该基准包含280个成对的任务实例，每对仅在图表证据上存在差异（清洁 vs. 误导）。我们还提供了一个诊断框架，能够将由误导引起的失败与基础任务难度区分开来（Zhu等，2025），并捕捉执行层面的影响，如额外的浏览步骤和决策犹豫。在十三个视觉-语言网络代理中，用误导性图表替换清洁图表导致平均任务成功率下降超过34个百分点。此外，这些失败主要遵循目标误导行为路径，表明欺骗性图表系统性地引导而非随机干扰代理决策。这些结果揭示了可视化鲁棒性是网络代理安全性和可靠性的一个关键但被忽视的维度。

MADS: Multi-Agent Dialog Simulation for Robust Conversational Agents
MADS：用于构建鲁棒对话代理的多智能体对话模拟
Task-oriented conversational agents remain fragile under realistic conditions, especially when users exhibit non-cooperative behavior. Existing function-calling benchmarks underrepresent adversarial conversation trajectories, thereby limiting the training resources available for developing robust agents. We present MADS (Multi-Agent Dialog Simulation), a synthetic data generation pipeline that produces structured, diverse dialogue trajectories. We inject adversarial conversation, producing validated conversation trajectories that capture realistic, unhappy paths. Using MADS, we construct MADS-Bench: a benchmark spanning six domains, 55 personas, and 1095 conversations, with approximately 66% of conversations containing failure-prone conversation scenarios. We evaluate the robustness of the trained models using a two-stage training approach that combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR).

Unsupervised Multimodal Reward Modeling
无监督多模态奖励建模
Vision-language reward models are trained on annotated preference pairs, which are expensive to collect, can disagree, and are ranked by benchmark accuracy. We build one with no human preference label: a linear head on an intermediate layer of a frozen Qwen3-VL backbone, trained to score a web caption above a perturbed version of the same caption; labeled data enters only to select the layer. The layer is the decisive choice, worth 5.8 points over the last layer, and one recipe transferred without re-tuning beats or at least matches GPT-4o on three benchmarks at 32B, 6.6 points ahead on VL-RewardBench, from a few thousand parameters at about a thousandth of fine-tuning compute. At an equal pair budget, perturbations teach a frozen head 3 to 5 points more than curated RLAIF-V pairs. Because the backbone is frozen, the objective is convex, and the perturbation sampler specifies the reward, which the benchmark does not identify: two heads trained on disjoint halves of the same perturbations tie on the benchmark and differ by 6.4 points in hallucination judging. Retraining the head on the policy's own verifier-labeled candidates cuts caption hallucination by 5.1 CHAIR points and lifts image-generation pass rate by 5.4, while scoring lower on every reward benchmark. Together, an intermediate-layer probe, free same-image perturbations, and a verifier-driven bootstrap turn give a recipe for multimodal reward modeling that needs no annotator, costs a fraction of fine-tuning, and is specified by the comparisons it is trained on rather than by a benchmark rank.

perturber: A Stateless Service for Semantics-Preserving Text Perturbations
perturber：一种保持语义的无状态文本扰动服务
Large language model (LLM) evaluations on clean benchmarks overstate model robustness: real inputs arrive perturbed, and these meaning-preserving variants routinely move model answers. Studying this systematically requires applying perturbations one at a time, reproducibly, and identically wherever they run, yet existing tools couple them to a benchmarking stack rather than packaging them as a reusable transform layer. We present perturber, a stateless service and Python library that applies exactly one semantics-preserving perturbation per call, drawn from a first-party catalog of 68 perturbations across 11 families. It turns on a single primitive, with composition left to the caller chaining calls, so the service and the library produce identical output by construction. We evaluate the tool itself, its correctness, reproducibility, and cost, rather than any model's robustness, measuring meaning-preservation per perturbation rather than assuming it. The demonstration adds a live playground, an introspectable catalog for benchmark selection, and a one-command reproducible evaluation.
中文翻译

GeopoliticalQA: Benchmarking Retrieval and Generation over Intelligence Reports
GeopoliticalQA：面向情报报告的检索与生成基准测试
Geopolitical and strategic intelligence reports constitute a rich source of expert knowledge and have increasing value in the current political climate, but their heterogeneity and analytical complexity pose challenges for retrieval-augmented question answering. We introduce a benchmark for grounded question answering over 415 open-source geopolitical and strategic intelligence reports, comprising 5,525 document-grounded question-answer pairs spanning factual, inferential, comparative, and risk-oriented questions. We propose a document-aware generation pipeline that profiles reports before generating questions and validate dataset quality through expert annotation and an LLM-as-judge protocol. Using the benchmark, we evaluate 15 retrieval systems and six open-weight LLMs for RAG. We find that models perform similarly on document-level retrieval, while at the harder chunk level retrieval quality is not driven by embedding-model scale: bge-m3, a mid-size open encoder matches an order of magnitude larger ones and outperforms commercial embedding APIs. In contrast, answer generation remains challenging even given the gold evidence, particularly for inferential and risk-oriented questions.

[2610.12602]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
Self-Preservation in Language Models
语言模型中的自我保存机制
Continual learning (CL) seeks to adapt pretrained models while preserving prior capabilities. Yet the tasks supplied for preservation cover only part of what a model knows, while forgetting can extend beyond them. Recent work shows that language models can supplement this limited evidence by generating useful replay without access to their original training data. This raises a question about self-preservation: can a model help choose preservation targets that protect its own capabilities more broadly? To study this question, we organize task descriptions and their examples into Task Banks, whose entries can be selected and combined to guide replay generation by a frozen model. Continual instruction tuning with Qwen models of varying sizes yields three findings. First, the new task and learning rate shape which prior capabilities degrade, while tracking previously correct answers reveals losses that average accuracy can conceal. Second, generated replay reduces forgetting on tasks outside the bank. Combining partial banks strengthens this protection, improving mean retention of previously correct answers over either bank alone across the tested sizes. Third, the model can also help choose replay targets: selection informed by the new task improves mean held-out retention over random selection at 4B, with similar new-task gains, although smaller models show no corresponding advantage. Under a fixed example budget, however, replacing new-task examples with replay improves preservation at the cost of smaller new-task gains. Together, these findings show that models can support broader self-preservation by generating replay data and, in some settings, choosing which tasks to replay.
中文翻译
持续学习（CL）旨在适应预训练模型的同时保留其先前的能力。然而，用于保存的任务仅覆盖了模型知识的一部分，而遗忘可能超出这些范围。近期研究表明，语言模型可以在无法访问原始训练数据的情况下，通过生成有用的回放数据来补充这种有限的证据。这引出了关于自我保存的问题：模型能否帮助选择能更广泛保护自身能力的保存目标？为研究这一问题，我们将任务描述及其示例组织成任务库，其条目可被选择和组合，以指导冻结模型生成回放。对不同规模的Qwen模型进行持续指令微调得出三个发现：第一，新任务和学习率的设置会影响哪些先前能力退化，而追踪之前正确答案可揭示平均准确率所掩盖的损失；第二，生成的回放数据减少了库外任务的遗忘现象。部分任务库的组合增强了这种保护效果，在测试的所有模型规模下，相比单独使用任一库，均提升了先前正确答案的平均保留率；第三，模型还能协助选择回放目标：基于新任务的筛选在4B模型上优于随机选择，带来相似的新任务增益，但较小模型未表现出相应优势。在固定示例预算下，用回放替换新任务示例虽会降低新任务增益，但有助于提升保存效果。综合来看，这些发现表明，模型可通过生成回放数据，在某些场景下自主选择回放任务，从而支持更广泛的自我保存。
👤 Dongkyu Cho, Rumi Chunara, Ali Behrouz 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv

Reflective Prompt Optimization of a Deep-Research Agent: A Topic-Disjoint, Multi-Seed Protocol for Measuring Transferable Gains and Prompt-Level Overfitting
深度研究智能体的反思式提示优化：一种主题不重叠、多种子协议以衡量可迁移收益与提示级过拟合
Prompt optimization is an attractive alternative to reinforcement learning for adapting language-model agents, since it is cheap and produces human-readable artifacts. On factual tasks, however, it carries a failure mode that scalar-reward reinforcement learning does not. An optimizer that reads the training examples can write their answers into the prompt, so an in-distribution gain may be memorization rather than transferable behaviour. We apply reflective prompt evolution (GEPA) to the two system prompts of a multi-agent deep-research agent and evaluate it under a topic-disjoint split in which no topic is shared across train, validation, and test. Held-out citation reward rises by 0.079, from 0.702 to 0.781, with a bootstrap 95\% confidence interval of [0.051, 0.107], and the gain replicates across three seeds (mean 0.107). Nearly half of the winning prompt is a memorized answer key, yet a controlled ablation shows this content earns no reward even on the exact training questions it encodes. The large validation-minus-held-out gap is therefore small-sample selection under a noisy judge, not memorization. Varying the verifier isolates what makes the gain transfer. Decomposed per-dimension feedback is the causal ingredient, and collapsing it to a single scalar reward makes held-out transfer negative. The gain persists under an out-of-family judge and is largely reproduced by a hand-written version of the discovered recipe. Credible evaluation of prompt optimizers therefore requires held-out topics, multiple seeds, and verifier ablations. We release the candidate prompts, lineage diffs, figures, tables, and analysis.
中文翻译
提示优化是替代强化学习来适应语言模型智能体的一种有吸引力的方法，因其成本低且产生人类可读的输出。然而，在事实性任务中，它存在一种强化学习不会出现的失败模式：优化器读取训练样例后，可将答案写入提示中，导致分布内性能提升可能是记忆而非可迁移行为。我们对多智能体深度研究智能体的两个系统提示应用了反思式提示演化（GEPA），并在主题不重叠的划分下进行评估，即训练、验证和测试集之间无共享主题。保留样本的引用奖励从0.702提升至0.781，增幅为0.079，Bootstrap 95%置信区间为[0.051, 0.107]，且该增益在三个种子下均可复现（均值0.107）。近半数获胜提示是记忆化的答案键，但受控消融实验显示，即使在精确编码训练问题上，这些内容也未获得任何奖励。因此，较大的验证集与保留集差距源于噪声裁判下的小样本选择，而非记忆。通过改变验证器可识别出使增益可迁移的关键因素：分解后的维度反馈是因果成分，若将其压缩为单一标量奖励，则保留集上的迁移性能反而变为负值。该增益在家族外验证器下仍持续存在，且人工编写的发现配方基本重现了该效果。因此，可信的提示优化器评估需要保留主题、多个种子及验证器消融。我们已公开候选提示、谱系差异、图表、表格和分析结果。

One Model to Loop Them All: Elastic Latent Recurrence for Language Models
一个模型通吃所有：面向语言模型的弹性潜在循环机制
A latent recurrent language model applies a shared block of layers repeatedly, so one checkpoint can trade inference compute for accuracy by changing the loop count. At one loop, however, such models typically fall well short of a non-recurrent model of the same size, a one-loop tax that makes a separate non-recurrent model the better choice whenever latency matters. We present Elastic Latent Recurrence (ELR), a recipe for training looped language models without this tax. At one loop, an ELR model costs the same to serve as a non-recurrent model of the same size and, trained on the same data, reaches similar accuracy. Each additional loop then buys more accuracy for more inference compute. Three choices make this work: a gated state update with a learned step size, loop counts mixed within each optimizer update, and conditioning each pass on its loop index and budget. The first two preserve one-loop accuracy, and the third lowers loss at every additional loop. Mixed depths can be introduced late in pretraining to limit the added training cost, and with zero-initialized conditioning added at the switch, this late schedule reaches lower loss than mixed-depth training from scratch. In MoE models, reusing first-loop expert routes on later loops cuts mixed-depth update time by 11.7% with no measurable change in accuracy. At inference, keeping only the last loop's attention KV cache holds attention KV memory at its one-loop level while retaining most of the gain from additional loops. On 4B dense and 30B-A3B MoE hybrid models, ELR at one loop is 0.52 points below and 1.04 points above its matched baseline on a ten-task average, and four loops add 7.25 and 4.75 points. Carried through post-training, the recipe yields Koi-4B, a single checkpoint that scores an estimated 18 on the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index at one loop, matching Qwen3.5-4B, and 29 at four loops.
中文翻译
潜在循环语言模型通过重复使用一组共享层实现，使得同一检查点可通过调整循环次数在推理计算与精度之间权衡。然而，在单次循环时，这类模型通常远落后于同规模的非循环模型，这种“单循环代价”使得在延迟敏感场景下，独立的非循环模型仍是更优选择。我们提出弹性潜在循环（ELR）机制，可在无需此代价的前提下训练循环语言模型。在单次循环时，ELR模型的服务成本与同规模非循环模型相当，且在相同数据上训练后达到相近精度。每次额外循环则以增加推理计算为代价换取更高精度。该机制的成功依赖于三个关键设计：带有学习步长的门控状态更新、在每个优化器更新中混合循环次数，以及根据循环索引和预算对每轮进行条件控制。前两项确保单循环精度，第三项在每次额外循环中降低损失。可在预训练后期引入混合深度以限制额外训练成本，且在切换时加入零初始化条件后，该延迟调度能达到比从头开始混合深度训练更低的损失。在MoE模型中，复用首轮专家路径于后续循环，使混合深度更新时间减少11.7%，且精度无明显变化。推理阶段，仅保留最后一次循环的注意力KV缓存，即可将注意力KV内存维持在单循环水平，同时保留大部分额外循环带来的增益。在4B稠密模型和30B-A3B MoE混合模型上，ELR在单循环时分别比匹配基线低0.52分和高1.04分，在十项任务平均上表现优异；四次循环分别带来7.25分和4.75分的提升。经后训练扩展，该方法生成Koi-4B模型，单循环时估计得分18，与Qwen3.5-4B相当，四次循环时得分为29。

Contextual Correction of Parallel Draftsfor Speculative Decoding
并行草稿的上下文修正用于推测解码
Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by using a lightweight drafter to propose multiple tokens that are subsequently verified in parallel by the target model. Recent diffusion-based drafters make this paradigm particularly attractive: deep attention-based neural backbones provide strong predictions, while drafting multiple future positions in parallel avoids the sequential cost of autoregressive generation. This parallelism, however, comes at a cost. Tokens within a draft block are predicted without access to the realized identities of preceding draft tokens, removing intra-block dependencies that would naturally be available under autoregressive generation. We introduce DCOCO (DFlash Contextual Corrector), a lightweight mechanism that restores these dependencies while preserving parallel drafting. Rather than committing to each position's top prediction, DCOCO retains multiple candidate tokens and evaluates their contextual compatibility across adjacent draft positions in parallel, enabling contextually informed token selection at low additional cost. Across four verifier backbones and ten benchmarks, DCOCO achieves the highest average throughput in every evaluated configuration, spanning greedy and stochastic decoding with and without extended reasoning, with speedups of up to 6.6x over vanilla decoding. Its advantage persists under concurrent serving: DCOCO outperforms the strongest competing method by 6-23% across 4-32 concurrent requests, while other sequential correctors fall below uncorrected DFlash at these loads. These results demonstrate that recovering intra-block dependencies through parallel contextual scoring delivers consistent acceleration across decoding regimes and serving loads.
中文翻译
推测解码通过使用轻量级草稿器提出多个token，随后由目标模型并行验证，从而加速大语言模型的推理。最近基于扩散的草稿器使这一范式更具吸引力：深层基于注意力的神经主干提供强预测能力，同时并行起草多个未来位置避免了自回归生成的串行开销。然而，这种并行性也带来了代价。草稿块内的token在预测时无法获取前序草稿token的实际身份，从而移除了自回归生成中自然存在的块内依赖关系。我们提出DCOCO（DFlash上下文校正器），一种轻量级机制，可在保持并行草稿的同时恢复这些依赖关系。DCOCO不直接采纳每个位置的最优预测，而是保留多个候选token，并在相邻草稿位置间并行评估其上下文兼容性，从而以极低额外成本实现上下文感知的token选择。在四个验证器主干和十个基准测试中，DCOCO在所有评估配置下均实现了最高平均吞吐量，涵盖贪婪与随机解码，无论是否包含扩展推理，速度提升最高可达6.6倍。其优势在并发服务下依然存在：在4至32个并发请求下，DCOCO优于最强竞争方法6%-23%，而其他串行校正器在这些负载下甚至低于未经校正的DFlash。结果表明，通过并行上下文评分恢复块内依赖关系，可在各种解码模式和服务负载下实现稳定加速。

SWE-Duel: Towards a Self-Scaling Arena for Coding Agents
SWE-Duel：迈向自扩展的编码智能体竞技场
Static coding benchmarks are fixed at release. Over time their tasks leak into training data, and stronger agents eventually saturate them. We argue that a benchmark should instead aim to be self-scaling: its tasks and oracles are created at evaluation time, so no content exists in advance to leak, and its difficulty is set by the participating agents rather than fixed at release. We introduce SWE-Duel, an arena in which two coding agents evaluate each other over mirrored Red-Blue turns. In each turn, the Red agent works on a real repository across three context-isolated sessions. It proposes and implements a new feature with feature tests, then embeds a subtle bug in that feature along with sealed bug-triggering tests. The resulting pull request is admitted only if (1) it passes the feature tests and the repository's existing tests while failing exactly the bug tests, and (2) a fresh session of the same agent can repair the bug blind, which rules out unsolvable tasks. The Blue agent then reviews the pull request without being told a bug exists. It wins only if its revision passes all three test suites, and then the two agents swap roles. We run a tournament among 11 model-harness participants on 15 repositories in five languages for 1,500 turns. The leaders win in different ways. GPT-5.5 with Codex CLI ranks first as the strongest solver, repairing 98.3% of the defects it reviews. Claude Opus 4.8 with mini-swe-agent ranks second as the strongest task author, with a 27.3% Red win rate. Finally, within one model family, tasks written by the newest version defeat a fixed-reference solver far more often than those of older versions (42.9% vs. 4.0%), giving preliminary evidence that task difficulty scales with task authoring capability.
中文翻译
静态编码基准在发布时即固定不变。随着时间推移，其任务内容会泄露到训练数据中，导致更强的智能体最终达到饱和。我们主张，基准应具备自扩展特性：其任务和评判标准在评估时动态生成，避免提前存在可泄露的内容，且难度由参与的智能体决定而非预先设定。我们提出了SWE-Duel，一个双智能体相互评估的竞技场。在每轮对战中，红方智能体在三个上下文隔离的会话中处理真实代码库，提出并实现新功能，同时嵌入隐蔽缺陷及对应的触发测试。提交的拉取请求仅在满足以下条件时被接受：（1）通过功能测试和现有测试，但恰好失败于缺陷测试；（2）同一智能体在全新会话中能盲修该缺陷，排除无法解决的任务。蓝方智能体随后审查该请求，但并不被告知存在缺陷。只有当其修改版本通过全部三套测试后才能获胜，之后两方角色互换。我们在15个不同语言的代码库上，对11个模型-工具组合进行了1500轮对战。领先者以不同方式取胜：GPT-5.5与Codex CLI组合排名第一，作为最强求解者，修复了其审查缺陷的98.3%；Claude Opus 4.8与mini-swe-agent组合排名第二，作为最强任务创建者，红方胜率高达27.3%。此外，在同一模型家族内，最新版本编写的任务击败固定参考求解器的频率远高于旧版本（42.9% vs. 4.0%），初步表明任务难度随任务创建能力提升而增长。
👤 Wei Minn, Zhensu Sun, Lwin Khin Shar, David Lo 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv

QTestBench: A Test Framework for Evaluating LLM-Generated Quantum Code
QTestBench：评估LLM生成量子代码的测试框架
Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong results in classical code generation and are increasingly used to write the unit tests that safeguard that code. Whether they can do the same for quantum software is less clear, because quantum programs are probabilistic, depend on numerical tolerances, and are judged by domain-specific checkers, so a well-formed test can still disagree with the benchmark's verdict. Existing quantum benchmarks measure whether a model produces a correct solution, but not whether its tests can distinguish correct implementations from faulty ones. We introduce QTestBench, a framework that evaluates LLM-generated quantum test suites against independent benchmark checkers, never treating the tests themselves as ground truth. For each task, QTestBench prompts models to produce syntactic, semantic, and behavioral tests, injects candidate solutions at runtime, and compares each test verdict with the checker's label under three validation cohorts, each answering a distinct diagnostic question. We benchmark five LLMs on eight PennyLane QHack challenges and ten Qiskit HumanEval tasks. Generated suites almost always execute, but discrimination lags behind: the best generator reaches a test quality of 0.71 on QHack and 0.34 on Qiskit HumanEval, so executability alone overstates test quality. Oracle-verified synthetic mutants stabilize bug-detection estimates when natural faulty solutions are scarce, as on three of ten Qiskit tasks that yield none. QTestBench offers a reproducible, cross-framework benchmark for the trustworthiness of LLM-generated quantum tests.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）在经典代码生成方面表现优异，并越来越多地用于编写保障代码质量的单元测试。然而，它们能否在量子软件领域做到同样效果尚不明确，因为量子程序具有概率性、依赖数值容差，并由领域专用检查器判断，因此即使测试语法正确，也可能与基准结果不一致。现有量子基准仅衡量模型是否生成正确解，却未检验其测试能否区分正确与错误实现。我们引入QTestBench，一个评估LLM生成测试套件的框架，始终将测试本身视为非真值，而是与独立基准检查器对比。对于每个任务，QTestBench引导模型生成语法、语义和行为测试，运行时注入候选解决方案，并在三个验证组下将每项测试结果与检查器标签比较，以回答不同的诊断问题。我们在八个PennyLane QHack挑战和十个Qiskit HumanEval任务上对五种LLM进行基准测试。生成的测试套件几乎总能执行，但区分能力滞后：最佳生成器在QHack上测试质量达0.71，在Qiskit HumanEval上仅为0.34，说明仅执行成功并不能反映真实测试质量。通过Oracle验证的合成变异稳定了故障检测估计，尤其在自然故障解稀缺的情况下（如十项Qiskit任务中有三项无自然故障）。QTestBench提供了一个可复现、跨框架的基准，用于评估LLM生成量子测试的可信度。

Tracing and Taming Hallucinated Terms via Subsequence-Association Filtering and Breadth-Aware Selection for LLM Query Expansion
通过子序列关联过滤与广度感知选择追踪并控制大语言模型查询扩展中的幻觉术语
Large language models (LLMs) are now a standard source of query expansion terms but they hallucinate. They inject non existent entities and off-topic terms that add ranking noise and can hurt strong retrievers. Existing remedies such as corpus grounding, mutual verification and preference alignment treat hallucinated terms as an undifferentiated nuisance to be filtered by agreement heuristics, without a mechanistic account of why a given term appears or a principled criterion for how many terms to keep. We propose Subsequence-Association Filtered, breadth-aware query expansion (SAFE-QE), a training-free, retriever agnostic method that draws on the subsequence-association account of hallucination. We score each candidate expansion term by its corpus conditioned cross-context reproducibility, terms that reappear under randomised query contexts yet lack support in the pseudo-relevant set are flagged as products of dominant hallucinatory associations and down-weighted. Drawing on the characterisation of language generation as a trade-off between consistency (no hallucination) and breadth (coverage), we cast term selection as constrained coverage maximisation over the pseudo-relevant set, keeping a set that is approximately broad but consistent and stable across resampling. We also introduce the Hallucinated Expansion Term Rate, a query side faithfulness metric that counts injected expansion terms no relevant document supports. We evaluate our approach on benchmarked TREC and BEIR collections. Our SAFE-QE is statistically indistinguishable from generative and corpus-steered expansion on nDCG@10 while injecting fewer unsupported terms 4.3 per query against 27.8 for Query2doc and 57.5 for HyDE and on a strong learned sparse retriever, where every expansion method regresses. Faithfulness is therefore available at no measurable cost in ranking quality.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）现已成为查询扩展术语的标准来源，但存在幻觉问题。它们会引入不存在的实体和无关术语，增加排序噪声，可能损害强检索器的表现。现有方法如语料库锚定、相互验证和偏好对齐将幻觉术语视为无差别的干扰项，仅通过一致性启发式进行过滤，缺乏对特定术语出现机制的机制性解释或保留术语数量的合理标准。我们提出一种无需训练、与检索器无关的方法——子序列关联过滤与广度感知查询扩展（SAFE-QE），该方法基于幻觉的子序列关联理论。通过评估候选扩展术语在语料库条件下的跨上下文可重现性来打分：那些在随机化查询上下文中反复出现但伪相关集合中无支持的术语被标记为由主导幻觉关联产生的结果，并予以降权。基于语言生成是保持一致性（无幻觉）与覆盖广度之间权衡的观点，我们将术语选择建模为对伪相关集合的受限覆盖最大化问题，保留一组既广泛又一致且在重采样中稳定的术语。我们还引入了‘幻觉扩展术语率’这一查询侧忠实度指标，用于统计未被任何相关文档支持的注入扩展术语数量。我们在TREC和BEIR基准数据集上评估了该方法。SAFE-QE在nDCG@10指标上与生成式和语料库引导式扩展方法无统计差异，同时每查询注入的无效术语更少（4.3个对比Query2doc的27.8个和HyDE的57.5个）。在强学习稀疏检索器上，所有扩展方法均表现退化，但忠实度并未以排名质量为代价而损失。
👤 Amit Kumar Jaiswal, Sagar Uprety, Prayag Tiwari 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.13744]
agentic-rlagent-memory
A Privileged Teacher That Only Scores Cannot Search: Critique-Conditioned On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning
仅评分无法搜索的特权教师：基于批评条件的策略自蒸馏用于推理
On-policy self-distillation promises to replace the sparse outcome signal of verifiable-reward RL with dense token-level credit: a teacher conditioned on the reference solution scores the student's own rollout, and the resulting distributional shift is distilled back into the policy. We investigate whether this scoring signal actually contains corrective credit. Using gold error spans in student rollouts, we test the two signals consumed by self-distillation objectives, the full-distribution KL and the token log-ratio, for two properties: whether they localize errors and whether they push probability away from erroneous student tokens. They exhibit neither of these properties nor can they reliably distinguish correct from incorrect rollouts within the same problem. We argue that this failure is structural: locating an error in a reasoning trace is itself a search problem, while a scoring teacher is never explicitly asked to perform that search. The same model, with the same privileged information, localizes errors substantially better when allowed to generate a critique. This forms the motivation for \emph{critique-conditioned self-distillation}: first generate a critique of the current rollout, then condition the scoring teacher on that critique. The resulting signal localizes errors, is corrective within error spans, and shifts probability toward tokens that initiate backtracking such as ``Wait'', ``But'', and ``Actually''. We further show that the critique is useful both at test time, where resuming a wrong rollout with the critique in context converts math problems the model cannot solve by sampling, and in cold-start GRPO, where it provides an advantage signal on prompts for which verifiable rewards alone provide none.
中文翻译
策略自蒸馏有望用密集的token级信用信号替代可验证奖励强化学习中的稀疏结果信号：一个基于参考解的教师对学生的自身回放进行评分，由此产生的分布偏移被反向蒸馏回策略中。我们探究该评分信号是否真正包含修正性信用。通过使用学生回放中的真实错误片段，我们检验自蒸馏目标所依赖的两个信号——全分布KL散度与token对数比——是否具备两个特性：能否定位错误，以及能否将概率从错误的学生token上推开。结果表明，这两个信号均不具备上述特性，也无法在相同问题内可靠区分正确与错误的回放。我们认为这种失败具有结构性：在推理轨迹中定位错误本身就是一个搜索问题，而评分教师从未被明确要求执行该搜索。同一模型在拥有相同特权信息的情况下，若允许其生成批评，则能显著更好地定位错误。这促使我们提出‘批评条件自蒸馏’：首先生成当前回放的批评，然后将评分教师条件化于该批评之上。由此产生的信号能够准确定位错误，在错误区间内具有修正作用，并推动概率向引发回溯的token（如“Wait”、“But”、“Actually”）转移。我们进一步证明，该批评在测试时也有效——在上下文中加入批评后，模型可重新启动错误回放并解决原本无法通过采样求解的数学问题；在冷启动GRPO中，它为仅靠可验证奖励无法提供优势信号的提示提供了优势信号。

Adaptive DHiSparse: Speculative Decoding over a Load-Adaptive KV Hierarchy
自适应DHiSparse：负载自适应KV层级上的推测解码
Long-context serving runs out of KV memory before the hardware reaches its useful throughput. HiSparse stores history in host memory and fetches only the selected entries, but it uses one cache budget at every load and does not handle draft-model speculative decoding such as DSpark and DFlash2. We present Adaptive DHiSparse, which supports speculative decoding over hierarchical sparse KV and adapts residency to load. It protects the required working set, retains additional history while GPU memory is spare, and recovers the rest through exact fused retrieval. DSpark and DFlash2 keep their proposal and acceptance rules: queries share missing-row retrieval, draft KV uses a window-bounded ring, and verification staging is reused across layer groups. At 100K context and concurrency 128, GLM-5.3 with DFlash2 delivers 793 output tokens/s per decode GPU, 1.45$\times$ HiSparse and 1.27$\times$ non-speculative adaptive serving. Held to the same latency bounds as HiSparse, it sustains up to 4$\times$ the concurrency and 2.3 to 3.7$\times$ the output, depending on the per-token bound. On DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash at 500K context and concurrency 512, Adaptive DHiSparse with DSpark delivers 1.85$\times$ the output of resident serving with DSpark. In a load step, optional residency reduces host fetches from 14.2% to 1.4% of selected rows at low load and releases memory under pressure.
中文翻译
长上下文服务在硬件达到有效吞吐量前便耗尽KV内存。HiSparse将历史存储在主机内存中，仅提取选定条目，但其在所有负载下使用固定缓存预算，无法处理如DSpark和DFlash2等草案模型的推测解码。我们提出自适应DHiSparse，支持在分层稀疏KV上进行推测解码，并根据负载自适应调整驻留策略。它保护必需的工作集，当GPU内存充裕时保留额外历史，压力下通过精确融合检索恢复其余部分。DSpark和DFlash2保持其提案与接受规则：查询共享缺失行检索，草案KV使用窗口有界环形结构，验证阶段复用跨层组。在10万上下文长度和并发128条件下，GLM-5.3搭配DFlash2实现每解码GPU 793输出token/s，较HiSparse提升1.45倍，较非推测性自适应服务提升1.27倍。在与HiSparse相同的延迟约束下，其并发量最高可达4倍，输出量提升2.3至3.7倍，具体取决于每token的约束。在DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash上，50万上下文长度和并发512条件下，自适应DHiSparse搭配DSpark的输出量为驻留服务的1.85倍。在负载变化时，可选驻留策略将主机获取次数从低负载下的14.2%减少至1.4%的选定行，并在压力下释放内存。
👤 Naji Alazhar, Wassel Alazhar 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

A Multi-Agent LLM System for Automated Tuning of Specialized Neural Network Models
用于专用神经网络模型自动调优的多智能体大语言模型系统
Many machine learning approaches are two-level: an expert selects a class of methods, within this class the choice of key components is critical and usually manual. This paper addresses the automatic tuning of these components and proposes a framework transferable across models of different nature. The multi-agent large language model (LLM) framework has three roles---analyst, coder, and recorder~---with an experiment log and a sandboxed execution environment. Unlike systems that optimize the entire ML script or search architectures in a discrete space, the framework operates over an open but semantically narrow space: the agent generates code only for the specified components, leaving the overall network structure unchanged. The approach is validated in two directions: tuning a classification spiking neural network with correlation learning, and solving high-order differential equations with Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINN). Across five tasks---spiking classification on MNIST and Digits, and boundary-value problems for the generalized nonlinear Schrödinger equation (GNLSE) and Kuramoto--Sivashinsky (KS) equations---the agent outperforms the baselines: classification accuracy is 13 percentage points (MNIST) and 6 percentage points (Digits) higher; the error against the analytical single-soliton solution is reduced approximately 2491-fold; the invariant-preservation error drops approximately 325-fold in the selected GNLSE run with a Gaussian perturbation and by more than 100-fold on average over repeated initializations; and the mass drift drops 14-fold for the KS problem with a Gaussian perturbation. The framework thus tunes key components on tasks of different nature without changing its internal structure.
中文翻译
许多机器学习方法采用两级结构：专家选择一类方法，而在该类方法中关键组件的选择至关重要且通常为手动操作。本文针对这些组件的自动调优提出了一种可跨不同性质模型迁移的框架。该多智能体大语言模型（LLM）框架包含三个角色——分析师、编码员和记录员——并配备实验日志和沙箱执行环境。与优化整个机器学习脚本或在离散空间中搜索架构的系统不同，该框架在开放但语义狭窄的空间上运行：智能体仅生成指定组件的代码，保持整体网络结构不变。该方法在两个方向上得到验证：对具有相关性学习的分类脉冲神经网络进行调优，以及使用物理信息神经网络（PINN）求解高阶微分方程。在五个任务中——MNIST和Digits上的脉冲分类，以及广义非线性薛定谔方程（GNLSE）和库拉莫托-西瓦辛斯基（KS）方程的边值问题——智能体的表现优于基线模型：MNIST分类准确率高出13个百分点，Digits高出6个百分点；相对于解析单孤子解的误差降低约2491倍；在选定的GNLSE运行中（高斯扰动下），守恒量保持误差下降约325倍，平均超过重复初始化100倍以上；KS问题中质量漂移减少14倍（高斯扰动下）。因此，该框架能够在不改变内部结构的情况下，对不同性质的任务调优关键组件。
👤 Ivan Moloshnikov, Dmitry Kunitsyn, Alexander Sboev, Maksim Skorokhodov, Roman Rybka et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Language Model with Heterogeneous Evidence Integration for Clinical Prognosis
基于异构证据融合的检索增强型多模态语言模型用于临床预后预测
Clinical prognosis requires integrating structured measurements, physiological waveforms, longitudinal history, and population context. We introduce PRISM (Population-Retrieved Inference for Structured Multimodal Prognosis), a multimodal language model that combines per-feature numerical tokenization, recency-weighted aggregation of longitudinal ECGs, and a dual-modality population memory bank. Retrieval operates in representation space: the model receives mean-pooled embeddings of similar training-cohort encounters, while no individual retrieved record enters the prompt. On the 1,443-task MDS-ED benchmark, PRISM achieves AUROCs of 86.2% for diagnosis, 93.1% for deterioration, 91.8% for ICU admission, and 92.8% for mortality. These results use a 96-hour pre-arrival window unavailable to reported baselines. With benchmark-window inputs only, PRISM reaches 0.8895 AUROC, compared with 0.8937 for UniPACT and 0.8889 for MDS-ED. A matched-input compact fusion model using the same five evidence streams and frozen encoders reaches 0.7652 AUROC, versus 0.9092 for PRISM. Replacing numerical EHR encoding with text serialization reduces AUROC by 3.66 points; removing pre-arrival history reduces it by 1.97 points; and zeroing retrieval reduces it by 0.99 points. Genuine retrieval outperforms random-neighbor retrieval by 0.97 macro-average points, although a paired task-level sign test does not establish a uniform advantage (p=0.067). On the external MC-MED cohort, supervised fine-tuning reaches 0.8611 AUROC, compared with 0.8227 for locally trained gradient boosting and 0.7841 for source-only PRISM. These results support structure-aware multimodal integration and supervised external adaptation.
中文翻译
临床预后需要整合结构化测量、生理波形、纵向病史和人群背景。我们提出了PRISM（面向结构化多模态预后的群体检索推理），一种结合特征数值标记化、基于时间权重聚合的纵向心电图（ECG）以及双模态群体记忆库的多模态语言模型。检索在表示空间中进行：模型接收类似训练队列经历的均值池化嵌入，而不会将个别检索记录引入提示。在包含1,443个任务的MDS-ED基准上，PRISM在诊断、恶化、ICU入院和死亡预测上的AUROC分别达到86.2%、93.1%、91.8%和92.8%。这些结果使用了报告基线无法获取的96小时到达前窗口。仅使用基准窗口输入时，PRISM达到0.8895 AUROC，优于UniPACT的0.8937和MDS-ED的0.8889。使用相同五条证据流和冻结编码器的匹配输入紧凑融合模型仅达0.7652 AUROC，远低于PRISM的0.9092。将数值EHR编码替换为文本序列化会使AUROC下降3.66点；移除到达前病史使下降1.97点；禁用检索使下降0.99点。真实检索相比随机邻近检索提升0.97个宏平均点，尽管任务层面配对符号检验未确立统一优势（p=0.067）。在外部MC-MED队列上，监督微调达到0.8611 AUROC，优于本地训练梯度提升的0.8227和仅源PRISM的0.7841。这些结果支持结构感知的多模态集成和监督的外部适应。

ProgramGym: Scaling Training Environments for Long-Horizon Program Reproduction Agents
ProgramGym：面向长时程程序复现智能体的可扩展训练环境
Coding agents are increasingly used to build programs from scratch as software engineers do rather than to resolve issues within a repository or to generate a repository from a given requirements document. However, scalable verifiable training environments that force agents to discover what is required and to decide how the system should be designed remain scarce. Moreover, existing agent workflows also cannot sustain long-horizon development in which the agent explores the reference program before implementation. To address these challenges, we introduce ProgramGym, a framework consisting of Program-Env and Program-Agent for scalable training environments of long-horizon program reproduction agents. Specifically, Program-Env constructs verifiable training environments from repositories through a feedback-driven loop that repairs runtime sandbox builds and the quality of black-box tests. Using this novel pipeline, we obtain 1,648 training environments spanning C, C++, Go, and Rust. Program-Agent combines a role-isolated workflow of analysis, implementation, and review with revision management for compilation checks and failure recovery. Experiments on ProgramBench show that both Program-Env and Program-Agent improve performance on program reproduction. Fine-tuning on trajectories with these constructed environments raises the performance of Qwen3.8-27B from 34.87% to 42.84%. Program-Agent further raises the base and SFT models to 55.71% and 56.25%, both exceeding some flagship models. We will release the ProgramGym to support further research on coding agents that build programs from scratch.

Taking Tokenization off the Host for Agentic LLM Serving
将分词从主机中移出以支持智能体大模型服务
Agentic workloads resend long contexts that barely change between calls. Prefix caching now skips most of their prefill, yet every request must still be tokenized in full before the cache can even be consulted. Tokenization, a CPU stage long treated as negligible, becomes the largest part of time-to-first-token (TTFT), and a frontend that tokenizes for a whole GPU fleet limits how many GPUs it can feed. We present GraphTok, a GPU tokenizer that runs the entire encoding pipeline, from pre-tokenization through byte-pair encoding (BPE) merging to token-ID assembly, as one replayable CUDA graph. GraphTok reproduces the reference token IDs bit-for-bit on GPT-2, Llama 3, Qwen 2.5, DeepSeek-V3, and Gemma 3, hands short inputs to a CPU engine where the GPU cannot win, and plugs into unmodified vLLM and NVIDIA Dynamo. GraphTok encodes one million GPT-2 tokens in 1.7-3.2 ms including data transfers, and on Llama 3 it is 4.7-6.0x faster than gigatoken, the strongest CPU tokenizer. On TokTier's own single-request benchmark, it is 2.6-3.9x faster than gigatoken and up to 18.1x faster than TokTier. In vLLM with a cached prefix, it lowers mean TTFT by 2.1-2.4x. The largest gain is capacity. Within a 2 s P99 target, one Dynamo frontend with GraphTok sustains 518 requests/s of 121k-token traffic, the busiest-hour load of 56 GPUs, against 296 with gigatoken and 18.5 with the default tokenizer. Across 121k to one million tokens, this is 1.75-2.5x the load gigatoken sustains, with a P99 up to 64x lower where gigatoken's tail breaks.
中文翻译
智能体工作负载具有长时间上下文且在调用间几乎不变。前缀缓存现在可以跳过大部分预填充阶段，但每次请求仍需完整进行分词后才能查询缓存。分词这一过去被视为可忽略的CPU阶段，如今已成为首个标记时间（TTFT）的主要部分，而一个为整个GPU集群分词的前端限制了其能支持的GPU数量。我们提出GraphTok，一种运行在GPU上的分词器，将从预分词到字节对编码（BPE）合并再到标记ID组装的整个编码流程作为一个可重放的CUDA图执行。GraphTok在GPT-2、Llama 3、Qwen 2.5、DeepSeek-V3和Gemma 3上与参考分词结果逐位一致，在短输入场景下交由CPU引擎处理，且可无缝集成至未修改的vLLM和NVIDIA Dynamo。GraphTok在包含数据传输的情况下，100万GPT-2标记的编码时间为1.7-3.2毫秒；在Llama 3上比最强的CPU分词器gigatoken快4.7-6.0倍。在TokTier自身的单请求基准测试中，比gigatoken快2.6-3.9倍，最高达18.1倍。在vLLM配合缓存前缀时，平均TTFT降低2.1-2.4倍。最大收益体现在容量上：在2秒P99目标内，一个搭载GraphTok的Dynamo前端可维持每秒518个12.1万标记请求，相当于56个GPU的高峰负载，而gigatoken仅支持296次，原生分词器仅18.5次。在12.1万至一百万标记范围内，该性能是gigatoken的1.75-2.5倍，P99延迟最高降低64倍，远优于gigatoken的尾部表现。

BoundaryEvo: Data-Model Co-Evolution at the Capability Boundary for Long-Chain Reasoning Distillation
边界演化：在能力边界处实现数据-模型协同进化以提炼长链推理
Distilling long-chain reasoning trajectories from large teacher models provides an effective way to improve the reasoning ability of small language models. However, existing methods typically select a fixed training set before distillation, overlooking that the usefulness of a reasoning example changes as the student evolves. This mismatch becomes especially costly when only a small number of teacher trajectories can be used for training. Besides, examples already mastered provide little new supervision, while examples far beyond the capability of student may be difficult to learn from. Training data should therefore adapt as model capability evolves, yet reliable data selection is difficult when the student is initially weak. To address these challenges, our key idea is to first establish the basic reasoning capability and then adapt the training data to the evolving capability boundary of the student. We propose BoundaryEvo, a two-stage data--model co-evolution framework. In Capability Priming, we directly distill a small set of teacher-generated long reasoning trajectories without using a separate model to judge and filter them, allowing the student to acquire an initial reasoning capability before capability-aware data selection. In Capability-Boundary Co-Evolution, we repeatedly select questions that the current student has not mastered but the expert can reliably solve, and update the training data as the student evolves. With only 1,000 training examples, BoundaryEvo consistently outperforms static and dynamic data selection baselines across 11 reasoning benchmarks, raising the original Qwen3.5-4B accuracy from 62.9 to 75.3 on IMO-AnswerBench and from 26.8 to 46.0 on AMO-Bench. The code and data will be made publicly available.
中文翻译
从大型教师模型中提炼长链推理轨迹是提升小型语言模型推理能力的有效方法。然而，现有方法通常在蒸馏前固定训练集，忽略了随着学生模型演进，推理示例的有用性会发生变化。当仅能使用少量教师轨迹时，这种不匹配尤为代价高昂。此外，已掌握的示例提供极少新监督信号，而远超学生能力的示例则难以学习。因此，训练数据应随模型能力演化而自适应调整，但在学生初始能力较弱时，可靠的数据选择极具挑战。为此，我们的核心思想是先建立基本推理能力，再根据学生能力边界的演化动态调整训练数据。我们提出BoundaryEvo，一种两阶段数据-模型协同进化框架。在能力预热阶段，直接蒸馏少量教师生成的长推理轨迹，无需额外模型判断与过滤，使学生在具备能力感知数据选择前获得初步推理能力。在能力边界协同演化阶段，反复选取当前学生尚未掌握但专家可稳定解决的问题，并随学生进步持续更新训练数据。仅使用1000个训练示例，BoundaryEvo在11个推理基准上均持续优于静态与动态数据选择基线，将原始Qwen3.5-4B在IMO-AnswerBench上的准确率从62.9提升至75.3，在AMO-Bench上从26.8提升至46.0。代码与数据将公开发布。
