agentic-rl
CAST: Credit Alignment for Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation
CAST：跨分词器策略蒸馏中的信用对齐
Cross-tokenizer on-policy distillation (OPD) requires aligning teacher and student sequences before their scores can be compared. Decoded-text alignment provides such correspondence at the chunk level, but leaves a distinct problem unresolved: credit alignment. A chunk-level signal specifies how much a span should change, but not how that signal should be distributed across student tokens, while tokens without teacher correspondence remain unconstrained. We propose CAST (Chunk-aware credit Assignment with Selective Token Anchoring), a shift-or-stay framework that addresses both issues. For aligned chunks, CAST redistributes each chunk's advantage across student tokens using predictive uncertainty and local teacher-signal smoothness, emphasizing learning opportunities supported by locally consistent teacher feedback while preserving the chunk's total advantage. The resulting weights exactly solve a KL-regularized credit-allocation problem. For unaligned positions, CAST selectively anchors the student to its initial policy through a KL penalty, limiting drift where teacher supervision is unavailable. Because credit assignment is separated from chunk construction, CAST also applies to punctuation-defined or fixed-length chunks under shared tokenization. Across six mathematics, science, and code benchmarks, CAST improves the average over the strongest evaluated baseline by $2.45$ and $1.32$ points for cross-tokenizer transfer from Qwen3-8B to Mistral-7B and Llama-3.1-8B, respectively, and by $2.10$ points for shared-tokenizer transfer to Qwen3-1.7B.
中文翻译
跨分词器策略蒸馏（OPD）要求在比较教师与学生序列得分前先对齐它们。解码文本对齐可在块级别提供对应关系，但仍存在一个未解决的问题：信用对齐。块级信号指定了某段应改变的程度，但未说明如何在学生标记间分配该信号，而无教师对应关系的标记则完全不受约束。我们提出CAST（块感知的信用分配与选择性标记锚定），一种“转移或保持”框架，同时解决这两个问题。对于对齐块，CAST利用预测不确定性与局部教师信号平滑性，将每块的优势重新分配给学生标记，强调由局部一致教师反馈支持的学习机会，同时保留块的总优势。所得权重恰好解决了KL正则化的信用分配问题。对于未对齐位置，CAST通过KL惩罚选择性地将学生锚定在其初始策略上，防止在缺乏教师监督时发生漂移。由于信用分配与块构建分离，CAST也适用于由标点符号定义或固定长度的块，以及共享分词器的情况。在六个数学、科学和代码基准上，CAST分别在从Qwen3-8B到Mistral-7B和Llama-3.1-8B的跨分词器迁移中，平均性能超过最强基线2.45和1.32分；在共享分词器迁移至Qwen3-1.7B时，提升2.10分。
👤 Jingru Yang, Jinhang Wu, Minjing Shi, Shengfeng He, Xuyan Li et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.13202]
agentic-rl
S$^3$R: Fast and Faithful RL Rollouts via Self-Sparse Speculative Decoding
S$^3$R：通过自稀疏推测解码实现快速且忠实的强化学习滚动采样
Fast rollout generation for long-context reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) requires reducing KV-cache traffic without changing the response groups used for policy optimization. In our profiled RLVR workloads, attention accounts for 72--76\% of autoregressive decoding kernel time. Sparse attention reduces these reads, but sampling directly from a sparse policy changes the response groups used for learning. For group-relative optimization, we show that even exact response-wise reweighting does not generally recover the expected dense-policy update because the advantage of each response depends on the rewards of other group members. We propose Self-Sparse Speculative Rollouts (S$^3$R). It uses sparse attention to propose tokens, then generates responses through full-attention verification and exact speculative sampling. Drafting and verification share the same policy parameter, eliminating the need to train or update a separate drafter. The resulting response groups follow the dense-policy distribution. Under S$^3$R, we characterize speedup conditions and the costs of drafting and verification. We found that dense verification still requires the complete historical KV cache. A KV-aware scheduler therefore limits KV-cache repeated offloading and restoration while maintaining rollout parallelism. % OR thrashing In a matched ablation with an aggressive sparse drafting budget, S$^3$R maintains stable training while direct sparse sampling with training-side correction becomes unstable. On long-context mathematical reasoning, S$^3$R achieves $1.39\times~1.35\times$ the end-to-end training throughput of autoregressive rollouts on Qwen3 model, with comparable task performance.
中文翻译
针对具有可验证奖励的长上下文强化学习（RLVR）任务，快速生成滚动采样需要在不改变用于策略优化的响应组的前提下减少 KV 缓存流量。在我们对 RLVR 工作负载的性能分析中，注意力机制占自回归解码内核时间的 72%–76%。稀疏注意力虽能减少读取次数，但直接从稀疏策略中采样会改变用于学习的响应组。对于组相对优化，我们发现即使采用精确的逐响应重加权也无法普遍恢复预期的密集策略更新，因为每个响应的优势取决于其他组成员的奖励。为此，我们提出自稀疏推测滚动采样（S$^3$R）。它使用稀疏注意力提出候选 token，再通过全注意力验证和精确推测采样生成最终响应。起草与验证共享同一策略参数，无需训练或更新独立的起草器。由此产生的响应组符合密集策略分布。在 S$^3$R 下，我们刻画了加速条件及起草与验证的成本。研究发现，密集验证仍需完整的历史 KV 缓存。因此，一个 KV 友好的调度器可在保持滚动并行性的同时，限制 KV 缓存的重复卸载与恢复。在与激进稀疏起草预算的对比实验中，S$^3$R 保持训练稳定，而直接稀疏采样结合训练端修正则变得不稳定。在长上下文数学推理任务中，S$^3$R 在 Qwen3 模型上实现了 1.39× 至 1.35× 的端到端训练吞吐量提升，同时保持相近的任务性能。

JANUS: Enhancing Long-Horizon Autonomous Research with Co-Evolving World Models
JANUS：通过共演化世界模型增强长周期自主研究
Long-horizon autonomous research has shown promise in accelerating scientific progress through sustained cycles of hypothesis generation, implementation, and validation. However, this Generate-Execute-Feedback paradigm relies on reactive trial-and-error, where costly and time-consuming executions introduce severe feedback delays, throttling evolutionary progress under explosively expanding search space. To address this challenge, we propose JANUS, a framework that unifies future outcomes forecasting and past experience reflection through a Predict-Verify-Evolve loop driven by a co-evolving world model. Within an evolving graph, the research agent proposes candidates while world model scores them by forecasting value improvement, information gain, and exploration utility, then accordingly selects, refines, or prunes branches prior to execution. After receiving ground-truth feedback, JANUS distills experience into evolving skills: hierarchical skills for research agent, exploration-strategy skills for world model, and their coordination protocols. This mechanism enables continual co-evolution: the research agent sharpens candidate generation while the world model refines its predictive judgment over extended horizons. Extensive evaluations on MLS-Bench-Lite and real research tasks demonstrate that JANUS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving both higher evolving efficiency and ceiling.
中文翻译
长周期自主研究在通过持续的假设生成、实施与验证循环加速科学进步方面展现出巨大潜力。然而，这种‘生成-执行-反馈’范式依赖于反应式的试错过程，昂贵且耗时的执行引入了严重的反馈延迟，在搜索空间急剧扩张的背景下严重制约了进化进展。为应对这一挑战，我们提出 JANUS 框架，通过一个由共演化世界模型驱动的‘预测-验证-演化’循环，统一未来结果预测与过往经验反思。在一个动态演化的图结构中，研究智能体提出候选方案，世界模型通过预测价值提升、信息增益和探索效用对它们进行评分，随后据此选择、优化或剪枝分支后再执行。接收真实反馈后，JANUS 将经验提炼为不断演化的技能：研究智能体的分层技能、世界模型的探索策略技能及其协调协议。这一机制实现了持续的共演化：研究智能体不断提升候选生成能力，世界模型则增强其在长期范围内的预测判断力。在 MLS-Bench-Lite 和真实科研任务上的广泛评估表明，JANUS 显著优于现有最先进基线，实现了更高的演化效率和上限性能。

Travel Back To Move Forward: Retrospective Exploration and Evolving for Long Horizon Agents
回溯以向前：面向长时程智能体的回顾性探索与演化
Large language model agents increasingly operate in long-horizon environments where early mistakes can trigger cascading failures and trap agents in dead-end trajectories. Agents must therefore be able to proactively recover from such states and turn their exploratory experience into guidance for subsequent decisions. We introduce ReTRAIL, a framework that enables agents to rewind the environment to earlier states while preserving valid progress and carrying forward knowledge learned from abandoned exploration. The environment state moves backward while the agent's knowledge continues to move forward. ReTRAIL trains a shared policy with two complementary signals: State-Anchored GRPO compares alternative continuations from revisited states to improve execution, while a branch-advancement reward guides reflection through the progress of subsequent exploration. Experiments across four interactive benchmark MineSweeper, Sokoban, ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that simply adding a rewind primitive provides limited benefit and can even degrade performance. In contrast, ReTRAIL enables agents to rewind from unproductive states and use insights from abandoned branches to guide subsequent exploration. These results demonstrate the value of coupling environment rewinding with experience reuse within an ongoing task for long-horizon task solving. The code is available at this https URL .
中文翻译
大型语言模型智能体越来越多地运行在长时程环境中，早期错误可能引发级联失败并使智能体陷入死胡同轨迹。因此，智能体必须能够主动从这些状态中恢复，并将探索经验转化为后续决策的指导。我们提出了ReTRAIL框架，使智能体能够在保留有效进展的同时，将环境状态回退到早期状态，并携带从被放弃的探索中学习到的知识继续前进。环境状态向后移动，而智能体的知识则持续向前发展。ReTRAIL通过两个互补信号训练共享策略：基于状态锚定的GRPO比较从重访状态出发的替代延续路径以改进执行，而分支推进奖励则通过后续探索的进展引导反思。在四个交互式基准测试（MineSweeper、Sokoban、ALFWorld和ScienceWorld）上的实验表明，仅添加回退原语带来的收益有限，甚至可能降低性能。相比之下，ReTRAIL使智能体能够从无产出状态回退，并利用被放弃分支中的洞察来指导后续探索。这些结果证明了在持续任务中将环境回退与经验复用相结合对于长时程任务求解的价值。代码可在该链接获取。

EmbodiedCLBench: Evaluating Continual Learning for Self-Evolving Embodied Agents
EmbodiedCLBench：评估自演化具身智能体的持续学习能力
Continual learning requires models to accumulate past experience and transfer it to new tasks. Despite substantial research on continual learning, existing work remains largely confined to non-embodied settings, without extending to embodied environments. To address this gap, we introduce EmbodiedCLBench, the first benchmark in embodied simulation for evaluating the continual learning capability of self-evolving agents. With compositional generalization as the core evaluation principle, our benchmark evaluates whether agents can first learn from basic tasks and then recombine the acquired skills to solve complex, unseen advanced tasks. Based on EmbodiedCLBench, we conduct extensive experiments across multiple agent harnesses and learning methods, revealing consistent limitations and distinctive behaviors. Our results show that while advanced tasks can be hardly solved under zero-shot setting, the experience from basic tasks enables consistent improvement. Such benefit from the experience remains stable even if the task complexity increases. However, this improvement does not scale accordingly with the number of learning samples, and current agents cannot extract meaningful information from additional experience. Collectively, our findings highlight continual learning as a fundamental yet underexplored capability, and our benchmark offers valuable resources for designing effective self-evolving agents in embodied environments. Our code and dataset are available at this https URL .
中文翻译
持续学习要求模型积累过往经验并将其迁移到新任务中。尽管持续学习研究已取得大量成果，但现有工作仍主要局限于非具身环境，未能扩展至具身环境。为填补这一空白，我们引入EmbodiedCLBench，这是首个用于具身仿真环境中评估自演化智能体持续学习能力的基准。以组合泛化为核心评估原则，我们的基准测试智能体是否能先从基础任务中学习，再重组所获技能以解决复杂且未见过的高级任务。基于EmbodiedCLBench，我们在多个智能体架构和学习方法上进行了广泛实验，揭示了系统性的局限性和独特行为。结果显示，尽管零样本设置下高级任务几乎无法解决，但来自基础任务的经验能带来一致的性能提升。这种经验带来的益处即使在任务复杂度增加时依然稳定。然而，这种提升并未随学习样本数量成比例增长，当前智能体无法从额外经验中提取有意义的信息。总体而言，我们的发现强调持续学习是一种基础但尚未充分探索的能力，而本基准为设计有效的具身环境中自演化智能体提供了宝贵资源。代码和数据集可在该链接获取。

SmartSalesBench: A Resistance-Aware Multi-Turn Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Sales Agents
SmartSalesBench：面向LLM销售智能体的抗阻型多轮对话基准
We present SmartSalesBench, the first multi-turn conversational benchmark explicitly designed to evaluate large language model (LLM) sales agents against psychologically grounded, resistant buyer personas. Existing task-oriented dialogue benchmarks assume cooperative users and measure task completion; SmartSalesBench inverts this assumption, placing agents against personas programmed to resist and challenge across conversations of up to twenty turns. The benchmark includes five agent profiles, twelve buyer personas, fifteen smartphone products across six price tiers, and four controlled experiments with 3 iterations totaling 13,680 sessions. Both sales-agent and buyer persona generations are grounded through retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). We introduce nine evaluation metrics grouped into three tiers: primary outcome metrics (CR, BRS, RIR), secondary quality metrics (SMS, PFS, ETI), and exploratory metrics planned for future reporting (ITS, ARI, SPI). Our evaluation combines automated LLM-as-judge scoring with internal human assessment (n = 50, \k{appa} = 0.71, Pearson r = 0.81 with BRS).

MVGraph: Benchmarking LVLMs on Evidence Composition across Multi-View Visual Graph
MVGraph：跨多视角视觉图的证据组合对大视觉语言模型的基准测试
We introduce MVGraph, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models (LVLMs) on multi-graph reasoning where the evidence required by a query is distributed across multiple interrelated visual graph views. Although such evidence commonly resides in source-specific graphs, temporal snapshots, or local observations, existing graph reasoning benchmarks remain confined to a single self-contained structure, assessing only graph perception and reasoning while leaving the alignment and composition of evidence across views underexplored. MVGraph comprises 8k instances spanning 8 \textit{graph-theoretic} tasks, abstracted from two multi-graph scenarios (partial and temporal graph views). Each problem is programmatically constructed and verified to be unsolvable from a single view. Beyond end-to-end evaluation, we decompose multi-graph reasoning into four complementary capabilities---Perception, Alignment, Composition, and Reasoning---and design 29 diagnostic tasks with 64k instances to probe each one, along with golden intermediate representations for evaluating the intermediate process. Across 15 LVLMs, we reveal the difficulty of multi-graph reasoning and identify cross-view edge alignment and relation-level composition as prominent bottlenecks through stage-oriented diagnostics. Meanwhile, temporal constraints and increasing view counts expose further capability boundaries. Code and data are available at this https URL .

VEPA: Verified Evolving Formal Policy for Lifelong LLM Agent Guardrails
VEPA：可验证演化的形式化策略用于持续学习的LLM智能体防护机制
LLM agents increasingly operate over sensitive resources, such as private documents and mutable system states, which can create serious safety and security risks. Runtime guardrails should therefore make inspectable, reproducible, and deterministic allow or refuse decisions before potentially irreversible agent actions are executed. However, safety and security requirements are rarely complete before deployment, so guardrails need to keep adapting as new risks emerge. Existing adaptive guardrails learn from deployment experience, but they usually apply safety policies through LLM inference, which can lead to inconsistent decisions and ambiguous policy interpretations. Formal guardrails, on the other hand, enforce policies deterministically, but they usually rely on pre-deployment or task-specific policies and have no mechanism to adapt to newly observed risks. We introduce VEPA, a framework for the verified evolution of formal agent guardrails. VEPA represents the guardrail as a formal policy of executable symbolic rules over typed agent events. During deployment, we use an LLM guided by deployment feedback to propose policy patches that fix newly observed failures while preserving previously correct behavior. Then, a deterministic verifier only accepts policies that are structurally valid and conflict-free. Finally, an evidence-aware authority layer activates a verified rule only when it has enough supporting evidence, so uncertain updates cannot directly affect guardrail decisions. Across ASSEBench-Safety, ASSEBench-Security, and ATBench, VEPA consistently outperforms baselines while expanding the scope of deterministic formal-policy enforcement. These gains also hold under more challenging deployment conditions, including mixed streams, corrupted feedback, and longer horizons.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体越来越多地操作敏感资源，如私有文档和可变系统状态，这可能带来严重的安全与隐私风险。因此，运行时防护机制应在执行不可逆动作前做出可检查、可复现且确定性的允许或拒绝决策。然而，安全与安全需求在部署前往往不完整，防护机制需随新风险出现而持续适应。现有自适应防护机制通过LLM推理学习部署经验，但常导致决策不一致和策略解释模糊。形式化防护机制虽能确定性地执行策略，但通常依赖于预部署或任务特定策略，缺乏应对新风险的适应机制。我们提出VEPA框架，实现形式化智能体防护机制的可验证演化。VEPA将防护机制表示为在类型化智能体事件上可执行的符号规则形式策略。部署期间，我们利用部署反馈引导的LLM提出修复新发现失败的策略补丁，同时保持先前正确行为不变。随后，确定性验证器仅接受结构有效且无冲突的策略。最后，基于证据的权威层仅在拥有足够支持证据时激活已验证规则，确保不确定更新不会直接影响防护决策。在ASSEBench-Safety、ASSEBench-Security和ATBench上，VEPA始终优于基线方法，同时扩展了确定性形式策略执行的范围。这些优势在更具挑战性的部署条件下（如混合流、损坏反馈、更长时间窗）依然成立。
👤 Duy Nguyen, Lesly Miculicich, Bhavana Dalvi Mishra, Shuo Li, Krishnamurthy Dj Dvijotham et al. 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv

Meta-Reflective Evolution Effectively Improves Production AI Systems
元反思进化有效提升生产环境AI系统的性能
Large Language Model (LLM)-guided evolutionary optimization has successfully improved prompts, programs, and even entire AI systems. Most optimization methods select a candidate from the search frontier, then mutate it in one LLM pass based only on its failures. This focus on the frontier can stall on production AI systems, where isolated failures reveal little about complex, hidden interactions. We introduce CoCoEvolve (Cortex Code Evolve), an evolutionary optimizer built around what we call meta-reflection, wherein an agentic mutator (Cortex Code) selectively retrieves evidence from the entire evolutionary history and probes the live system before verifying and proposing mutations. We apply CoCoEvolve to optimize three classes of production AI systems: data agents, LLM-powered data pipelines, and data engineering workflows. On DABStep, CoCoEvolve tops the public leaderboard, reaching 92.8% held-out hard accuracy compared to 78.4% for Meta-Harness and 30.4% for GEPA under matched conditions. Across six LLM data pipeline workloads, it outperforms every evaluated baseline. On data-eng-bench, CoCoEvolve lifts test-set solve rate from 59.1% to 77.4%, past the best baseline of 73.6%. It also has 11 percentage points higher accuracy than GEPA on HotpotQA while remaining competitive with specialized optimizers on established mathematical and systems benchmarks. Matched ablations confirm that each component of CoCoEvolve contributes to these gains, demonstrating the value of meta-reflection for optimizing production AI systems.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）引导的进化优化已成功用于改进提示词、程序乃至整个AI系统。大多数优化方法仅从搜索前沿选择候选者，并在一次LLM调用中根据其失败进行突变。这种对前沿的关注在生产环境中容易停滞，因为孤立的失败难以揭示复杂隐藏的交互。我们提出CoCoEvolve（Cortex Code Evolve），一种基于“元反思”的进化优化器，其中代理型突变器（Cortex Code）会从完整的进化历史中选择性检索证据，并在验证和提出突变前探查实时系统。我们将CoCoEvolve应用于三类生产环境AI系统：数据代理、LLM驱动的数据流水线和数据工程工作流。在DABStep上，CoCoEvolve在公开排行榜上领先，达到92.8%的保留硬样本准确率，远超Meta-Harness的78.4%和GEPA的30.4%（在相同条件下）。在六个LLM数据流水线工作负载中，其表现优于所有评估的基线。在data-eng-bench上，CoCoEvolve将测试集求解率从59.1%提升至77.4%，超过最佳基线的73.6%。在HotpotQA上，其准确率比GEPA高出11个百分点，同时在经典数学与系统基准上仍保持与专用优化器相当的竞争力。匹配消融实验确认了CoCoEvolve各组件均贡献显著，证明了元反思在优化生产AI系统中的价值。
👤 Seung Won Wilson Yoo, Chang Zeng, Aditi Somayajula, Sahithi Ankireddy, Rohan Kosalge et al. 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv
[2610.12769]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
REDaCT: Collapse-Free Unlearning in Large Reasoning Models
REDaCT：大推理模型中的无崩溃遗忘机制
Unlearning in large reasoning models (LRMs) requires removing forget-set information from both the reasoning trace and the final answer. Existing approaches achieve this objective at the cost of reasoning collapse, where the model fails to produce a coherent chain-of-thought (CoT) and does not properly terminate its reasoning process. To address this challenge, we propose REDaCT (REgenerate via Detection and Corrective Tuning), an inference-time unlearning framework for LRMs. REDaCT consists of two key components: (1) a binary leak detector that identifies sentences in the reasoning trace that leak forget-set information, and (2) an embedding optimization module that learns a corrective embedding offline, such that when injected at the first detected leak during inference, it guides subsequent reasoning towards sentences that do not leak forget-set information. Experimental results show that REDaCT completely avoids reasoning collapse, substantially improving reasoning preservation compared to state-of-the-art unlearning methods while maintaining perfect answer forgetting and near-perfect CoT forgetting. Our code is available at: this https URL .
中文翻译
大推理模型（LRMs）中的遗忘需要从推理过程和最终答案中移除遗忘集信息。现有方法虽达成此目标，但代价是引发推理崩溃，即模型无法生成连贯的思维链（CoT），也无法正确终止推理过程。为解决此挑战，我们提出REDaCT（通过检测与纠正微调再生），一种面向LRMs的推理时遗忘框架。REDaCT包含两个核心组件：(1) 二分类泄露检测器，用于识别推理过程中泄露遗忘集信息的句子；(2) 嵌入优化模块，离线学习一个纠正嵌入，当在推理过程中首次检测到泄露时注入该嵌入，从而引导后续推理走向不泄露遗忘集信息的句子。实验结果表明，REDaCT完全避免了推理崩溃，在推理保留方面显著优于当前最优遗忘方法，同时保持完美的答案遗忘和近乎完美的CoT遗忘。代码已公开。
👤 Joshua Maccoby, Wendy Hui Wang 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv
[2610.12668]

Recurrent Harness for Agentic Continual Learning
用于智能体持续学习的循环式Harness框架
Language model agents increasingly operate over long interaction streams, requiring online adaptation to distribution shifts through continual learning. However, existing agent harnesses typically either evolve their states only through forward information flow or rely on additional replay or validation data for harness optimization. To address this, we propose ReHarness, a recurrent harness framework for agentic continual learning that complements forward read and write with backward revision under a strictly online streaming protocol. We ask what information could have been captured differently in the harness state to better inform the current instance in the stream. Specifically, after each instance, we leverage the resulting trajectory as feedback to revise the reflections retrieved for that instance, thereby propagating feedback backward through the textual harness state. Across three continual learning benchmarks and three language model backbones, ReHarness consistently outperforms strong raw-context baselines, memory- and reflection-based methods, and prompt optimization approaches. Further analysis shows that ReHarness produces source-grounded and meaningful revisions, corrects overgeneralized or outdated experience under distribution shifts, and improves both stability and plasticity during continual learning.
中文翻译
语言模型智能体越来越多地在长时间交互流中运行，需要通过持续学习实现对分布变化的在线适应。然而，现有的智能体Harness通常仅通过前向信息流来更新状态，或依赖额外的重放数据或验证数据进行优化。为解决这一问题，我们提出了ReHarness，一种用于智能体持续学习的循环式Harness框架，该框架在严格的在线流协议下，将前向读写与后向修订相结合。我们探讨了Harness状态中可能以不同方式捕获的信息，以更好地指导当前流中的实例。具体而言，在每个实例之后，我们利用生成的轨迹作为反馈，对相应实例的反思内容进行修订，从而将反馈反向传播至文本形式的Harness状态中。在三个持续学习基准和三种语言模型主干上，ReHarness始终优于强大的原始上下文基线、基于记忆和反思的方法以及提示优化方法。进一步分析表明，ReHarness能够产生基于源信息且有意义的修订，纠正在分布变化下过度泛化或过时的经验，并提升持续学习过程中的稳定性和可塑性。
👤 Yuxin Xiao, Zhiyun Lu, Jianyu Wang, Yangtian Zhang, Yang Song et al. 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv
[2610.12596]

SpireBench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Strategic Play of Agentic Models with Slay the Spire
SpireBench：基于《杀戮尖塔》评估智能体的长周期战略表现
Agentic large language models now tackle complex tasks, yet strategic reasoning over long horizons is under-studied: most agentic benchmarks are short and episodic, and performance on long tasks is hard to dissect into stored knowledge, reasoning and luck. We introduce SpireBench, in which models play the complete commercial deck-builder Slay the Spire (StS), where early decisions decide long-term success. Across 20 large language models (LLMs) at the game's highest difficulty, frontier models beat heuristic baselines and match the average human player but remain far behind experts. This gap widens as the run lengthens: the strongest models win the first act as often as experts, then fall behind. What separates strong models from weak is how well they play each fight; all of them misjudge risk over a run, preserving health rather than spending it to grow stronger. A comprehensive, self-consistent renaming of every game term shows that part of their performance rests on prior knowledge of the game, and models do not improve from the memories they write across runs, which are often wrong and overly cautious. Our harness is the first to give a model the complete original game, and we compare it with existing harnesses. Overall, SpireBench offers an unsaturated long-horizon benchmark with tools to locate where and why agents fail.
中文翻译
智能体大语言模型如今能够处理复杂任务，但长周期的战略推理仍缺乏充分研究：大多数智能体基准测试短小且具有片段性，而长任务的表现难以分解为存储知识、推理能力和运气成分。我们引入SpireBench，让模型完整体验商业级卡牌构建游戏《杀戮尖塔》（StS），其中早期决策决定长期成功。在最高难度下对20个大语言模型进行测试，前沿模型击败了启发式基线并达到平均人类玩家水平，但仍远落后于专家。随着游戏进程延长，差距进一步扩大：最强模型在第一幕胜率与专家相当，随后迅速落后。区分强弱模型的关键在于每场战斗的策略执行质量；所有模型在整局游戏中都错误评估风险，倾向于保命而非消耗生命以增强实力。一项全面且自洽的游戏术语重命名分析表明，部分性能依赖于模型对游戏的先验知识，而模型并未从跨轮次所写的记忆中获得改进，这些记忆往往错误且过于保守。我们的Harness是首个让模型接触完整原版游戏的系统，并与现有Harness进行了比较。总体而言，SpireBench提供了一个尚未饱和的长周期基准，具备定位智能体失败位置与原因的工具。
👤 Scott C. Lowe, Sander W. Keemink 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv

[2610.13910]
When VLMs Destroy What They Know: Spatial Representations Fall Below Lexical Priors
当视觉语言模型破坏其已知内容时：空间表征低于词汇先验
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) increasingly analyze and reason about images, yet it remains unclear whether their internal representations faithfully encode the structured relations required for such reasoning. We introduce EICS (Evaluation-Invariant Concept Stability), a metric for testing whether representations consistently encode concepts under controlled one-concept interventions, and CASP-VL (Causally controlled Spatial Perturbations for Vision-Language models), a benchmark of causally controlled spatial perturbations derived from Visual Genome scene graphs. Across multiple VLM architectures, we find that spatial relations are represented substantially less faithfully than lexical information, even when the same models score well above chance on compositional benchmarks. Further analysis shows that the relation is identifiable at the predicate's own tokens but is lost when the sentence is aggregated into a global representation, whether through the (EOS) token or by mean pooling. These findings reveal a systematic failure invisible to output-based evaluation: VLMs do not preserve, in the representation used downstream, relational information that is present in their own input.
中文翻译
视觉语言模型（VLMs）越来越多地用于分析和推理图像，但其内部表征是否忠实编码了推理所需的结构化关系仍不明确。我们引入EICS（评估不变概念稳定性）作为测试表征在受控单概念干预下是否一致编码概念的度量，并提出CASP-VL（面向视觉语言模型的因果控制空间扰动），一个基于Visual Genome场景图的因果控制空间扰动基准。在多个VLM架构中，我们发现空间关系的表征远不如词汇信息忠实，即使这些模型在组合性基准上的表现远超随机水平。进一步分析表明，关系信息可在谓词自身的标记中识别，但在句子被聚合为全局表征时（无论是通过EOS标记还是平均池化）便丢失。这些发现揭示了一种输出评估无法察觉的系统性失败：VLMs在其下游使用的表征中并未保留输入中存在的关系信息。
👤 Shubh Garg, Chen Shani 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.13807]
Error Rate is Not Enough: Costs of Augmentation in Low-Resource ASR
错误率不足以说明问题：低资源语音识别中数据增强的成本
Data augmentation is standard practice for low-resource automatic speech recognition (ASR), but its benefits are almost always reported as aggregate error rates, leaving open why and when a method helps. On four low-resource languages and English at five data sizes, we cross two augmentations that modify existing audio (speed perturbation and SpecAugment) with two that modify text transcriptions and synthesize audio from it. We analyze errors at the phoneme level and find that they help for complementary reasons. SpecAugment has the most reliable aggregate improvements, but increases tone and palatalization errors, whereas text-based methods introduce consonant insertions; in combination, each reduces the errors the other introduces. This complementarity is predictable. Tone and palatalization are each signaled by a single acoustic property and occur least frequently in training (lowest training exposure), and text-based methods that flatten word frequency raise their frequency by 37--38%, reversing those errors while further improving aggregate error. The improvement from combining the methods exceeds the sum of their individual improvements; the superadditivity vanishes by 100 hours, limiting the complementarity to the low-resource regime. Where specific error types matter, we recommend selecting augmentation by training exposure and acoustic properties, both measurable before any training.
中文翻译
数据增强是低资源自动语音识别（ASR）中的标准做法，但其收益通常仅以整体错误率报告，未阐明方法为何以及在何时有效。我们在四种低资源语言和英语上，于五个不同数据规模下，交叉对比了两种修改现有音频的增强方法（速度扰动和SpecAugment）与两种修改文本转录并据此合成音频的方法。通过音素级别的错误分析，我们发现它们具有互补性原因。SpecAugment在整体改进上最可靠，但会增加声调和腭化错误；而基于文本的方法则引入辅音插入；两者结合时，各自减少了对方引入的错误。这种互补性可预测：声调和腭化分别由单一声学特征表示，且在训练中出现频率最低（训练暴露最少），而基于文本的方法通过降低词频平坦化使这些特征频率提升37%~38%，从而逆转错误并进一步改善整体错误率。结合方法带来的改进超过各自单独改进之和；但在100小时后，这种超加性消失，表明互补性仅存在于低资源场景。当特定错误类型重要时，我们建议根据训练暴露程度和声学特性选择增强方法，二者均可在训练前测量。
👤 Katsumi Ibaraki, David Chiang 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.13797]
REFLECT: Role-Aware Detection of Sentiment, Emotion, Politeness, and Formality
REFLECT：角色感知的情感、情绪、礼貌与正式性检测
Detecting communicative meaning in dialogue requires modeling not only lexical content but also the participant-role structures through which sentiment, emotion, politeness, and formality (SEPF) are expressed. Existing approaches rely on contextual language representations while leaving semantic roles implicit. We introduce a role-aware transformer architecture that maps the four SEPF dimensions to theory-grounded Semantic Role Labeling (SRL) configurations, incorporating these configurations directly into the transformer's self-attention mechanism. Role-aware attention models non-directional internal states (sentiment and emotion), while directional role-aware attention captures initiator-to-recipient relations for external cues (politeness and formality). The resulting role-aware representations are fused with contextual embeddings through a learned gate that adaptively weights how much role information each input contributes, rather than appending semantic roles as an auxiliary feature vector. Across four benchmark datasets, the framework yields statistically reliable gains over strong BERT-based baselines for emotion, politeness, and formality, with gains concentrated in theoretically predicted cases such as low-frequency, complex emotions and highly polite expressions; sentiment improvements are marginal and non-significant, suggesting that sentiment is conveyed more through lexical choice than participant-role structure. Additional evaluation on real-world data demonstrates that the framework improves both predictive performance and model traceability. These results demonstrate that linguistically grounded modifications to transformer self-attention improve the detection of communicative meaning, generalize beyond benchmark datasets to real-world mental-health data, and yield traceable, role-grounded explanations that extend beyond predictive performance to interpretability.
中文翻译
对话中语用意义的检测不仅需要建模词汇内容，还需考虑参与者角色结构，因为情感、情绪、礼貌和正式性（SEPF）正是通过该结构表达的。现有方法依赖上下文语言表示，但隐含了语义角色。我们提出一种角色感知的Transformer架构，将四个SEPF维度映射到理论基础坚实的语义角色标注（SRL）配置，并将这些配置直接融入Transformer的自注意力机制中。角色感知注意力模型捕捉非方向性内部状态（如情感和情绪），而方向性角色感知注意力则捕获从发起者到接收者的外部线索关系（如礼貌和正式性）。由此产生的角色感知表示通过一个可学习门控机制与上下文嵌入融合，自适应地权衡每个输入的角色信息贡献，而非将语义角色作为辅助特征向量附加。在四个基准数据集上，该框架在情绪、礼貌和正式性检测上均显著优于强大的BERT基线，且增益集中在理论上预期的情况，如低频复杂情绪和高度礼貌表达；情感改进微弱且不显著，表明情感更多通过词汇选择传达，而非参与者角色结构。在真实世界数据上的额外评估显示，该框架提升了预测性能和模型可追溯性。结果表明，对Transformer自注意力进行语言学基础的改造，能有效提升语用意义检测能力，泛化至真实世界心理健康数据，并提供可追溯、基于角色的解释，超越单纯预测性能，增强可解释性。
👤 Sangpil Youm, Annabel Stocks Natalias, Hayden Heninger, Leah Jones, Xiaoyang Jin et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.13794]
Not Just an Evaluation: A Case Study of Discourse Particle Sensitivity in Language Models
不仅仅是评估：语言模型对话语标记敏感性的案例研究
Discourse particles subtly shape the flow of discourse by reflecting conversational context and interlocutor attitudes, often leaving the truth-conditional meaning untouched. They are also often polyfunctional, with distinct senses which partially overlap with other particles; for example, the exclusive use of English "just" and "only" ("John only/just drinks water"). Despite the frequency of these words in natural language, they have gone largely unstudied due to the subtle nature of their effects. We investigate LLM sensitivity to "just" and "only" in natural conversations using behavioral and mechanistic analyses. First, we find that LLMs and humans similarly prefer the presence of these particles in context, and both are more sensitive to "just" compared to "only". Second, when sampling from LLMs with and without these particles, we find LLMs consistently rate generations as more likely, given their source context, demonstrating their particle sensitivity. In both experiments, instruction tuning increases sensitivity. Finally, we localize particle-sensitive units within LLMs and find the overlap between "just" and "only" is asymmetric: "only" units transfer selectively to exclusive "just", while all "just" units transfer to "only" equally well regardless of sense, suggesting LLM lexical-level particle representations are partially organized by sense. We release our code, sense-labeled dialogue stimuli, and human preference annotations at this https URL .
中文翻译
话语标记通过反映对话背景和对话者态度，微妙地影响话语流，通常不改变命题真值条件。它们也常具有多重功能，不同含义部分重叠；例如，英语中“just”和“only”的专有使用（如“John only/just drinks water”）。尽管这些词在自然语言中频繁出现，但由于其效果细微，长期缺乏系统研究。我们通过行为和机制分析，探究大语言模型（LLM）对“just”和“only”的敏感性。首先，我们发现LLM与人类在上下文中对这些标记的存在偏好相似，且对“just”比“only”更敏感。其次，在有无这些标记的情况下采样LLM生成内容，发现LLM始终认为生成内容在源上下文中更可能，证明其具备标记敏感性。在两项实验中，指令微调均增强了敏感性。最后，我们定位了LLM中对标记敏感的单元，发现“just”与“only”的重叠呈不对称性：“only”单元仅选择性地转移到“just”的排他性用法，而所有“just”单元无论语义如何，都能等效迁移到“only”。这表明LLM在词汇层面的话语标记表征部分按语义组织。我们已公开代码、带语义标注的对话刺激材料及人类偏好标注。
👤 William Sheffield, Jiahui Chen, Amy Zhang, Alex Warstadt, Junyi Jessy Li et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

Enhancing Legal Reasoning in Pre-trained Language Models via Knowledge Graph-Guided Multi-Task Pre-training
通过知识图谱引导的多任务预训练增强预训练语言模型的法律推理能力
Legal language understanding requires reasoning over semi-structured regulatory documents, yet standard pre-training objectives do not explicitly target this structural capability. Because current legal language models rely solely on masked language modeling, they treat statutory text as flat token sequences and ignore the underlying hierarchical constraints. To bridge this gap, we present a multi-task pre-training approach for German traffic law (StVO). Our approach combines three complementary objectives: Next Sentence Prediction (NSP), Knowledge Graph (KG) Guided Clustering (CLU), and Knowledge Graph (KG) Aware Paragraph Classification (CLS). This approach explicitly encodes structured legal knowledge directly into the model weights. This encoding enables zero-shot legal reasoning at inference time and removes the need for discrete graph traversal. We evaluate our approach across nine Transformer-based encoders and 324 experimental runs. On synthetic StVO question answering, our models demonstrate consistent relative improvements between 10.3% and 155.0% over baselines. Furthermore, relative to the established LexGLUE baselines (Chalkidis et al., 2022), our models improve over prior results on four of seven legal benchmarks. It also achieves consistent zero-shot transfer gains between 3.4% and 15.8% on Austrian driving regulations. These experimental results indicate that our training approach generalizes successfully beyond the initial training legal domain.
中文翻译
法律语言理解需要对半结构化的法规文档进行推理，但当前的标准预训练目标并未明确针对这种结构性能力。由于现有法律语言模型仅依赖掩码语言建模，将法规文本视为扁平的词元序列，忽略了底层的层级约束。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了针对德语交通法（StVO）的多任务预训练方法。该方法结合三种互补目标：下一句子预测（NSP）、知识图谱（KG）引导聚类（CLU）和知识图谱（KG）感知段落分类（CLS）。该方法将结构化法律知识显式编码进模型权重中，使模型在推理时实现零样本法律推理，无需离散图遍历。我们在九个基于Transformer的编码器上进行了324次实验。在合成StVO问答任务中，我们的模型相对于基线表现出10.3%至155.0%的一致相对提升。此外，相较于已建立的LexGLUE基线（Chalkidis et al., 2022），我们的模型在七个法律基准中的四个上优于先前结果。同时，在奥地利驾驶法规上实现了3.4%至15.8%的一致零样本迁移增益。实验结果表明，该训练方法成功泛化至初始训练法律领域之外。
👤 Ibrahim Siddig, Munir Georges 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

Verifiable Retrieval for Legal Reasoning through Structure-driven Knowledge Graph
通过结构驱动的知识图谱实现法律推理的可验证检索
Any automation within reliability-critical contexts such as medicine or law has to be transparent and verifiable. Despite their advantages, current LLM-based systems do not meet these requirements due to their inherent black-box nature. Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) attempts to ground the generative process in the relevant interpretable documents, thereby reducing the risk of AI 'hallucinations' and potentially enabling verification of the initial generation context. However, as the number and size of potential sources increases, naive retrieval approaches tend to perform worse, ignoring critical context elements. In this paper we aim to tackle this problem in the context of German Legal Code. [2610.13678]
Retractions Revisited: A Replication, Extension, and Mechanistic Evaluation of Linguistic Obfuscation Effects in Fraudulent Science
重新审视撤稿：欺诈性科学中语言模糊效应的复制、扩展与机制评估
Prior work suggests fraudulent scientists write their articles with higher rates of linguistic obfuscation than genuine scientists, with a possible mechanism suggesting obfuscation makes research more costly for a reader to analyze. This result has not undergone additional empirical scrutiny in the open science era, nor have other psychological mechanisms been evaluated. The current replication and extension addressed this opportunity by comparing 800 fraudulent and 800 genuine control papers (2013-2025). The results supported the linguistic obfuscation hypothesis, suggesting fraudulent papers were more obfuscated than genuine ones. A parallel mediation model evaluated three competing mechanisms, finding that reality monitoring, as measured through decreased rates of adjectives, best explained the link between fraudulent science and linguistic obfuscation. Cross-validated detection accuracy using obfuscation features (60.7%) and an elastic net of over 100 language dimensions (71.6%) were significantly greater than chance. Implications for scientific fraud and psychology of language research are discussed.
中文翻译
先前研究认为，欺诈性科学家撰写的论文比真实科学家的论文具有更高的语言模糊程度，可能的机制是模糊性使读者分析研究的成本更高。这一结果在开放科学时代尚未经过进一步的实证检验，其他心理机制也未被评估。本研究通过比较800篇欺诈性论文和800篇真实对照论文（2013–2025年）进行了复制与扩展。结果支持语言模糊假设，表明欺诈性论文的模糊程度更高。一个并行中介模型评估了三种竞争性机制，发现通过形容词使用率下降所测量的现实监控最能解释欺诈科学与语言模糊之间的联系。利用模糊特征的交叉验证检测准确率为60.7%，而超过100个语言维度的弹性网络达到71.6%，均显著高于随机水平。文章讨论了对科学欺诈及语言心理学研究的启示。

[2610.13630]
Multi-Stream Attention Residuals
多流注意力残差
Attention Residuals (AttnRes) enables content-dependent retrieval over network depth, which can be viewed as single-stream softmax attention along the depth axis. Inspired by the success of multi-head attention along the sequence axis, we introduce Multi-Stream Attention Residuals (MSAR), an extension of AttnRes that generalizes this mechanism to multi-head depth attention, yielding substantial performance gains with minimal additional overhead. MSAR draws stream diversity from sequence context through multi-scale causal convolutions, allowing different heads to retrieve historical representations through distinct contextual views while preserving the integrity of the hidden representation. The retrieved streams are combined through lightweight adaptive mixing to generate the single-stream layer input. By retaining single-stream sources and expanding them on demand, MSAR supports multi-stream computation without requiring persistent multi-stream storage or communication. Across all 12 downstream benchmarks at the 18B and 28B MoE scales, MSAR consistently outperforms prior residual variants, including standard residual connections, manifold-constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC), and AttnRes. Scaling-law experiments yield an iso-loss compute speedup of $1.58\times$ over the standard residual baseline, compared with $1.35\times$ for AttnRes and $1.30\times$ for mHC. These results make MSAR a promising residual architecture for large-scale pre-training.
中文翻译
注意力残差（AttnRes）实现了随内容变化的网络深度检索，可视为沿深度轴的单流softmax注意力。受序列轴上多头注意力成功的启发，我们引入了多流注意力残差（MSAR），这是AttnRes的扩展，将该机制推广到多头深度注意力，带来显著性能提升且额外开销极小。MSAR通过多尺度因果卷积从序列上下文中获取流多样性，使不同头能够通过不同的上下文视角检索历史表示，同时保持隐藏表示的完整性。检索到的流通过轻量级自适应混合方式合并，生成单流层输入。通过保留单流源并在需要时扩展，MSAR支持多流计算，无需持久的多流存储或通信。在18B和28B MoE规模下的全部12个下游基准测试中，MSAR始终优于先前的残差变体，包括标准残差连接、流形约束超连接（mHC）和AttnRes。缩放定律实验显示，与标准残差基线相比，MSAR实现1.58倍的等损失计算加速，而AttnRes为1.35倍，mHC为1.30倍。这些结果使MSAR成为大规模预训练中极具前景的残差架构。
👤 Xiaohan Qin, Xiangdong Zhang, Tuo Dai, Sunan Zou, Huaijin Wu et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv

AMLworld-Compact: Importance-Weighted Downsampling for Cost-Effective LLM Evaluation and Error Diagnosis
AMLworld-Compact：面向成本效益的LLM评估与错误诊断的重要性加权降采样
Evaluating large language models (LLMs) on AMLworld's smallest test splits is costly because each of over a million targets requires a separate graph prompt. Sampling reduces this cost, but small balanced subsets change unweighted precision and F1. We introduce AMLworld-Compact, an evaluation set that retains every illicit test transaction and samples benign transactions from supervised-score strata. Its HT-Coreset protocol reduces evaluation targets by 271x and 611x on HI-Small and LI-Small. Horvitz-Thompson weights give unbiased full-test confusion-count estimates for fixed predictions; recall is exact, while precision and F1 are ratio estimates. In a held-out study using Graph Feature Preprocessor (GFP) features, one boosted-tree family defines strata for evaluating the other over 500 draws per configuration. At the released sizes, F1 RMSE is 0.00-2.58 percentage points versus 4.44-7.41 for uniform sampling with HT weights; conservative intervals remain broad. Under the evaluated graph prompts, seven open-weight LLMs have weighted precision near the predict-all-illicit baseline. In an outcome-stratified audit of 1,000 HI-Small few-shot outputs from one run per model, lexical checks pass for 84.5% of correct and 56.2% of incorrect binary verdicts, so these markers do not establish correctness. We release the evaluation sets, construction code, and audit tools at this https URL and this https URL .
中文翻译
在AMLworld最小测试集上评估大语言模型（LLMs）成本高昂，因为超过一百万个目标都需要独立的图提示。采样可降低成本，但小规模平衡子集会改变未加权的精确率和F1值。我们提出AMLworld-Compact，一个评估集，保留所有非法测试交易，并从监督评分分层中采样良性交易。其HT-Coreset协议在HI-Small和LI-Small上分别将评估目标减少271倍和611倍。Horvitz-Thompson权重提供固定预测下的全测试混淆计数无偏估计；召回率精确，而精确率和F1为比率估计。在使用图特征预处理器（GFP）特征的保留研究中，一个提升树族定义分层，用于在每种配置下对另一模型进行500次抽样评估。在发布规模下，F1 RMSE为0.00–2.58个百分点，而均匀采样加HT权重为4.44–7.41；保守区间仍较宽。在评估的图提示下，七个开源权重LLM的加权精确率接近全预测非法基线。在对1,000个HI-Small少样本输出的成果分层审计中，正确和错误二元判断的词汇检查通过率分别为84.5%和56.2%，因此这些标记无法确立正确性。我们在此处https URL和此处https URL发布评估集、构建代码和审计工具。

[2610.13605]
zip2zip++: Towards Inference-Time Token Compression with Minimal Quality Loss
zip2zip++：推理时令牌压缩的最小质量损失方法
Dynamic vocabularies built on-the-fly via Lempel-Ziv-Welch (LZW) compression let a language model represent the same text in fewer tokens, but prior work shows that this efficiency comes at a cost to reasoning and perplexity. We show that this quality gap is not a fundamental price of compression but can be substantially closed through targeted improvements to the compression layer and LZW codec, without modifying the underlying pretrained language model. Our analysis reveals four key issues: (1) LZW's compression algorithm creates an implicit conflict between the input embedding and the output embedding, a tension that a single shared embedding cannot resolve; (2) naively applying RoPE to LZW-compressed sequences ignores that merged tokens can span multiple tokens; (3) merging certain tokens (notably digits) disproportionately hurts downstream accuracy; and (4) dynamic vocabularies introduce an inherent segmentation ambiguity that confounds standard evaluation. We address these through decoupled input/output dynamic embedding layer, size-aware RoPE positioning, and a modified LZW codec supporting selective merges and multi-view segmentations. Across four models, our recipe improves perplexity on every corpus we evaluated as well as mathematical reasoning accuracy, closing roughly half the quality gap between zip2zip and the uncompressed baseline at a similar compression ratio on general text. This represents a significant step toward lossless adaptive-vocabulary compression for language models.
中文翻译
通过Lempel-Ziv-Welch（LZW）压缩动态构建的词汇表可在不改变文本内容的前提下减少语言模型的令牌数量，但先前研究表明这种效率会以推理能力和困惑度下降为代价。我们证明这一质量差距并非压缩的本质代价，而是可以通过对压缩层和LZW编解码器进行针对性改进来显著缩小，且无需修改预训练语言模型本身。我们的分析揭示了四个关键问题：（1）LZW压缩算法在输入嵌入与输出嵌入之间产生隐式冲突，单一共享嵌入无法解决；（2）简单地将RoPE应用于LZW压缩序列忽略了合并后的令牌可能跨越多个原始令牌；（3）合并某些令牌（尤其是数字）会不成比例地损害下游准确性；（4）动态词汇引入了固有的分词歧义，干扰标准评估。我们通过解耦的输入/输出动态嵌入层、感知大小的RoPE定位以及支持选择性合并和多视角分段的改进LZW编解码器解决了这些问题。在四个模型上，我们的方法在所有评估语料上的困惑度均得到提升，并改善了数学推理准确率，在与未压缩基线相似的压缩比下，关闭了约一半的质量差距。这标志着向语言模型无损自适应词汇压缩迈出了重要一步。
👤 Saibo Geng, Andrea Gentilini, Xinxian Ma, Nathan Ranchin, Yunzhen Yao et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.13583]
Measuring Artifact Reliance in Machine-Generated Text Detectors
测量机器生成文本检测器中的人工制品依赖性
Detectors of machine-generated text score almost perfectly on scientific-literature benchmarks, implying we can reliably distinguish with these detectors human and machine-generated documents. We looked at ten scientific-document subsets from six popular resources, and on several of them that apparent ease is a side effect of how the documents were put together. A logistic regression using 27 superficial features reached 0.833-0.997 macro-F1, and on the three arXiv subsets it outperformed every detector we evaluate with F1-scores 0.951-0.997. The strongest cue to the detector is the PDF extraction artifact: human texts retain their original line breaks, whereas generated texts are extracted as a single block of prose. This cue, together with three other formatting features, tell the classes apart at 0.996 AUC. To distinguish benchmarks that contain such a signal from detectors that rely on one, we introduce format counterfactuals: just re-wrapping a machine-written abstract without changing a single word reverses 20-27% of a public detector's correct decisions. Normalizing formatting before training reduces the generalization gap from 0.32 to 0.22. On format-clean benchmarks, it also raises out-of-domain macro-F1 from 0.33 to 0.65 for detectors trained on artifact-containing subsets.
中文翻译
机器生成文本检测器在科学文献基准测试中得分几乎完美，暗示我们能可靠地区分人类与机器生成的文档。我们分析了来自六个流行资源的十个科学文档子集，发现这种看似简单的区分实际上是文档构建方式的副产品。使用27个表面特征的逻辑回归模型达到了0.833–0.997的宏F1分数，且在三个arXiv子集上，其F1分数（0.951–0.997）超过了所有评估的检测器。对检测器影响最大的线索是PDF提取的人工制品：人类文本保留原始换行，而生成文本则被提取为单一连续段落。这一线索与其他三个格式特征结合，使分类准确率达到0.996 AUC。为区分包含此类信号的基准测试与依赖单一线索的检测器，我们引入了格式反事实：仅重新排版机器撰写的摘要而不更改任何单词，即可逆转公开检测器20%-27%的正确判断。训练前规范化格式可将泛化差距从0.32降至0.22。在格式清洁的基准测试上，它还将基于含人工制品子集训练的检测器在域外的宏F1从0.33提升至0.65。

A Better Metric Will Not Save You: What You Compare Beats How You Compare It
更好的度量无法拯救你：你比较的内容比如何比较更重要
Representational similarity measures (RSA, CKA, Procrustes) are chosen for the transformations they treat as irrelevant, and all of them take the set of conditions as given. That set is fixed by two decisions, which inputs are admitted and how they are grouped, both taken before the matrix a measure operates on exists: no invariance property reaches them. We measure what they are worth where the arrangement the conditions ought to have is known independently of the systems compared, the acoustic organisation of vowels, across fifteen languages and twenty-seven representations. Changing which categories are compared, at a fixed number of them, moves the gap between two representations by more than twice the effect being measured, while changing how many moves it not at all. In this setting rankings of models are identified under these choices, and rankings across languages are not.
中文翻译
表示相似性度量（RSA、CKA、Procrustes）的选择取决于它们视为无关的变换，且所有这些度量都以条件集合为给定前提。该集合由两个决策决定：哪些输入被接受，以及它们如何分组，这两个决策在度量所作用的矩阵存在之前就已经确定：没有任何不变性属性能影响它们。我们在已知条件应有排列的情况下进行测量，即跨十五种语言和二十七种表征的元音声学组织结构。在固定比较类别数量的前提下，改变比较的类别会使两个表征之间的差距变化超过被测量效应的两倍；而改变类别数量则几乎不产生影响。在此设定下，模型排名在这些选择下被识别，但跨语言的排名并不一致。

Towards Enriching Design-Time Models with Evidence from Simulation and Operational Activities
迈向设计时模型的丰富化：来自仿真与运行活动的证据
Cyber-Physical Systems are developed through multiple model based activities, comprising several stages of development. As the development progresses through simulation, experimentation, and operation, observations of system execution may reveal missing, uncertain, or invalid design knowledge. The path from execution results to persistent design-time enrichment, however, is often implicit. This paper addresses this challenge by proposing an evidence driven workflow for systematically transferring knowledge produced during simulation and operation back into design-time models. We demonstrate the workflow using an Influence Model, a design-time artefact used to represent how design artefacts and environmental factors jointly affect system response properties, using a sign-following robot use case. We show how simulation evidence can enrich Influence Models by supporting participant pruning decisions, interaction characterisation, influence-function abstraction, and validity-scope information. The application demonstrates the feasibility of the workflow for simulation-based evidence and its potential benefits to improve design-time reasoning and feedback in subsequent development iterations.

[2610.13398]
Towards the Roof of the World: NLP Progress in Tibetan Languages
走向世界之巅：藏语自然语言处理的发展进展
Tibetan, spoken by some seven million people on and around the Qinghai-Xizang Plateau, remains underrepresented in natural language processing (NLP). We present, to our knowledge, the first peer-reviewed systematic bibliometric survey of Tibetan NLP: 140 publications from 2004 to 2026 in the ACL Anthology and DBLP, annotated for venue type, research area, first-affiliation country, and resource release. Output has grown rapidly, with 84% of the publications appearing since 2018, and has expanded bottom-up along the NLP pipeline, from corpora and word segmentation through script, speech, and applications to large language models (LLMs). Researchers based in China first-authored 87% of the publications, which span all 25 represented research areas, whereas work from other countries concentrates on Classical Tibetan. We argue that this full-pipeline paradigm, realized under the compounded constraints of a complex script, dialectal diversity, and geographic remoteness, offers a reference model for other low-resource languages of the Global South. We also identify gaps in dialect coverage, responsible NLP, native and comparable evaluation, cross-community visibility, and resource release (only 29% of the publications release data, code, or models), and recommend open science, shared standards, and community participation for the LLM era.

[2610.13321]
Host-Aware Web Crawling for LLM Pretraining Data
面向LLM预训练数据的主机感知网络爬取
Web crawling is a major source of large language model (LLM) pretraining data, yet data-selection pipelines discard a large share of crawled pages. We investigate how a crawler should allocate its budget across and within hosts to collect more high-quality pretraining data. Combining document-level quality classifier scores, web-graph centrality metrics, and Common Crawl's per-host crawl coverage, we find that roughly half of the variation in classifier scores occurs between hosts. In the crawls we analyze, a few pages from a host also provide a useful estimate of the quality of its remaining pages. Common Crawl uses harmonic centrality, a graph centrality metric, to guide its crawl allocation. In our simulations, using centrality to choose which hosts to crawl yields limited gains over random ordering and performs worse for some classifiers. For deciding how deeply to crawl each host, sampling a few pages and then fetching more from hosts with higher-scoring samples collects several times more high-quality pages per fetch than uniform depth or centrality-based allocation. Finally, Common Crawl's observed per-host coverage has little correlation with the evaluated quality signals. We estimate that tens of millions of high-quality pages remain among known but uncrawled URLs in each crawl examined, with most concentrated on a small share of hosts.
中文翻译
网络爬取是大型语言模型（LLM）预训练数据的主要来源，但数据选择管道会丢弃大量已爬取页面。我们研究爬虫应如何在主机之间及主机内部分配预算，以收集更多高质量的预训练数据。结合文档级质量分类器得分、网页图中心性指标以及Common Crawl的每主机爬取覆盖率，我们发现约一半的分类器得分差异发生在主机之间。在所分析的爬取中，少数来自某主机的页面即可有效估计其剩余页面的质量。Common Crawl使用调和中心性这一图中心性度量来指导爬取分配。在模拟中，使用中心性选择要爬取的主机带来的增益有限，且对某些分类器表现更差。对于决定每个主机的爬取深度，先采样少量页面，再从高分样本所在主机获取更多页面，相比均匀深度或基于中心性的分配方式，每次抓取可收集数倍于高质量页面。最后，Common Crawl的实际每主机覆盖率与评估的质量信号几乎无相关性。我们估算，在每次分析的爬取中，仍有数千万高质量页面存在于已知但未被爬取的URL中，且大部分集中于少数主机。

How Reliable are AMR Parser Outputs? Uncertainty Estimation for AMR Parsing
AMR解析结果的可靠性如何？基于AMR解析的不确定性估计
Recent advances have significantly improved the performance of Abstract Meaning Representation (AMR) parsers, yet determining when their outputs can be trusted remains largely unexplored. This work presents the first study of uncertainty estimation for AMR parsers in a black-box setting using top-k beam-search candidates. Uncertainty is estimated from pairwise candidate similarities and evaluated using the Prediction Rejection Ratio. This work characterises the relationship between parsing performance and uncertainty, evaluates the reliability of beam candidate distributions, analyses the effect of evaluation metrics on uncertainty assessment, and identifies parsers that provide the strongest balance between performance and reliability. Experimental results show the value of uncertainty estimation as a complementary dimension for evaluating AMR parsers.

SharP: Soft and Hard Prompt-Guided Augmentation with LLM for Low Resource Fake News Detection
SharP：基于大语言模型的软硬提示引导增强用于低资源假新闻检测
Fake news detection under low resource conditions is challenged by the scarcity of labeled data and the difficulty of capturing subtle deceptive patterns. In this work, we focus on low resource fake news detection, where only a small labeled set is available for training, while evaluation is performed under a fixed and unchanged test protocol. This setting is motivated by costly, ambiguous fact-checking and emerging topics and domains that are poorly covered by existing benchmarks. Existing data augmentation methods, such as synonym substitution, generative adversarial networks, and large language model (LLM)-based generation, often produce fluent but overly generic content, lacking the task-specific relevance needed for accurate detection. To address this, we propose SharP, a boundary-aware text generation framework guided by soft and hard prompts, which leverages LLMs as tools adapted to produce task-specific synthetic samples. SharP employs a dual prompt mechanism: hard prompts provide structured guidance and factual constraints, while soft prompts introduce learned semantic patterns from the data. These two types of prompts are combined to guide the generation process, enabling the model to produce samples that closely align with the characteristics of news. To further enhance detection performance, we incorporate a boundary-aware strategy that steers generation toward areas where the classifier is less confident, helping to clarify subtle distinctions between real and fake content. In addition, we adopt a dual objective optimization that balances semantic alignment with the source data and classifier feedback. This encourages the generation of samples that are both domain-consistent and helpful for refining decision boundaries, ultimately improving model robustness and generalization in low resource fake news detection.

在低资源条件下，假新闻检测面临标注数据稀缺和捕捉细微欺骗模式困难的挑战。本文聚焦于仅有少量标注数据可用于训练、而测试协议保持不变的低资源假新闻检测场景。该设定源于昂贵且模糊的事实核查以及现有基准覆盖不足的新出现话题与领域。现有的数据增强方法如同义词替换、生成对抗网络及基于大语言模型（LLM）的生成，往往产生流畅但过于通用的内容，缺乏准确检测所需的任务相关性。为此，我们提出SharP，一种边界感知的文本生成框架，通过软提示和硬提示引导LLM生成任务特定的合成样本。SharP采用双提示机制：硬提示提供结构化指导和事实约束，软提示引入从数据中学习到的语义模式。两者结合以引导生成过程，使模型生成的样本更贴近新闻特征。为进一步提升检测性能，我们引入边界感知策略，引导生成向分类器置信度较低的区域，帮助区分真实与虚假内容。此外，采用双目标优化，在源数据语义一致性与分类器反馈之间取得平衡，鼓励生成既符合领域特性又有助于优化决策边界的样本，最终提升模型在低资源假新闻检测中的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

[2610.13134]
Neurograf v1: An End-to-End Romanian-First Language Model Family
Neurograf v1：首个以罗马尼亚语为核心的端到端语言模型家族
We present Neurograf v1, an experimental end-to-end Romanian-first language-model pipeline developed to investigate the feasibility of training language models from scratch for a lower-resource European language. The pipeline comprises RoSfera, a 635 GB Romanian corpus containing 175.7 million documents; an 80k byte-level BPE tokenizer; five decoder-only models ranging from 160M to 6B parameters, trained on 131 billion tokens; three instruction-tuned assistants; and two contrastively trained embedding models. Training and evaluation were conducted using computing infrastructure at ELI-NP/IFIN-HH. The model family exhibits consistent scaling of validation loss and increasing Romanian factual and cultural knowledge with model size. On the 1,000-question WWTBM benchmark, Neurograf-6B achieves 69.89% accuracy on Romanian-context questions, while Neurograf-3.2B reaches 67.38%. Compute-optimal analysis yields a parameter-scaling exponent of approximately 0.423. Neurograf v1 serves as an experimental demonstrator rather than a production system. The study identifies practical challenges involving diacritic preservation, corpus quality, data composition, and tokenization efficiency, while demonstrating the feasibility of developing Romanian-first base models, instruction-following assistants, and semantic embedding models within a unified training pipeline.

Broadcast archives offer scale and diversity for self-supervised learning (SSL) but contain substantial noisy, non-speech, and multilingual content. Prior work typically relies on voice activity detection or language identification (LID) to extract speech, ensuring presence rather than quality. Whether and how to filter unlabeled speech by quality for SSL, particularly from noisy broadcasts, remains underexplored. We present a large-scale Finnish SSL study starting from 2.1M hours of unconstrained broadcast audio. [2610.13041]
RAMP: Recurrent Adaptive Mixed Precision for Efficient Prefill in Recurrent-Depth Language Models
RAMP：用于循环深度语言模型高效预填充的递归自适应混合精度
Recurrent-depth language models improve inference-time reasoning by repeatedly applying a shared network to refine internal representations, but the additional recurrent computation also increases inference cost. This overhead is particularly pronounced during long-context prefill, where the full input sequence is repeatedly processed across recurrent depth. Weight-and-activation quantization offers a natural way to accelerate this computation through lower-precision arithmetic, yet repeatedly applying quantized computation introduces numerical perturbations that accumulate along the recurrent trajectory. We systematically analyze how such errors propagate across recurrent depth and show that quantization errors introduced earlier are attenuated by more subsequent recurrent steps and therefore have a smaller effect on the final representation. Matched-budget experiments strongly support this prediction, showing that placing quantized computation earlier in recurrent depth consistently yields the best fidelity. Based on this insight, we introduce RAMP (Recurrent Adaptive Mixed Precision), which performs early recurrent refinement using low-precision weight-and-activation computation and restores high precision for terminal refinement. Across context lengths up to 4K, RAMP preserves near-full-precision prefill quality while achieving up to 1.16x end-to-end first-prefill speedup on an NVIDIA H100, corresponding to a 13.9% latency reduction.
中文翻译
循环深度语言模型通过反复应用共享网络来改进推理过程中的内部表示，但额外的循环计算也增加了推理成本。这种开销在长上下文预填充阶段尤为明显，因为整个输入序列需在循环深度中重复处理。权重与激活量化可通过低精度算术自然加速此计算，但重复应用量化计算会引入数值扰动，并沿循环轨迹累积。我们系统分析了此类误差在循环深度中的传播机制，发现早期引入的量化误差会被后续更多循环步骤衰减，因此对最终表示的影响较小。匹配预算实验强烈支持这一预测，显示在循环深度早期使用量化计算能持续获得最佳保真度。基于此洞察，我们提出RAMP（递归自适应混合精度），即在早期循环精炼中使用低精度权重与激活计算，并在最终精炼阶段恢复高精度。在长达4K的上下文长度下，RAMP在保持接近全精度预填充质量的同时，在NVIDIA H100上实现了最高1.16倍的端到端首次预填充速度提升，对应13.9%的延迟降低。
👤 Ke Wan, Chen Chen 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.12841]
Fear Is Not Always Fear: Variation in Emotion Label Equivalence Across Datasets
恐惧并不总是恐惧：跨数据集情绪标签等价性的差异
Emotion classifiers are routinely applied beyond their training data, on the assumption that same-named labels such as fear or surprise mean the same thing across resources. We test this assumption for five labels shared by GoEmotions, XED, and BRIGHTER, comparing models on identical inputs and under cross-corpus transfer. On the same texts, one fear classifier flags 71% as fearful and another 8%, yet the two largely agree on how the texts rank. Across 30 resource-label comparisons, training-label prevalence explains about half of this kind of scale mismatch, and a fitted intercept removes most of it. What recalibration cannot repair is larger: transferred models lose 55% of native discrimination in aggregate, agreement in ranking between classifiers is only weakly related to that loss, and for surprise the resources disagree about which texts express the emotion. The choice of training resource can therefore change conclusions: on two external tweet collections, BRIGHTER- and XED-trained classifiers give opposite answers about which shows more surprise, even after rank normalization, with intervals excluding zero in both directions. A shared emotion label name does not establish an interchangeable measurement target.

[2610.12802]
People Making Conversation: Evaluating How Naturally Speech Models Converse
人们在对话中：评估语音模型对话的自然程度
A conversational speech model has to decide, at every pause, whether to speak. Existing evaluations test boundary detection, policy adherence, or aggregate statistics, not this decision. We place real-time speech systems at 427 pauses and 482 turn ends from 176 unscripted conversations in Seamless Interaction, give each the conversation so far, and score what it generates against what the person did: whether and how soon it speaks at a pause; its latency, length, and wording at a turn end; and whether it can answer a question about the preceding 30 to 120 seconds. Across six systems, the hosted APIs (GPT Realtime, Gemini 3.1 Flash Live, Grok Voice) respond at almost every pause whichever endpointer they use; only their latency, about 1 to 4 s with silence-based endpointers and about 8 s with semantic turn detection, decides whether the words land inside the pause. The full-duplex models (PersonaPlex, Moshi) are the only systems that regularly stay silent and the only ones that interrupt within a human reaction time. Hosted systems reply longer than the person. Given the history as text, hosted systems recall 41 to 60% of conversation facts under a Claude Opus 5.5 judge and 19 to 26% under a strict word matcher; hearing it as audio, full-duplex models recall 17 to 22% and 12 to 15%. We release the labels, points, harness, and every output.

对话式语音模型必须在每个停顿处决定是否发言。现有评估方法测试边界检测、策略遵守或聚合统计指标，但并未真正考察这一决策过程。我们在Seamless Interaction中的176段非脚本化对话中，于427个停顿点和482个回合结束点部署实时语音系统，向每个系统提供当前对话历史，并根据人类实际行为评分：在停顿处是否以及何时发言；在回合结束时的延迟、长度和措辞；以及能否回答前30至120秒内容的相关问题。在六种系统中，托管API（GPT Realtime、Gemini 3.1 Flash Live、Grok Voice）几乎在每个停顿处都会响应，具体响应与否取决于所使用的端点检测方式：使用基于静音的端点检测时延迟约为1至4秒，而使用语义回合检测时则约为8秒。全双工模型（PersonaPlex、Moshi）是唯一能经常保持沉默且唯一能在人类反应时间内打断的系统。托管系统回复时间普遍长于人类。在仅以文本形式提供历史的情况下，托管系统在Claude Opus 5.5裁判下回忆起41%至60%的对话事实，而在严格字面匹配器下为19%至26%；若以音频形式听取，则全双工模型分别回忆起17%至22%和12%至15%。我们已公开标注数据、评分点、评测工具及所有输出结果。

[2610.12701]
Activating the Annotator: Operationalising Lived Experience in Data Annotation
激活标注者：在数据标注中实现生活经验的操作化
Perspectivist NLP has established that annotator positionality shapes labels for subjective tasks, but the field still lacks methods for operationalising it. Positionality is typically operationalised as a demographic form completed at recruitment: a static attribute recorded once and treated as a covariate, on the assumption that the knowledge it indexes is active when the annotator reads an item. This paper treats that assumption as a design problem and asks whether lived experience can instead be primed by the task itself. We introduce AXLE, a pre-annotation stage in which a scenario vignette anchors a short, axis-guided conversation that is synthesised into a first-person micro-narrative and returned to the annotator before labelling begins. In a 2x2 between-subjects study (104 annotators, 30 items, 1,040 annotations) on ambiguous hate speech spanning immigration, religion and gender, elicitation was associated with higher identity resonance and higher sensitivity to harm. It also increased label entropy and lowered inter-annotator agreement: the stage appears to surface plurality rather than manufacture consensus, which distinguishes it from annotator training, whose success criterion is convergence.
中文翻译
视角主义自然语言处理已证实标注者的立场会影响主观任务的标注结果，但该领域仍缺乏有效的方法来操作化这一概念。目前通常将立场操作化为招募时填写的人口统计表格——一种静态属性，仅记录一次并作为协变量处理，假设其所指的知识在标注时是活跃的。本文将此假设视为设计问题，探讨是否可通过任务本身来激发生活经验。我们提出AXLE，一种预标注阶段：通过情景小品锚定一段轴向引导的简短对话，合成第一人称微型叙事后返回给标注者，再开始正式标注。在一项2×2组间实验（104名标注者，30个样本，共1040次标注）中，针对涉及移民、宗教和性别等议题的模糊仇恨言论任务，该方法与更高的身份共鸣和更强的伤害敏感度相关。同时，它也提高了标签熵并降低了标注者间一致性：该阶段似乎揭示了多元性而非制造共识，这与以收敛为目标的标注培训形成鲜明对比。
👤 Sheza Munir, Ramaravind Kommiya Mothilal, Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed 📅 2026/10/08 🔗 arXiv
[2610.12580]
CLUE: A Contrastive Learning Framework for Unsupervised Event Type Induction and Annotation
CLUE：一种用于无监督事件类型归纳与标注的对比学习框架
Event ontology is the foundational framework for defining events in a dataset. Event type induction generally involves domain experts who define guidelines for creating event types and their names based on the dataset's domain and scope. To alleviate the need for manual event ontology creation, we propose the CLUE framework for event type induction, naming, and annotation. Our main contributions in this work are: 1) designing a novel joint embedding representation for expressing both the verb-centric view and the semantic view of events; 2) event type induction using contrastive loss guided clustering; 3) event naming and refinement. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. Our code is available at this https URL .

[2610.13840]
Studying Comics at Scale with Artificial Intelligence: The PAGE Framework and Three Studies of American Comics and Japanese Manga
利用人工智能大规模研究漫画：PAGE框架与三项关于美漫与日漫的研究
Comics are one of the great visual records of the twentieth century: hundreds of thousands of pages in which a mass readership saw its leaders, its wars and its heroes. Most of this record has never been studied as a whole; comics scholarship proceeds one book at a time. We argue that artificial intelligence, including generative models, can change this to the benefit of the medium itself. We propose PAGE, a framework that makes artificial intelligence an instrument for comics research. It parses every scan into panels, text and a bibliographic record; annotates each page with a vision-language model that follows a written codebook, as a human coder would; grounds samples of these readings in human annotation; and explains change across an archive, with estimates controlled for publisher and genre. We demonstrate the framework on 3,929 American comic books (1938--1956) and 87 volumes of Japanese manga, in three studies, each resting on a different capability. The first pairs text search with visual reading to recover how a mass medium portrayed political power. The second measures the page's visual form across an industry and recovers the hand of individual artists. The third follows who speaks across two comics traditions, checked against human annotation. Beyond research, the same instruments can make archives searchable by what is drawn, open comics to blind and low-vision readers, and help preserve fragile collections and credit uncredited artists. We outline a research agenda in which artificial intelligence strengthens the study, preservation and reach of the medium.

OpenPA: Forced-alignment-free Multi-granular Pronunciation Assessment with Mixed-transcript Adaptation

Recent advances in automatic pronunciation assessment (APA) have driven a shift from scripted to free-form assessment. However, existing methods often rely on multi-stage pipelines with forced alignment and separate feature extractors, limiting their practicality across response settings. In light of this, we propose OpenPA, a novel APA framework that leverages Whisper's cross-attention mechanism to jointly predict word- and utterance-level pronunciation scores without forced alignment. Through mixed-transcript adaptation and adaptive substitution weighting, OpenPA accommodates heterogeneous transcripts across closed- and open-response settings within a single model. An additional cross-task evaluation on oral-cancer speech suggests its potential applicability beyond non-native speech.

[2610.13447]
Oído: Open-Vocabulary Speech Recognition on a $5 Microcontroller without a Neural Accelerator
Oído：在无神经加速器的5美元微控制器上实现开放词汇英语语音识别
Speech recognition on low-cost microcontrollers is, in practice, limited to keyword spotting or a fixed list of commands. We present Oído, an inference engine that runs open-vocabulary English speech recognition entirely on an ESP32-S3 (240 MHz dual-core Xtensa LX7, 512 KB of SRAM, 8 MB of PSRAM, 16 MB of flash, no neural accelerator). Oído executes a 13M-parameter Conformer-CTC model with int8 weights, per-frame dynamic int8 activations and int8 attention on the chip's SIMD instructions, with a schedule designed around flash and PSRAM bandwidth. On LibriSpeech test-clean/test-other the on-device arithmetic reaches 3.70%/8.23% word error rate (WER), within 0.12 points of full precision, and 3.33%/7.15% with an on-chip 1.3M-parameter language model; a transducer head gives 2.99%/6.67% and an int4 variant fits in 8.3 MB. This is 2.3-2.8x fewer errors than the vendor's recognizer on the same chip, and fewer than Whisper this http URL and Moonshine tiny in full precision on a laptop, including in real noise and reverberation. Measured on a physical board, the firmware runs at 1.97x the duration of the audio on one core, with transcripts identical to an instruction-exact emulator: accurate, but not yet real time (a 4 s command is transcribed about 8.7 s after it ends). The two-core schedule gives wrong results on silicon and is disabled. A streaming variant shows partial text while the speaker talks and delivers the final text 4.1-11.1 s after clips of 3.6-10.6 s (7.2-22.4 s in utterance mode) at 4.86%/11.03% WER; a Spanish model makes 34-60% fewer errors than multilingual Whisper tiny. Code and models are released.
中文翻译
低成本微控制器上的语音识别在实践中通常仅限于关键词检测或固定命令列表。我们提出了Oído，一个完全运行在ESP32-S3（240 MHz双核Xtensa LX7，512 KB SRAM，8 MB PSRAM，16 MB闪存，无神经加速器）上的推理引擎，实现了开放词汇英语语音识别。Oído在芯片的SIMD指令上执行1300万参数的Conformer-CTC模型，使用int8权重、每帧动态int8激活和int8注意力，调度设计充分考虑了闪存和PSRAM带宽。在LibriSpeech test-clean/test-other数据集上，设备端计算的词错误率（WER）达到3.70%/8.23%，与全精度模型相差仅0.12个百分点；结合片上130万参数的语言模型后，WER降至3.33%/7.15%；采用转换器头可进一步降低至2.99%/6.67%，而int4版本仅需8.3 MB内存。该性能优于同一芯片上厂商提供的识别器2.3-2.8倍，且在笔记本电脑上以全精度运行时也优于Whisper tiny和Moonshine tiny，包括在真实噪声和混响环境下的表现。实测表明，固件在单核上运行速度为音频时长的1.97倍，转录结果与指令精确模拟器一致：准确但尚未实时（4秒指令约在结束后8.7秒完成转录）。双核调度在硅片上产生错误，已禁用。流式版本可在说话过程中显示部分文本，最终文本在3.6-10.6秒片段后4.1-11.1秒内输出（话语模式下为7.2-22.4秒），WER为4.86%/11.03%；西班牙语模型比多语言Whisper tiny减少34-60%错误。代码和模型已公开发布。

Revisiting Accent Classification with DistilHuBERT: Speaker-Disjoint Evaluation and Cross-Corpus Generalization
重新审视基于DistilHuBERT的口音分类：说话人独立评估与跨语料库泛化
Recent accent classification systems can achieve high accuracy in standard in-corpus evaluations. However, when speakers overlap between the training and test sets, such results may overestimate model robustness compared with real-world deployment scenarios. This study revisits the robustness of DistilHuBERT-based accent classification by using L2-ARCTIC and multiple external English accent datasets. The comparison covers file-level and speaker-disjoint splits, along with balanced external-data training and teacher-student pseudo-labeling. The large performance gap between in-corpus and external evaluations suggests that file-level evaluation substantially overestimates performance, while speaker-disjoint evaluation can provide a more realistic estimate of the generalization to unseen speakers. In this study, models trained on external data alone do not reliably generalize to L2-ARCTIC. Among the tested configurations, balanced external-data training offers the most promising supervised setting. The pseudo-labeling experiments further indicate that confidence-based filtering alone is insufficient under domain mismatch. Overall, this study suggests that robust accent classification evaluation should simultaneously include speaker-disjoint evaluation and cross-corpus testing.

[2610.13432]
Does Financial Speech Need Continual Learning Regularization? A Study of EWC and mini-KFAC on Earnings Call ASR
财务语音是否需要持续学习正则化？关于EWC与mini-KFAC在财报电话会议ASR中的研究
Earnings call transcription presents a realistic continual learning scenario where Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems are updated with new recordings but risk catastrophic forgetting of previously learned financial speech patterns. Adapting Whisper-small from a general-domain speech corpus to the Earnings22 benchmark reveals the opposite: all evaluated methods exhibit improvement on held-out financial speech from the same domain during adaptation rather than forgetting. This task complementarity makes regularization counterproductive. Nevertheless, when regularization is warranted, a layer-wise mini-Kronecker-Factored Approximate Curvature (mini-KFAC) approximation seems to achieve lower word error rates (WERs) and more stable optimization than standard diagonal Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC). [2610.13337]
Exploring Attention Sinks in Text-to-Image Transformers from Diffusion and Autoregressive Models
从扩散与自回归模型探索文本到图像Transformer中的注意力黑洞
Attention sinks are tokens or positions that consistently receive disproportionate attention mass, often beyond what their semantic content would suggest. Despite extensive study in large language models, their behavior and functional relevance in text-to-image (T2I) Transformers remain largely unexplored. To fill this gap and better understand how text-to-image Transformers allocate and route attention, we explore this phenomenon of text-to-image transformers across bidirectional diffusion models and causal autoregressive models. Specifically, we explore whether attention sinks exist, where they emerge, and how they functionally influence generation. Through systematic attention analysis and targeted interventions, we identify that attention sinks emerge in both bidirectional diffusion and autoregressive models but exhibit distinct patterns. The autoregressive model concentrates attention on the first and global-scale tokens, whereas the bidirectional models develop sinks near modality boundaries, within text regions, and at spatial corners. Moreover, these patterns vary across generation stages and are localized to a sparse subset of layers and heads. To assess their functional relevance, we perturb sink-related heads and token positions. It turns out that perturbing sink-ranked heads produces larger average changes than non-sink comparisons, while some sink-position perturbations have limited effects on bidirectional model scores. We further introduce register tokens to redistribute attention sinks and find that several configurations preserve or modestly improve generation performance. Overall, attention sinks are architecture-, inference-stage-, and component-dependent phenomena, providing a basis for mechanistic analysis and principled attention design in text-to-image transformers.
中文翻译
注意力黑洞是指某些标记或位置持续接收不成比例的注意力权重，其程度常超出其语义内容所应带来的影响。尽管在大型语言模型中已有广泛研究，但在文本到图像（T2I）Transformer中的行为及其功能相关性仍基本未被探索。为填补这一空白并更好地理解T2I Transformer如何分配和路由注意力，我们系统性地研究了双向扩散模型与因果自回归模型中的注意力黑洞现象。具体探究了注意力黑洞是否存在、其出现位置以及功能影响。通过系统的注意力分析与针对性干预，我们发现注意力黑洞在双向扩散模型和自回归模型中均会出现，但表现出不同模式：自回归模型将注意力集中在首标记和全局尺度标记上，而双向模型则在模态边界附近、文本区域内部以及空间角落形成黑洞。此外，这些模式随生成阶段变化，并局限于稀疏的层与注意力头。为评估其功能相关性，我们扰动与黑洞相关的注意力头和标记位置。结果表明，扰动黑洞排名高的注意力头会产生更大的平均变化，而部分黑洞位置的扰动对双向模型得分影响有限。我们进一步引入注册标记以重新分配注意力黑洞，发现若干配置能保持甚至适度提升生成性能。总体而言，注意力黑洞是架构、推理阶段和组件依赖的现象，为T2I Transformer的机制分析与有原则的注意力设计提供了基础。
👤 Binglei Li, Mengping Yang, Zhiyu Tan, Wenxu Qian, Junping Zhang et al. 📅 2026/10/09 🔗 arXiv
[2610.13252]
Vision Transformers Learn Word Meaning from Images Alone
视觉Transformer仅从图像中学习词语含义
Can a vision model learn what words mean from pixels alone? We show that the two largest DINOv3 vision transformers organize words rendered in images by word meaning, despite being pretrained using self-supervision on images only, without paired captions, class labels, or other language supervision. We derive word representations from embedding differences between word images and matched controls, then test whether semantically related words are neighbors within each model's embedding space, while controlling for spelling similarity. We find that brand names in the same industry group together, as do nouns from the same semantic domain. Country-capital pairs exhibit consistent vector offsets, similarly to word embeddings trained on text. These word representations also align with the representations of the visual object categories they name. DINOv3-H and DINOv3-7B are, to our knowledge, the first self-supervised vision models to show substantial semantic structure for rendered words. Comparisons with text and speech encoders reveal shared structure among representations of the same words. Contrastive alignment with either encoder further strengthened DINOv3-H's word-level semantic structure, while leaving the other finetuned vision models largely unchanged. Image-only training can therefore teach vision models not just what words look like, but also how their meanings relate.
中文翻译
视觉模型能否仅从像素中学习词语含义？我们发现，最大的两个DINOv3视觉Transformer尽管仅通过图像自监督预训练，未使用配对标题、类别标签或其他语言监督，仍能根据词语含义组织图像中的文字。我们通过词图像与匹配对照组之间的嵌入差异推导词表示，测试语义相关的词是否在各模型的嵌入空间中彼此邻近，同时控制拼写相似性。结果发现，同一行业内的品牌名称聚集在一起，同一语义领域的名词也形成聚类。国家首都对表现出一致的向量偏移，类似于文本训练的词嵌入。这些词表示还与它们所命名的视觉对象类别表示对齐。据我们所知，DINOv3-H和DINOv3-7B是首批展示渲染词语显著语义结构的自监督视觉模型。与文本和语音编码器的对比揭示了相同词语表示间的共享结构。与任一编码器进行对比对齐后，DINOv3-H的词级语义结构进一步增强，而其他微调视觉模型基本保持不变。因此，仅通过图像训练，视觉模型不仅能学会词语外观，还能理解其意义之间的关联。

[2610.13247]
What Do Audio-Language Judges of Generated Music Listen For? Four Probes with Controls from Expert Ratings
生成音乐的音视频语言评判者关注什么？基于专家评分对照的四项探测实验
Open audio-language models are used as judges of generated music: given a clip and its text prompt, the model rates the clip, and the judge is validated by how well its ratings correlate with expert ratings. The correlation does not show what the judge responds to: the music, the text prompt, or the way the question is asked. We propose four probes that test each of these, three of them read against a control computed from the human ratings: removing the audio, replacing the clip with another model's clip for the same prompt, swapping the order of two clips in pairwise judging, and rewording the instruction. We apply them to five open judges on all 2,748 clips of MusicEval, and the last two to the SongEval songs. Without audio, no judge exceeds what the prompt alone predicts from the human ratings, and for prompt alignment every judge falls below it. With audio, every judge follows the clip it hears when rating prompt alignment, two do when rating musical quality, and the strongest judge's quality score tracks the experts' alignment rating more than their quality rating. The pairwise verdicts of the four smaller judges follow the position of a clip in the pair (its slot) rather than its quality on both rating sets. For musical quality, rewording the instruction moves the correlation of most judges as much as changing the judge. MusicEval's prompt assignment bounds any prompt-only judge at the model level. We recommend reporting the four probes with every music judge.
中文翻译
开放的音视频语言模型被用作生成音乐的评判者：给定一段音频和其文本提示，模型对音频进行评分，评判者的有效性通过其评分与专家评分的相关性来验证。然而，这种相关性并未揭示评判者实际响应的是音乐本身、文本提示，还是问题的提问方式。我们提出了四项探测实验，分别测试这三个方面，并以人类评分计算出的对照组为基准：移除音频、用同一提示下另一模型生成的音频替换原音频、在成对评判中交换两个音频的位置、重写指令。我们将这些探测应用于 MusicEval 的全部 2,748 段音频上的五个开放评判者，以及 SongEval 歌曲中的后两项探测。在无音频条件下，没有任何评判者的表现超过仅由提示预测的人类评分水平；在提示一致性方面，所有评判者均低于该基准。有音频时，每个评判者在评估提示一致性时都遵循其所听音频的表现，其中两个在评估音乐质量时也如此，最强评判者的质量评分甚至比专家的一致性评分更贴近其质量评分。四个较小评判者的成对判断结果更依赖于音频在配对中的位置（槽位），而非其实际质量。对于音乐质量，重写指令的影响程度与更换评判者相当。MusicEval 的提示分配限制了仅基于提示的评判者在模型层面的表现上限。我们建议在报告每个音乐评判者时都附上这四项探测。

Les Misérables Dialogue: A film-based network representation of social communication
悲惨世界对话：基于电影的社交沟通网络表征
In this work, we revisit the classic Les Misérables benchmark network from the perspective of social communication.
