[2610.13035]
GeoGR: Spatial-Semantic Token Compression and Group Routing for Efficient 3D VLMs
GeoGR：用于高效3D视觉语言模型的空间-语义标记压缩与分组路由
Multi-view representations enable pretrained image and video vision-language models to understand 3D scenes, but their long visual token sequences make inference costly. Redundancy arises both from repeated observations of the same scene regions across views and from processing all visual tokens at every LLM layer, regardless of their relevance to the current computation. We propose GeoGR, a two-stage framework that jointly compresses scene representations and visual computation. At the projector level, GeoSemZip consolidates cross-view observations into 3D voxel tokens and combines semantic saliency with spatial coverage to construct a compact scene representation. Unselected voxel features are aggregated into contextual anchors to retain scene information that would otherwise be discarded. At the LLM level, we identify an effective visual computation window and observe that query-conditioned token relevance remains locally stable but changes over longer layer intervals. Guided by these findings, GroupRoute combines late entry and early exit with recoverable group-wise skipping. Low-relevance tokens bypass complete LLM layers while retaining their hidden states and can rejoin the active group at subsequent anchor layers selected under a computation budget. For compression-aware post-training, we construct a compact dataset of 67K samples by retaining 30% of the original training pool. With this protocol, GeoGR retains 95.2% and 96.8% of the dense post-trained models' performance on LLaVA-OneVision-7B and Video-3D LLM-7B, respectively, while accelerating LLM prefill by $6.8\times$ and $6.1\times$. These results demonstrate the consistent effectiveness of GeoGR across 3D VLMs with and without explicit 3D spatial encoding, highlighting a favorable performance-efficiency trade-off. The code is available at this https URL .

Benchmarking Embedding Geometries and Clustering Algorithms for Unsupervised Patent Claim Classification
专利权利要求无监督分类中嵌入几何与聚类算法的基准测试
Prior-art retrieval systems are structurally organized around classification codes, making cross-code discovery difficult. This paper asks if an unsupervised representation trained on patent text can recover relevant connections that code-restricted search cannot, and if hyperbolic space helps. We trained a hyperbolic and a matched Euclidean Variational Autoencoder (VAE) on 45,000 US patents (96 runs, 6 seeds). The hyperbolic model retrieves genuinely cross-category prior art 52% more often than the Euclidean model (18.76% vs. 12.34% of top-10 results, 3,000 queries). This represents a practical gain for the motivating search problem. The hyperbolic model agrees less with official classification (NMI 0.2392 vs. 0.3206), which we explain geometrically: the two measures capture different goals, and only one aligns with prior-art search needs. In a study of 10 technology domains (50,000 more patents), we find that a domain's classification breadth--the number of distinct CPC subgroups spanned--predicts approach performance (Pearson r=-0.642, p=0.045, N=10), and that patents per subgroup is an even stronger predictor (r=0.715, p=0.020). This provides a checkable signal for practitioners to determine where the method is most valuable. We report all findings with statistical transparency and release a complete, 91-test automated implementation.

[2610.12644]
Cancerous Backdoor Attack: Gradual Activation via Invisible Tokens and A Stage Controller
癌变型后门攻击：通过不可见标记与阶段控制器实现渐进激活
Backdoor Attacks on Large Language Models (LLMs) typically follow a trigger-response pattern, where satisfying a trigger condition activates a single terminal behavior. However, this single-point backdoor activation does not explore attacks in which malicious behavior develops gradually through multiple intermediate stages before reaching the terminal activation stage. We present Cancerous Backdoor Attack, that gradually corrupts an LLM's reasoning over a sequence of interactions by leveraging invisible tokens and a stage controller. Specifically, invisible tokens provide hidden stages to the model without appearing in the user's visible request and the stage controller tracks the attack's progression during inference to realize gradual activation. We evaluate Cancerous Backdoor Attack across several reasoning datasets. The attack consistently produces gradual reasoning corruption across models and tasks. We compare Cancerous Backdoor Attack with prior backdoor attack baselines to demonstrate our proposed attack outperforms the baselines. Our results indicate that reasoning corruption can develop gradually before it becomes visible, making answer accuracy alone insufficient for detecting backdoor attacks.
中文翻译

[2610.12610]
Knowing Is Not Telling: Identity Ossification in LLM Representations of Trans-border Communities
知而不言：跨边境社区在LLM表征中的身份固化现象
Ethnic and cultural identities rarely align with political borders, a tension made sharper by migration and long-standing cross-border ties. Communities such as the Sami, the Rohingya, and the Kurds have long lived across more than one state. This tension is especially clear in mainland Southeast Asia, where many communities share history, language, and culture across state lines. We study nine such transborder ethnic communities along China's southwestern border with mainland Southeast Asia, looking at how large language models (LLMs) represent them. We define and study identity ossification: a pattern where LLMs turn a fluid, cross-border ethnic identity into a single, fixed nation-state identity. We introduce the Transborder Identity Ossification Diagnostic Framework (TIODF) that compares direct factual questions with open-ended narrative questions to test whether ossification comes from missing knowledge or from how models frame their answers. We test GPT-5.1 and DeepSeek-V3.2 in English and Chinese. Ossification varies substantially across models and query languages. It appears in only 10.1% of GPT-5.1's English answers, but in 62.6% of DeepSeek-V3.2's Chinese answers. Models often still tell a single-nation story even when they answer the factual question correctly. This decoupling suggests that ossification reflects narrative framing rather than simple knowledge absence, which matters for how we test cultural fairness in multilingual AI systems.
中文翻译
民族与文化身份很少与政治边界一致，这一张力因移民和长期跨境联系而更加突出。像萨米人、若开人和库尔德人这样的社区长期生活在多个国家之间。这种张力在东南亚大陆尤为明显，许多社区跨越国界共享历史、语言和文化。我们研究了中国西南部与东南亚大陆接壤的九个跨边境民族社区，考察大型语言模型（LLMs）如何表征这些群体。我们定义并研究了‘身份固化’现象：即LLMs将原本流动的跨边境民族身份简化为单一固定的国家身份。为此，我们提出了跨边境身份固化诊断框架（TIODF），通过对比直接事实性问题与开放式叙事问题，测试身份固化是源于知识缺失还是模型回答框架的问题。我们在GPT-5.1和DeepSeek-V3.2模型上分别用英语和中文进行了测试。结果显示，身份固化在不同模型和查询语言间差异显著：GPT-5.1的英文回答中仅出现10.1%，而DeepSeek-V3.2的中文回答中高达62.6%。即使模型在事实性问题上回答正确，仍常讲述单一国家的故事。这种脱节表明，身份固化反映的是叙述框架而非简单的知识缺失，这对多语言AI系统中的文化公平性评估具有重要意义。

[2610.12495]
Kairos: Toward a Byte-Level Foundation Model for Every Modality
Kairos：迈向适用于所有模态的字节级基础模型
Small language models trained on a single local machine leave little room for architectural trial and error. Kairos combines bidirectional gated-DeltaNet linear attention and sliding-window attention over shared projections (LiZAttention2), block-wise Attention-Residual aggregation, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) feed-forward layer and a multi-scale byte-level codec, trained with a uniform-noise denoising objective over one byte space shared by text, image, audio, video, lidar and control streams; this report asks whether the combination optimizes at all before asking whether it is useful. We give code-derived algorithms for each component, including a cross-session recurrent-state memory gate, then run convergence sanity checks: overfittinga single 440-byte sentence at 1--15M parameters, text only, with three seeds for codec and attention choices and single-run sweeps for seven further factors. The two attention variants tested at 3\,000 steps (LiZAttention2 and window attention alone) reach a minimum loss near $10^{-3}$. After 600 steps, the mean minimum loss ranges from 0.018 (patch codec) to 0.354 (depthwise convolutional codec) with an identical ranking on three seeds. An apparent advantage of shared over separate attention projections closes with more steps or more diverse data and reverses when the projections are frozen: it is a convergence-speed effect, not a capacity effect. These checks suggest that the implementation optimizes; they say nothing about generalization, multimodal training or comparison with existing models, which a planned pretraining run is meant to address. The Discussion sizes a 200M-total/25M-active target analytically; none of its throughput figures is measured. Code is available at this https URL . Python package at this https URL .
中文翻译
在单台本地机器上训练的小型语言模型留给架构试验的空间很小。Kairos结合了双向门控-DeltaNet线性注意力与共享投影上的滑动窗口注意力（LiZAttention2）、分块注意力-残差聚合、稀疏专家混合（MoE）前馈层以及多尺度字节级编码器，采用统一噪声去噪目标在文本、图像、音频、视频、激光雷达和控制流共用的一个字节空间上进行训练；本报告首先探讨这种组合是否真正优化，再讨论其是否有用。我们为每个组件提供了基于代码的算法，包括跨会话循环状态记忆门，随后进行了收敛性合理性检查：在1--15M参数范围内对单个440字节句子进行过拟合测试，仅使用文本数据，针对编码器和注意力选择设置三种随机种子，并对七个额外因素进行单次运行扫描。在3000步时测试的两种注意力变体（LiZAttention2和仅滑动窗口注意力）均达到接近$10^{-3}$的最小损失。经过600步后，平均最小损失范围从0.018（块编码器）到0.354（深度卷积编码器），三个种子下的排名一致。共享注意力投影相比独立投影的明显优势在更多步数或更多样化数据下消失，当投影被冻结时甚至逆转：这属于收敛速度效应，而非容量效应。这些检查表明实现过程确实具备优化能力；但它们未涉及泛化性能、多模态训练或与现有模型的比较，这些问题计划通过后续预训练运行来解决。讨论部分从理论上分析了一个总计2亿/活跃2500万的模型目标；但其中所有吞吐量数据均未实测。代码可在该https链接获取，Python包可在该https链接获取。
